namespace SLM

	[ComImport]
	[Guid("1460FDB6-752B-11d4-B067-0090271CF383")]
	[InterfaceType(ComInterfaceType.InterfaceIsIUnknown)]
	internal interface ISecCallback
	{
		void CheckoutFail([In] int cookie, [In][MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.BStr)] string errstring);

		void LicenseFail([In] int cookie, [In][MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.BStr)] string errstring);
	}
	[Guid("198817B0-D833-40aa-9CCF-174EF6C1957C")]
	[InterfaceType(ComInterfaceType.InterfaceIsIUnknown)]
	internal interface ISLMUtility
	{
		bool CheckLicense([In][MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.BStr)] string license, [In][MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.BStr)] string licversion);

		[return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.BStr)]
		string GetLicenseLocation([In] int cookie);
	}
	[Guid("1460FDB5-752B-11d4-B067-0090271CF383")]
	[InterfaceType(ComInterfaceType.InterfaceIsIUnknown)]
	internal interface _ISecServer
	{
		[return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.BStr)]
		string CheckoutLicense([In][MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.BStr)] string license, [In][MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.BStr)] string licversion, out int cookie, [In][MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Interface)] object lostLicenseCallback, [In] int importance);

		void CheckoutLicenseDeferred([In][MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.BStr)] string license, [In][MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.BStr)] string licversion, out int cookie, [In][MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Interface)] object lostLicenseCallback, [In] int importance);

		void ValidateLicense([In] int cookie, [In] int waitforit, out int valid);

		void ReleaseLicense([In] int cookie);

		[return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.BStr)]
		string InfoString([In] int cookie, out int infover);

		[return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.BStr)]
		string GetErrorString([In] int errcode);

		void StartAuth([In][MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.Interface)] object auth, out int authcookie);

		void StopAuth([In] int authcookie);

		void RegisterSoftLicense([In][MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.BStr)] string license, [In][MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.BStr)] string versionInfo, [In] int versionNumber, [In] int referencecookie, out int softCookie);

		void RevokeSoftLicense([In] int softCookie);
	}
	[ComImport]
	[Guid("1460FDB4-752B-11d4-B067-0090271CF383")]
	internal class _CSecServer
	{
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public enum LicenseStatus
	{
		NoLic,
		Pending,
		Ok
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public enum LicenseMode
	{
		Auto,
		Standard,
		Token,
		Unified
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public delegate void FailedLicenseCallBack(int cookie, string error);
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public delegate void FailedCheckoutCallBack(int cookie, string error);
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class CSecCallback : ISecCallback
	{
		private FailedLicenseCallBack failedLicense;

		private FailedCheckoutCallBack failedCheckout;

		public FailedLicenseCallBack FailedLicense
		{
			get
			{
				return failedLicense;
			}
			set
			{
				failedLicense = value;
			}
		}

		public FailedCheckoutCallBack FailedCheckout
		{
			get
			{
				return failedCheckout;
			}
			set
			{
				failedCheckout = value;
			}
		}

		public CSecCallback()
		{
			failedLicense = null;
			failedCheckout = null;
		}

		public CSecCallback(FailedLicenseCallBack _failedLicense, FailedCheckoutCallBack _failedCheckout)
		{
			failedLicense = _failedLicense;
			failedCheckout = _failedCheckout;
		}

		void ISecCallback.CheckoutFail(int cookie, string error)
		{
			if (failedCheckout != null)
			{
				failedCheckout(cookie, error);
			}
		}

		void ISecCallback.LicenseFail(int cookie, string error)
		{
			if (failedLicense != null)
			{
				failedLicense(cookie, error);
			}
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class CSecServer
	{
		private _CSecServer myServer;

		private CSecCallback myCallback;

		public bool Load()
		{
			bool result = true;
			if (myCallback == null)
			{
				myCallback = new CSecCallback();
			}
			if (myServer == null)
			{
				try
				{
					myServer = new _CSecServer();
					result = true;
				}
				catch
				{
					result = false;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public string CheckOut(string license, string version, out int cookie)
		{
			cookie = 0;
			string text = "";
			if (myServer != null)
			{
				try
				{
					return ((_ISecServer)myServer).CheckoutLicense(license, version, out cookie, null, 0);
				}
				catch
				{
					return "Error calling SLM COM Checkout function.";
				}
			}
			return "SLM COM object has not been loaded.";
		}

		public string CheckOutDeferred(string license, string version, out int cookie, FailedLicenseCallBack failedLicense, FailedCheckoutCallBack failedCheckout)
		{
			cookie = 0;
			string result = "";
			if (myServer != null)
			{
				try
				{
					_ISecServer obj = (_ISecServer)myServer;
					if (myCallback != null)
					{
						myCallback.FailedLicense = failedLicense;
						myCallback.FailedCheckout = failedCheckout;
					}
					obj.CheckoutLicenseDeferred(license, version, out cookie, myCallback, 0);
				}
				catch
				{
					result = "Error calling SLM COM CheckOutDeferred function.";
				}
			}
			else
			{
				result = "SLM COM object has not been loaded.";
			}
			return result;
		}

		public LicenseStatus ValidateLicense(int cookie)
		{
			LicenseStatus result = LicenseStatus.NoLic;
			if (myServer != null)
			{
				try
				{
					_ISecServer obj = (_ISecServer)myServer;
					int valid = 0;
					obj.ValidateLicense(cookie, 1, out valid);
					result = valid switch
					{
						1 => LicenseStatus.Pending, 
						2 => LicenseStatus.Ok, 
						_ => LicenseStatus.NoLic, 
					};
				}
				catch
				{
					result = LicenseStatus.NoLic;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void CheckIn(int cookie)
		{
			if (myServer != null)
			{
				try
				{
					((_ISecServer)myServer).ReleaseLicense(cookie);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
		}

		public bool CheckLicense(string license, string version)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (myServer != null)
			{
				try
				{
					return ((ISLMUtility)myServer).CheckLicense(license, version);
				}
				catch
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public string GetLicenseLocation(int cookie)
		{
			string text = "";
			if (myServer != null)
			{
				try
				{
					return ((ISLMUtility)myServer).GetLicenseLocation(cookie);
				}
				catch
				{
					return "Error calling SLM COM GetLicenseLocation function.";
				}
			}
			return "SLM COM object has not been loaded.";
		}

		public string GetVendorInfo(int cookie, out int version)
		{
			version = 0;
			string text = "";
			if (myServer != null)
			{
				try
				{
					return ((_ISecServer)myServer).InfoString(cookie, out version);
				}
				catch
				{
					return "Error calling SLM COM GetVendorInfo function.";
				}
			}
			return "SLM COM object has not been loaded.";
		}

		public string GenerateErrorMessageFromLastError(string error)
		{
			string text = "";
			if (error.Length > 0)
			{
				string[] array = error.Split('\r');
				foreach (string text2 in array)
				{
					if (!(text2 != ""))
					{
						continue;
					}
					int num = text2.IndexOf(" ");
					if (num == -1)
					{
						continue;
					}
					string text3 = text2.Substring(0, num);
					while (text3[0] == '\n')
					{
						text3 = text3.Substring(1, text3.Length - 1);
					}
					string s = text2.Substring(num + 1, text2.Length - num - 1);
					int num2 = text3.IndexOf(":");
					if (num2 != -1)
					{
						text += "Configuration Flag: \"";
						string text4 = text3.Substring(num2 + 1, text3.Length - num2 - 1);
						text += text4;
						text += "\"\n";
						continue;
					}
					text += "Server: \"";
					text += text3;
					int num3 = 0;
					num3 = int.Parse(s);
					if (num3 != 0)
					{
						text += "\" returned error: ";
						string lastError = GetLastError(num3);
						if (lastError != "")
						{
							text += "\"";
							text += lastError;
							text += "\"";
						}
						else
						{
							string text5 = $"0x{num3} {num3}";
							text += text5;
						}
						text += "\n";
					}
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		public string GetLastError(int errcode)
		{
			if (myServer != null)
			{
				try
				{
					return ((_ISecServer)myServer).GetErrorString(errcode);
				}
				catch
				{
					return "Error calling SLM COM GetLastError function.";
				}
			}
			return "SLM COM object has not been loaded.";
		}

		public bool RegisterSoftLicense(string license, string version, int versionNumber, int parentCookie, out int cookie)
		{
			cookie = 0;
			bool result = false;
			if (myServer != null)
			{
				try
				{
					((_ISecServer)myServer).RegisterSoftLicense(license, version, versionNumber, parentCookie, out cookie);
					if (cookie != 0)
					{
						result = true;
					}
				}
				catch
				{
					result = false;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				result = false;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void RevokeSoftLicense(int cookie)
		{
			if (myServer != null)
			{
				try
				{
					((_ISecServer)myServer).RevokeSoftLicense(cookie);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
		}

		public bool StopAuth(int cookie)
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (myServer != null)
			{
				try
				{
					((_ISecServer)myServer).StopAuth(cookie);
					result = true;
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
			else
			{
				result = false;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void BatchCheckIn(int[] cookies)
		{
			if (myServer == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				_ISecServer iSecServer = (_ISecServer)myServer;
				for (int i = 0; i < cookies.Length; i++)
				{
					iSecServer.ReleaseLicense(cookies[i]);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		private bool SetJob(int number)
		{
			return false;
		}

		private bool SetLocation(int location)
		{
			return false;
		}

		private bool SetDepartment(int department)
		{
			return false;
		}

		private string Commute(string server, string[] licenses, int days, LicenseMode mode)
		{
			return "";
		}

		private string UnCommute(string server, string[] licenses, int days, LicenseMode mode)
		{
			return "";
		}

		private string[] GetBroadcastServerList()
		{
			return null;
		}

		private bool GetConfiguredServerList(out string[] forceservers, out string[] servers)
		{
			forceservers = null;
			servers = null;
			return false;
		}

		private string[] GetServerLicenseList(string server)
		{
			return null;
		}
	}

		[DllImport("admdll.dll")]
		private static extern void ADM_DLL_BUILD([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] ref int build);

		[DllImport("admdll.dll")]
		[return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)]
		private static extern int ADM_READ_TABFILE([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPStr)] StringBuilder fileName, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int stringLength);

		[DllImport("admdll.dll")]
		[return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)]
		private static extern int ADM_STRFUN([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int admPointer, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPStr)] StringBuilder description, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int descriptionLength, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int offsetPointer, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPStr)] StringBuilder String, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int stringLength);

		[DllImport("admdll.dll")]
		[return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)]
		private static extern int ADM_INTFUN([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int admPointer, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPStr)] StringBuilder description, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int descriptionLength, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int offsetPointer, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] ref int admInt);

		[DllImport("admdll.dll")]
		[return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)]
		private static extern int ADM_RELFUN([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int admPointer, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPStr)] StringBuilder description, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int descriptionLength, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int offsetPointer, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.R8)] ref double admReal);

		[DllImport("admdll.dll")]
		[return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)]
		private static extern int ADM_DPFUN([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int admPointer, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPStr)] StringBuilder description, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int descriptionLength, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int offsetPointer, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] ref int admDP);

		public int GetStringValue(Dsc valuePointer, string description, int offsetPointer, ref string value)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(description.ToString());
			StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder("                                                                                                    ");
			int result = ADM_STRFUN((int)valuePointer, stringBuilder, stringBuilder.Length, offsetPointer, stringBuilder2, stringBuilder2.Length);
			value = stringBuilder2.ToString().Trim();
			return result;
		}

		public int GetIntegerValue(Dsc valuePointer, string description, int offsetPointer, ref int value)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(description.ToString());
			return ADM_INTFUN((int)valuePointer, stringBuilder, stringBuilder.Length, offsetPointer, ref value);
		}

		public int GetDoubleValue(Dsc valuePointer, string description, int offsetPointer, ref double theValue)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(description.ToString());
			return ADM_RELFUN((int)valuePointer, stringBuilder, stringBuilder.Length, offsetPointer, ref theValue);
		}

		public int GetValuePointer(Dsc admPointer, string description, int offsetPointer, ref int value)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(description.ToString());
			return ADM_DPFUN((int)admPointer, stringBuilder, stringBuilder.Length, offsetPointer, ref value);
		}

		public bool Initialise(string message)
		{
			int build = 0;
			int num = 33;
			ADM_DLL_BUILD(ref build);
			if (build != num)
			{
				message = "Different version of admdll.dll installed - build found = ADM" + build + ", build expected = ADM" + num;
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public int ReadTabFile(string fName)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(fName.ToString());
			return ADM_READ_TABFILE(stringBuilder, stringBuilder.Length);
		}

}
namespace AspenTech.Hydraulics.MathLibrary
{
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public delegate double UnaryFunction(double x);
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public enum BracketExpandType
	{
		DecreaseMinimum,
		IncreaseMaximum,
		ChangeBoth
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class SecantRootFinder
	{
		private double mAccuracy;

		private int maxIterations;

		private double mExpansionFactor;

		public double Accuracy
		{
			get
			{
				return mAccuracy;
			}
			set
			{
				mAccuracy = value;
			}
		}

		public int MaximumIterations
		{
			get
			{
				return maxIterations;
			}
			set
			{
				maxIterations = value;
			}
		}

		public double ExpansionFactor
		{
			get
			{
				return mExpansionFactor;
			}
			set
			{
				mExpansionFactor = value;
			}
		}

		public SecantRootFinder()
		{
			mAccuracy = 0.0001;
			maxIterations = 100;
			mExpansionFactor = 0.1;
		}

		public double FindRoot(double xmin, double xmax, UnaryFunction func)
		{
			double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			double num = func(xmin);
			if (num == 0.0)
			{
				return xmin;
			}
			double num2 = func(xmax);
			if (num2 == 0.0)
			{
				return xmax;
			}
			if (num * num2 < 0.0)
			{
				result = FindRoot(xmin, xmax, num, num2, func);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public double FindRoot(double xmin, double xmax, UnaryFunction func, BracketExpandType expType)
		{
			double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			double num = func(xmin);
			if (num == 0.0)
			{
				return xmin;
			}
			double num2 = func(xmax);
			if (num2 == 0.0)
			{
				return xmax;
			}
			double num3 = xmin;
			double num4 = xmax;
			double num5 = num;
			double num6 = num2;
			bool flag = num * num2 < 0.0;
			if (!flag && (expType == BracketExpandType.IncreaseMaximum || expType == BracketExpandType.ChangeBoth))
			{
				for (int i = 1; i < 25; i++)
				{
					num4 = xmax * Math.Pow(1.0 + mExpansionFactor, i);
					num6 = func(num4);
					if (num * num6 <= 0.0)
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (!flag && (expType == BracketExpandType.DecreaseMinimum || expType == BracketExpandType.ChangeBoth))
			{
				num4 = xmax;
				num6 = num2;
				for (int j = 1; j < 25; j++)
				{
					num3 = xmin * Math.Pow(1.0 - mExpansionFactor, j);
					num5 = func(num3);
					if (num5 * num2 <= 0.0)
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				result = FindRoot(num3, num4, num5, num6, func);
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal double FindRoot(double xmin, double xmax, double ymin, double ymax, UnaryFunction func)
		{
			double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			if (ymin * ymax <= 0.0)
			{
				double num = ymin;
				double num2 = ymax;
				double num3 = 0.0;
				double num4 = 0.0;
				if (Math.Abs(num) < Math.Abs(num2))
				{
					num3 = xmin;
					num4 = xmax;
					double num5 = num;
					num = num2;
					num2 = num5;
				}
				else
				{
					num4 = xmin;
					num3 = xmax;
				}
				double num6 = 0.0;
				for (int i = 1; i < maxIterations; i++)
				{
					num6 = (num4 - num3) * num2 / (num2 - num);
					if (num3 + num6 <= xmin)
					{
						num6 = xmin - num3;
					}
					num4 = num3;
					num = num2;
					num3 += num6;
					num2 = func(num3);
					if (Math.Abs(num6 / num3) < mAccuracy)
					{
						result = num3;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class GoldenMinimumFinder
	{
		private double mAccuracy;

		private int maxIterations;

		public double Accuracy
		{
			get
			{
				return mAccuracy;
			}
			set
			{
				mAccuracy = value;
			}
		}

		public int MaximumIterations
		{
			get
			{
				return maxIterations;
			}
			set
			{
				maxIterations = value;
			}
		}

		public GoldenMinimumFinder()
		{
			mAccuracy = 1E-05;
			maxIterations = 100;
		}

		public double FindMinimum(double xmin, double xmax, UnaryFunction func)
		{
			double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			double num = xmax;
			double num2 = func(xmin);
			double num3 = func(num);
			double num4 = 1.01 * xmin;
			double num5 = func(num4);
			if (num5 > num2)
			{
				return xmin;
			}
			bool flag = num3 > num5;
			if (!flag)
			{
				for (int i = 1; i < 25; i++)
				{
					num = xmax * Math.Pow(1.1, i);
					num3 = func(num);
					if (num3 > num5)
					{
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				result = FindMinimum(xmin, num4, num, num2, num5, num3, func);
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal double FindMinimum(double xmin, double xmid, double xmax, double ymin, double ymid, double ymax, UnaryFunction func)
		{
			double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			if (ymid < ymin && ymax > ymid)
			{
				double num = 0.61803399;
				double num2 = 1.0 - num;
				double num3 = xmin;
				double num4 = 0.0;
				double num5 = 0.0;
				double num6 = xmax;
				if (Math.Abs(xmax - xmid) > Math.Abs(xmid - xmin))
				{
					num4 = xmid;
					num5 = xmid + num2 * (xmid - xmin);
				}
				else
				{
					num5 = xmid;
					num4 = xmid - num2 * (xmid - xmin);
				}
				double num7 = func(num4);
				double num8 = func(num5);
				int num9 = 0;
				while (Math.Abs(num6 - num3) > mAccuracy * (Math.Abs(num4) + Math.Abs(num5)) && num9 < maxIterations)
				{
					if (num8 < num7)
					{
						num3 = num4;
						num4 = num5;
						num5 = num * num4 + num2 * num6;
						num7 = num8;
						num8 = func(num5);
					}
					else
					{
						num6 = num5;
						num5 = num4;
						num4 = num * num5 + num2 * num3;
						num8 = num7;
						num7 = func(num4);
					}
					num9++;
				}
				result = ((!(num7 < num8)) ? num5 : num4);
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
}
namespace AspenTech.Hydraulics.PropertyUtilities

	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class FlashFailedException : Exception
	{
		public FlashType FlashType { get; } = (FlashType)(-1);

		public double Data1 { get; } = double.NaN;

		public double Data2 { get; } = double.NaN;

		public FlashFailedException(FlashType flashType, double data1, double data2, string message = null)
			: base(message ?? "Flash at specified conditions failed.")
		{
			FlashType = flashType;
			Data1 = data1;
			Data2 = data2;
		}
	}

		public double CalculateCv(IFlowStream Stream, PhaseType PT)
		{
			IPhase phaseByType = Stream.GetPhaseByType(PT);
			double value = phaseByType.Pressure.Value;
			double value2 = phaseByType.Density.Value;
			double num = value2;
			double num2 = value2;
			double value3 = phaseByType.Temperature.Value;
			double num3 = -100.0;
			IFlowStream flowStream = (IFlowStream)Stream.Clone();
			double num4 = 0.0;
			bool flag = false;
			double num5 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			double num6 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			bool flag2 = false;
			int num7 = -32767;
			bool flag3 = false;
			double fEMPTY = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			fEMPTY = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PT).MolarVapourFraction.Value;
			if (fEMPTY < 0.0001)
			{
				num6 = -0.1;
			}
			else if (fEMPTY > 0.9999)
			{
				num6 = 0.1;
			}
			flag3 = false;
			flag = true;
			flag2 = false;
			num7 = 0;
			while (!flag2)
			{
				if (!flag3)
				{
					num7++;
					num5 = num6 * (double)num7;
				}
				else
				{
					num6 *= -1.0;
					flag3 = false;
				}
				if (Math.Abs(num5) > 2.0)
				{
					break;
				}
				FlashStream(flowStream, FlashType.PTFlash, value, value3 + num5);
				num = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PT).Density.Value;
				if (flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PT).MolarVapourFraction.Value > 0.0 && flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PT).MolarVapourFraction.Value < 1.0 && !flag3 && flag)
				{
					flag3 = true;
					flag = false;
				}
				else if (Math.Abs(num - value2) > 1E-06)
				{
					flag2 = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				double num8 = Math.Pow((1.0 / num - 1.0 / value2) / num5, 2.0);
				if (PT == PhaseType.Liquid)
				{
					num3 = 1000.0;
				}
				ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(flowStream, FlashType.PTFlash, value + num3, value3);
				num2 = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(PT).Density.Value;
				if (Math.Abs(num2 - value2) < 1E-06)
				{
					num4 = phaseByType.Cp.Value;
				}
				else
				{
					double num9 = (1.0 / num2 - 1.0 / value2) / num3;
					num4 = phaseByType.Cp.Value + value3 * num8 / num9;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				num4 = Stream.GetPhaseByType(PT).Cv.Value;
			}
			if (num4 < 0.0)
			{
				num4 = phaseByType.Cp.Value;
			}
			return num4;
		}

		public double CalculateDRhoDP(IFlowStream Stream, PhaseType PT)
		{
			double num = -100.0;
			double value = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
			double value2 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value;
			FlashStream(Stream, FlashType.PTFlash, value, value2);
			double value3 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarVapourFraction.Value;
			if (value3 < 0.5)
			{
				num = 0.0 - num;
			}
			if (value3 > 0.99999)
			{
				num = (0.0 - value) / 1000.0;
				if (num > -100.0)
				{
					num = -100.0;
				}
			}
			if (value3 < 1E-05)
			{
				num = 1000.0;
			}
			double num2 = Stream.GetPhaseByType(PT).Density.Value / Stream.GetPhaseByType(PT).MolecularWeight.Value;
			ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(Stream, FlashType.PTFlash, value + num, value2);
			double result = (Stream.GetPhaseByType(PT).Density.Value / Stream.GetPhaseByType(PT).MolecularWeight.Value - num2) / num;
			Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value = value;
			Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value = value2;
			return result;
		}

		public double CalculateDRhoDT(IFlowStream Stream, PhaseType PT)
		{
			double num = 0.1;
			double value = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
			double value2 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value;
			FlashStream(Stream, FlashType.PTFlash, value, value2);
			double value3 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarVapourFraction.Value;
			if (value3 < 0.5)
			{
				num = 0.0 - num;
			}
			if (value3 < 1E-05)
			{
				num = -1.0;
			}
			double num2 = Stream.GetPhaseByType(PT).Density.Value / Stream.GetPhaseByType(PT).MolecularWeight.Value;
			ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(Stream, FlashType.PTFlash, value, value2 + num);
			double result = (Stream.GetPhaseByType(PT).Density.Value / Stream.GetPhaseByType(PT).MolecularWeight.Value - num2) / num;
			Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value = value;
			Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value = value2;
			return result;
		}

		public void ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(IFlowStream Stream, FlashType FT)

			double data2 = -32767.0;
			try

				GetData(Stream, FT, out data, out data2);
				ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(Stream, FT, data, data2);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
