			}
		}

		public virtual void ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(IFlowStream Stream, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			try
			{
				mDoFlash = true;
				if (Stream.StreamID != mLastStreamID)
				{
					mReady = false;
				}
				if (mChangedComps == ChangedComponentsActionEnum.ReInitComThermo || !mReady)
				{
					Check(Stream);
				}
				if (mZone > 0.0 && mReady)
				{
					IPhase phaseByID = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0);
					double value;
					double value2;
					switch (FT)
					{
					case FlashType.PTFlash:
						value = phaseByID.Pressure.Value;
						value2 = phaseByID.Temperature.Value;
						break;
					case FlashType.PHFlash:
						value = phaseByID.Pressure.Value;
						value2 = phaseByID.Enthalpy.Value;
						break;
					case FlashType.PSFlash:
						value = phaseByID.Pressure.Value;
						value2 = phaseByID.Entropy.Value;
						break;
					case FlashType.PVFlash:
						value = phaseByID.Pressure.Value;
						value2 = phaseByID.MolarVapourFraction.Value;
						break;
					case FlashType.THFlash:
						value = phaseByID.Temperature.Value;
						value2 = phaseByID.Enthalpy.Value;
						break;
					case FlashType.TSFlash:
						value = phaseByID.Temperature.Value;
						value2 = phaseByID.Entropy.Value;
						break;
					case FlashType.TVFlash:
						value = phaseByID.Temperature.Value;
						value2 = phaseByID.MolarVapourFraction.Value;
						break;
					default:
						throw new Exception("Unsupport flash type detected. ");
					}
					if (value != RealValue.FEMPTY && value != 0.0 && value2 != RealValue.FEMPTY && value2 != 0.0 && Math.Abs((value - data1) / value) <= mZone && Math.Abs((value2 - data2) / value2) <= mZone && Stream.NumberOfPhases > 0)
					{
						mDoFlash = false;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("ReflashStream failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected abstract void Check(IFlowStream Stream);

		protected abstract void Check(IFlowStream Stream, bool Disable);

		public virtual void CombinePhases(IPhase[] Phases, IPhase NewPhase)
		{
			double[] array = null;
			int num = -32767;
			int num2 = -32767;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			try
			{
				GetOverallStateParameters(Phases, NewPhase);
				double num4 = 0.0;
				double num5 = 0.0;
				for (num = 0; num < Phases.GetLength(0); num++)
				{
					num4 += Phases[num].MassFraction.Value * Phases[num].Cp.Value;
					num5 += Phases[num].MassFraction.Value * Phases[num].Cv.Value;
				}
				NewPhase.Cp.Value = num4 / NewPhase.MassFraction.Value;
				NewPhase.Cv.Value = num5 / NewPhase.MassFraction.Value;
				NewPhase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
				CombineTransportProperty(Phases, NewPhase);
				num2 = NewPhase.NumberOfCompounds;
				array = new double[num2];
				num3 = 0.0;
				for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
				{
					array[num] = 0.0;
					for (int i = 0; i < Phases.GetLength(0); i++)
					{
						array[num] += Phases[i].ComponentMoleFractions[num].Value * Phases[i].MolarFraction.Value;
					}
					num3 += array[num];
				}
				for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
				{
					NewPhase.ComponentMoleFractions[num].Value = array[num] / num3;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("CombinePhases Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		public virtual void CombineTransportProperty(IPhase[] Phases, IPhase NewPhase)
		{
			IPhase phase = null;
			IPhase phase2 = null;
			IPhase phase3 = null;
			double num = 0.018016;
			try
			{
				if (Phases.GetLength(0) > 2)
				{
					phase = new Phase(Phases[0].NumberOfCompounds);
					IPhase[] array = new Phase[2];
					IPhase[] array2 = array;
					array = new Phase[2];
					IPhase[] array3 = array;
					if (Math.Abs(Phases[0].MolecularWeight.Value - 18.016) < num || Phases[0].PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
					{
						phase2 = Phases[0];
						array2[0] = Phases[1];
						array2[1] = Phases[2];
						CombinePhases(array2, phase);
						array3[0] = phase2;
					}
					else if (Math.Abs(Phases[1].MolecularWeight.Value - 18.016) < num || Phases[1].PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
					{
						phase2 = Phases[1];
						array2[0] = Phases[0];
						array2[1] = Phases[2];
						CombinePhases(array2, phase);
						array3[0] = phase2;
					}
					else if (Math.Abs(Phases[2].MolecularWeight.Value - 18.016) < num || Phases[2].PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
					{
						phase2 = Phases[2];
						array2[0] = Phases[0];
						array2[1] = Phases[1];
						CombinePhases(array2, phase);
						array3[0] = phase2;
					}
					else
					{
						array2[0] = Phases[0];
						array2[1] = Phases[1];
						CombinePhases(array2, phase);
						array3[0] = Phases[2];
					}
					array3[1] = phase;
					CombinePhases(array3, NewPhase);
				}
				else
				{
					if (Phases[0].Viscosity.Value == RealValue.FEMPTY || Phases[1].Viscosity.Value == RealValue.FEMPTY)
					{
						return;
					}
					switch (mCM)
					{
					case CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine:
					case CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.HysysMethodForWater_Oil:
						if (Math.Abs(Phases[0].MolecularWeight.Value - 18.016) < num || Phases[0].PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
						{
							phase2 = Phases[0];
							phase3 = Phases[1];
						}
						else if (Math.Abs(Phases[1].MolecularWeight.Value - 18.016) < num || Phases[1].PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
						{
							phase2 = Phases[1];
							phase3 = Phases[0];
						}
						if (phase2 != null)
						{
							IEmulsionViscosityCorrelation emulsionViscosityCorrelation = mEmulsionViscosityCorrelation;
							if (emulsionViscosityCorrelation == null)
							{
								emulsionViscosityCorrelation = new HYSYSEmulsionViscosityCorrelation();
							}
							NewPhase.Viscosity.Value = emulsionViscosityCorrelation.EmulsionViscosity(phase3, phase2);
							double num3 = phase2.Viscosity.Value / phase3.Viscosity.Value * Math.Sqrt(phase3.Density.Value / phase2.Density.Value);
							NewPhase.Conductivity.Value = phase3.Conductivity.Value * phase2.Conductivity.Value * ((num3 + 1.0) / (phase3.Conductivity.Value * num3 + phase2.Conductivity.Value));
							if (phase2.MassFraction.Value / phase2.Density.Value / (phase2.MassFraction.Value / phase2.Density.Value + phase3.MassFraction.Value / phase3.Density.Value) > 0.5)
							{
								NewPhase.SurfaceTension.Value = phase2.SurfaceTension.Value;
							}
							else
							{
								NewPhase.SurfaceTension.Value = phase3.SurfaceTension.Value;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							NewPhase.Viscosity.Value = ViscosityCalculator.NonPolarMixingViscosity(Phases[0], Phases[1]);
							double num4 = Phases[0].MassFraction.Value / Phases[0].Density.Value;
							num4 /= Phases[0].MassFraction.Value / Phases[0].Density.Value + Phases[1].MassFraction.Value / Phases[1].Density.Value;
							NewPhase.Conductivity.Value = Phases[0].Conductivity.Value * num4 + Phases[1].Conductivity.Value * (1.0 - num4);
							NewPhase.SurfaceTension.Value = Phases[0].SurfaceTension.Value * num4 + Phases[1].SurfaceTension.Value * (1.0 - num4);
						}
						break;
					default:
					{
						double num2 = Phases[0].MassFraction.Value / Phases[0].Density.Value;
						num2 /= Phases[0].MassFraction.Value / Phases[0].Density.Value + Phases[1].MassFraction.Value / Phases[1].Density.Value;
						NewPhase.Viscosity.Value = ViscosityCalculator.VolumeWeightedViscosity(Phases[0], Phases[1]);
						NewPhase.Conductivity.Value = Phases[0].Conductivity.Value * num2 + Phases[1].Conductivity.Value * (1.0 - num2);
						NewPhase.SurfaceTension.Value = Phases[0].SurfaceTension.Value * num2 + Phases[1].SurfaceTension.Value * (1.0 - num2);
						break;
					}
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("CombineTransportProperty Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected void GetData(IFlowStream Stream, FlashType FT, out double data1, out double data2)
		{
			switch (FT)
			{
			case FlashType.PHFlash:
				data1 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
				data2 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
				break;
			case FlashType.PTFlash:
				data1 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
				data2 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value;
				break;
			case FlashType.HVFlash:
				data1 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
				data2 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarVapourFraction.Value;
				break;
			case FlashType.PSFlash:
				data1 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
				data2 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Entropy.Value;
				break;
			case FlashType.PVFlash:
				data1 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Pressure.Value;
				data2 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarVapourFraction.Value;
				break;
			case FlashType.THFlash:
				data1 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value;
				data2 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
				break;
			case FlashType.TSFlash:
				data1 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value;
				data2 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Entropy.Value;
				break;
			case FlashType.TVFlash:
				data1 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Temperature.Value;
				data2 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarVapourFraction.Value;
				break;
			default:
				data1 = 0.0;
				data2 = 0.0;
				break;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void GetOverallStateParameters(IPhase[] Phases, IPhase OverallPhase)
		{
			double num = -32767.0;
			double num2 = -32767.0;
			double num3 = -32767.0;
			int num4 = -32767;
			double num5 = -32767.0;
			int num6 = -32767;
			double num7 = -32767.0;
			double num8 = 0.0;
			double num9 = 0.0;
			num6 = Phases.GetLength(0);
			num2 = 0.0;
			num3 = 0.0;
			num5 = 0.0;
			num7 = 0.0;
			num = 0.0;
			num8 = 0.0;
			num9 = 0.0;
			for (num4 = 0; num4 < num6; num4++)
			{
				num8 += Phases[num4].MolecularWeight.Value * Phases[num4].MolarFraction.Value;
				num9 += Phases[num4].MolarFraction.Value;
				num2 += Phases[num4].Enthalpy.Value * Phases[num4].MassFraction.Value;
				num3 += Phases[num4].Entropy.Value * Phases[num4].MassFraction.Value;
				num5 += Phases[num4].InternalEnergy.Value * Phases[num4].MassFraction.Value;
				num += Phases[num4].MassFraction.Value / Phases[num4].Density.Value;
				num7 += Phases[num4].MassFraction.Value;
			}
			OverallPhase.MolarFraction.Value = num9;
			OverallPhase.MolecularWeight.Value = num8 / num9;
			num2 /= num7;
			num3 /= num7;
			num5 /= num7;
			OverallPhase.Density.Value = num7 / num;
			OverallPhase.Enthalpy.Value = num2;
			OverallPhase.Entropy.Value = num3;
			OverallPhase.InternalEnergy.Value = num5;
			OverallPhase.Temperature.Value = Phases[0].Temperature.Value;
			OverallPhase.Pressure.Value = Phases[0].Pressure.Value;
			OverallPhase.MassFraction.Value = num7;
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateOverallPhase(IFlowStream Stream, int ActiveCount)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			int num = -32767;
			int num2 = -32767;
			int num3 = -32767;
			int num4 = -32767;
			int num5 = -32767;
			IPhase[] array = null;
			IPhase phase = null;
			IPhase phase2 = null;
			IPhase[] array2 = null;
			IPhase[] array3 = null;
			IPhase[] array4 = null;
			IPhase[] array5 = null;
			bool[] array6 = new bool[ActiveCount];
			int num6 = -1;
			try
			{
				num5 = Stream.NumberOfPhases;
				for (num = 0; num < num5; num++)
				{
					phase2 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(num + 1);
					if (((Phase)phase2).IsTrueAqueous())
					{
						phase2.PhaseType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
					}
				}
				num6 = 0;
				num5 = Stream.NumberOfPhases;
				double num7 = 0.0;
				for (num = 0; num < num5; num++)
				{
					phase2 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(num + 1);
					num7 += phase2.MolecularWeight.Value * phase2.MolarFraction.Value;
					if (phase2.PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid || phase2.PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
					{
						array6[num] = true;
						num6++;
					}
					else
					{
						array6[num] = false;
					}
				}
				for (num = 0; num < num5; num++)
				{
					phase2 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(num + 1);
					phase2.MassFraction.Value = phase2.MolecularWeight.Value * phase2.MolarFraction.Value / num7;
				}
				phase = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0);
				if (ActiveCount < 2)
				{
					IPhase[] array7 = new Phase[1];
					array2 = array7;
					array2[0] = Stream.GetPhaseByID(1);
					array2[0].MolarFraction.Value = 1.0;
					array2[0].MassFraction.Value = 1.0;
					array2[0].CopyStateParameters(phase);
					if (array2[0].PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour)
					{
						phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
					}
					else
					{
						phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
					}
					flag = true;
					Stream.CombinedLiquidPhase = null;
					if (IsFixedPhaseSet)
					{
						AdjustEquilibriumPhases1(Stream);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					switch (ActiveCount)
					{
					case 2:
					{
						IPhase[] array7 = new Phase[2];
						array2 = array7;
						array2[0] = (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(1).Clone();
						array2[1] = (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(2).Clone();
						if (array2[0].PhaseType == array2[1].PhaseType || num6 > 1)
						{
							if (array2[0].PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour)
							{
								throw new Exception("Prediction Of Multiple Vapour Phases");
							}
							array5 = new IPhase[1] { (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(1).Clone() };
							CombinePhases(array2, array5[0]);
							Stream.CombinedLiquidPhase = array5[0];
							if (mCM != CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine || (array2[0].PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid && array2[1].PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid))
							{
								Stream.NumberOfPhases = 1;
								Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, array5[0]);
							}
						}
						else
						{
							Stream.CombinedLiquidPhase = null;
						}
						if (IsFixedPhaseSet)
						{
							if (Stream.NumberOfPhases == 1)
							{
								AdjustEquilibriumPhases1(Stream);
							}
							else
							{
								AdjustEquilibriumPhases2(Stream);
							}
						}
						flag = false;
						break;
					}
					case 3:
					{
						IPhase[] array7 = new Phase[3];
						array2 = array7;
						array2[0] = (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(1).Clone();
						array2[1] = (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(2).Clone();
						array2[2] = (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(3).Clone();
						if (array2[0].PhaseType == array2[1].PhaseType && array2[1].PhaseType == array2[2].PhaseType)
						{
							if (array2[0].PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour)
							{
								throw new Exception("Prediction Of Multiple Vapour Phases");
							}
							array7 = new Phase[1];
							array = array7;
							array[0] = (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(1).Clone();
							CombinePhases(array2, array[0]);
							Stream.CombinedLiquidPhase = array[0];
							if (mCM != CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine)
							{
								Stream.NumberOfPhases = 1;
								Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, array[0]);
							}
						}
						else
						{
							switch (num6)
							{
							case 2:
								num2 = -32767;
								num3 = -32767;
								if (array6[0] && array6[1])
								{
									num2 = 0;
									num3 = 1;
									num4 = 2;
								}
								if (array6[0] && array6[2])
								{
									num2 = 0;
									num3 = 2;
									num4 = 1;
								}
								if (array6[1] && array6[2])
								{
									num2 = 1;
									num3 = 2;
									num4 = 0;
								}
								if (num2 != -32767)
								{
									if (array2[num2].PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour)
									{
										throw new Exception("Prediction Of Multiple Vapour Phases");
									}
									array7 = new Phase[2];
									array = array7;
									array7 = new Phase[2];
									array3 = array7;
									array[0] = (IPhase)array2[num4].Clone();
									array3[0] = array2[num2];
									array3[1] = array2[num3];
									array[1] = (IPhase)array2[num2].Clone();
									CombinePhases(array3, array[1]);
									Stream.CombinedLiquidPhase = array[1];
									if (mCM != CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine || (array2[num2].PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid && array2[num3].PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid))
									{
										Stream.NumberOfPhases = 2;
										Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, array[0]);
										Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, array[1]);
									}
								}
								break;
							case 3:
								num2 = -32767;
								num3 = -32767;
								num4 = -32767;
								if (array2[0].PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
								{
									num2 = 1;
									num3 = 2;
									num4 = 0;
								}
								else if (array2[1].PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
								{
									num2 = 0;
									num3 = 2;
									num4 = 1;
								}
								else if (array2[2].PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
								{
									num2 = 0;
									num3 = 1;
									num4 = 2;
								}
								if (num4 != -32767)
								{
									array7 = new Phase[3];
									array = array7;
									array7 = new Phase[2];
									array3 = array7;
									array[0] = (IPhase)array2[num4].Clone();
									array3[0] = array2[num2];
									array3[1] = array2[num3];
									array[1] = (IPhase)array2[num2].Clone();
									CombinePhases(array3, array[1]);
									array[2] = (IPhase)array2[num4].Clone();
									array3[0] = array[1];
									array3[1] = array2[num4];
									CombinePhases(array3, array[2]);
									Stream.CombinedLiquidPhase = array[2];
									if (mCM != CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine || (array2[num2].PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid && array2[num3].PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid))
									{
										Stream.NumberOfPhases = 2;
										Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, array[1]);
										Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, array[0]);
									}
								}
								break;
							}
						}
						if (IsFixedPhaseSet)
						{
							if (Stream.NumberOfPhases == 1)
							{
								AdjustEquilibriumPhases1(Stream);
							}
							else if (Stream.NumberOfPhases == 2)
							{
								AdjustEquilibriumPhases2(Stream);
							}
							else
							{
								AdjustEquilibriumPhases3(Stream);
							}
						}
						flag = false;
						break;
					}
					default:
						throw new Exception("Prediction Of More Than 3 Phases");
					}
				}
				if (!flag)
				{
					num5 = Stream.NumberOfPhases;
					array4 = new IPhase[num5];
					for (num = 0; num < Stream.NumberOfPhases; num++)
					{
						array4[num] = Stream.GetPhaseByID(num + 1);
					}
					GetOverallStateParameters(array4, phase);
					phase2 = Stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
					if (phase2 == null)
					{
						phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
					}
					else
					{
						phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = phase2.MolarFraction.Value;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("UpdateOverallPhase Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void AdjustEquilibriumPhases1(IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			int num = -32767;
			int num2 = -32767;
			IPhase phase = null;
			IPhase[] array = null;
			PhaseType phaseType = PhaseType.UnknownPhase;
			try
			{
				IPhase[] array2 = new Phase[1];
				array = array2;
				array[0] = (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(1).Clone();
				phaseType = array[0].PhaseType;
				if (mCalculatedPhases == CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquid)
				{
					Stream.NumberOfPhases = 2;
				}
				else
				{
					Stream.NumberOfPhases = 3;
				}
				num2 = Stream.NumberOfPhases;
				for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
				{
					phase = Stream.GetPhaseByID(num + 1);
					phase.UpdatePhaseByValue(array[0]);
					switch (num)
					{
					case 0:
						phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
						if (phaseType != PhaseType.Vapour)
						{
							phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Vapour;
						}
						break;
					case 1:
						phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
						switch (phaseType)
						{
						case PhaseType.Vapour:
							phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
							break;
						case PhaseType.Aqueous:
							if (mCalculatedPhases != CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquid)
							{
								phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
								phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
							}
							break;
						}
						break;
					case 2:
						phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
						switch (phaseType)
						{
						case PhaseType.Vapour:
							phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
							break;
						case PhaseType.Liquid:
							phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
							break;
						}
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("AdjustEquilibriumPhases1", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void AdjustEquilibriumPhases2(IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases combinationMethodForLiquidPhases = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine;
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			IPhase phase = null;
			IPhase[] array = null;
			PhaseType[] array2 = null;
			try
			{
				IPhase[] array3 = new Phase[2];
				array = array3;
				array[0] = (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(1).Clone();
				array[1] = (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(2).Clone();
				array2 = new PhaseType[2]
				{
					array[0].PhaseType,
					array[1].PhaseType
				};
				if (mCalculatedPhases == CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquid)
				{
					if (array2[0] != PhaseType.Vapour && array2[1] != PhaseType.Vapour)
					{
						combinationMethodForLiquidPhases = mCM;
						mCM = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.HysysMethodForWater_Oil;
						phase = new Phase();
						phase = (IPhase)array[0].Clone();
						CombinePhases(array, phase);
						phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
						phase.MolarFraction.Value = 1.0;
						phase.MassFraction.Value = 1.0;
						phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
						Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, phase);
						mCM = combinationMethodForLiquidPhases;
						phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Vapour;
						phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
						phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
						phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
						Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, phase);
					}
					else if (array2[0] == PhaseType.Aqueous || array2[1] == PhaseType.Aqueous)
					{
						phase = Stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Aqueous);
						phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
					}
				}
				if (mCalculatedPhases != CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid)
				{
					return;
				}
				Stream.NumberOfPhases = 3;
				if (array2[0] != PhaseType.Vapour && array2[1] != PhaseType.Vapour)
				{
					if (array[0].Density.Value < array[1].Density.Value && array[0].PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid)
					{
						num = 0;
						phase = (IPhase)array[0].Clone();
					}
					else
					{
						num = 1;
						phase = (IPhase)array[1].Clone();
					}
					phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Vapour;
					phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, phase);
					phase = array[num];
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, phase);
					phase = array[1 - num];
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(3, phase);
					return;
				}
				if (array2[0] == PhaseType.Vapour)
				{
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, array[0]);
					num2 = 1;
				}
				else if (array2[1] == PhaseType.Vapour)
				{
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, array[1]);
					num2 = 0;
				}
				if (array2[num2] == PhaseType.Liquid)
				{
					phase = array[num2];
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, phase);
					phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(3, phase);
				}
				else if (array2[num2] == PhaseType.Aqueous)
				{
					phase = array[num2];
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(3, phase);
					phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
					phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, phase);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("AdjustEquilibriumPhases2", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void AdjustEquilibriumPhases3(IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases combinationMethodForLiquidPhases = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine;
			int num = 0;
			int[] array = new int[2];
			int num2 = 0;
			IPhase[] array2 = new IPhase[2];
			int num3 = 0;
			int num4 = 0;
			IPhase phase = null;
			IPhase[] array3 = new IPhase[3];
			PhaseType[] array4 = new PhaseType[3];
			try
			{
				for (num = 0; num < 3; num++)
				{
					array3[num] = (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(num + 1).Clone();
					array4[num] = array3[num].PhaseType;
				}
				for (num = 0; num < 3; num++)
				{
					if (array4[num] == PhaseType.Vapour)
					{
						num4++;
						num2 = num;
					}
					else if (array4[num] == PhaseType.Liquid || array4[num] == PhaseType.Aqueous)
					{
						array[num3] = num;
						array2[num3] = array3[num];
						num3++;
					}
				}
				if (num4 != 1 || num3 != 2)
				{
					throw new Exception("Unexpected Phase Type In 3 Phases");
				}
				if (mCalculatedPhases == CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquid)
				{
					Stream.NumberOfPhases = 2;
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, array3[num2]);
					combinationMethodForLiquidPhases = mCM;
					mCM = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.HysysMethodForWater_Oil;
					phase = new Phase();
					phase = (IPhase)array3[0].Clone();
					CombinePhases(array2, phase);
					phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
					phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, phase);
					mCM = combinationMethodForLiquidPhases;
				}
				else if (mCalculatedPhases == CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid)
				{
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, array3[num2]);
					if (array2[0].Density.Value < array2[1].Density.Value && array2[0].PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid)
					{
						Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, array2[0]);
						Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(3, array2[1]);
					}
					else
					{
						Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, array2[1]);
						Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(3, array2[0]);
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("AdjustEquilibriumPhases3", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected string getPropertyNameByType(PropertyType propType)
		{
			string text = "";
			return propType switch
			{
				PropertyType.Enthalpy => "Enthalpy", 
				PropertyType.Entropy => "Entrop", 
				PropertyType.Cp => "Cp", 
				PropertyType.Cv => "Cv", 
				PropertyType.Density => "Molar Volume", 
				PropertyType.SurfaceTension => "Surface Tension", 
				PropertyType.Conductivity => "Thermal Conductivity", 
				PropertyType.Viscosity => "Viscosity", 
				PropertyType.SonicVelocity => "Sonic Velocity", 
				PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure => "dDensity/dP", 
				PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature => "dDensity/dT", 
				_ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("propType", "Unsupported property type"), 
			};
		}

		protected void LogMessage(MessageLogType aType, string aMessage)
		{
			if (mLog != null)
			{
				mLog.Add(aType, aMessage);
			}
		}

		protected void SetPhaseThermalProperty(IPhase phs, double t, double p, double h, double s, double mw, double v, double moleFrac, int nComp, double[] comp)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			phs.Temperature.Value = t;
			phs.Pressure.Value = p;
			phs.Enthalpy.Value = h / mw;
			phs.Entropy.Value = s / mw;
			phs.MolecularWeight.Value = mw;
			phs.Density.Value = mw / v;
			if (phs.PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour)
			{
				phs.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
			}
			else
			{
				phs.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
			}
			phs.MolarFraction.Value = moleFrac;
			for (int i = 0; i < nComp; i++)
			{
				phs.ComponentMoleFractions[i].Value = comp[i];
				num += comp[i];
			}
			if (Math.Abs(num - 1.0) > 0.0001)
			{
				throw new Exception("Mole fraction does not add to unit. Sum = " + num);
			}
		}

		protected void CheckStreamPhases(IFlowStream stream, int activePhases, bool vpalive, bool lp1alive, bool lp2alive)
		{
			try
			{
				if (stream.NumberOfPhases != activePhases)
				{
					stream.NumberOfPhases = activePhases;
				}
				switch (activePhases)
				{
				case 1:
					if (vpalive)
					{
						stream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType = PhaseType.Vapour;
					}
					else
					{
						stream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
					}
					break;
				case 2:
					if (vpalive)
					{
						stream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType = PhaseType.Vapour;
						stream.GetPhaseByID(2).PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
					}
					else
					{
						stream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
						stream.GetPhaseByID(2).PhaseType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
					}
					break;
				case 3:
					stream.GetPhaseByID(1).PhaseType = PhaseType.Vapour;
					stream.GetPhaseByID(2).PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
					stream.GetPhaseByID(3).PhaseType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
					break;
				default:
					throw new Exception("Unexpected unmber of phases, nPhases = " + activePhases);
				}
				IPhase phaseByID = stream.GetPhaseByID(0);
				int numberOfCompounds = phaseByID.NumberOfCompounds;
				for (int i = 1; i <= activePhases; i++)
				{
					IPhase phaseByID2 = stream.GetPhaseByID(i);
					phaseByID2.RemoveAllComponents();
					for (int j = 0; j < numberOfCompounds; j++)
					{
						phaseByID2.AddComponent(phaseByID.GetComponent(j));
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("check stream phases failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public virtual void Dispose()
		{
			if (server == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				if (flashManagerCookie != 0 && server != null)
				{
					server.RevokeSoftLicense(flashManagerCookie);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			try
			{
				if (propManagerCookie != 0 && server != null)
				{
					server.RevokeSoftLicense(propManagerCookie);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}
	}
	[Guid("3E3E287B-0574-455F-9D34-A8C53A5F6A9D")]
	public interface IPPPWithAspenProperties : IAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase, IPhysicalPropertyPackage
	{
		void InitFromFile(string fileName, IFlowStream stream);

		void LinkedFromHysys(IFlowStream stream);
	}
	[Guid("CA1B8E5A-F892-4904-BFE7-616C3CC770A6")]
	[ProgId("AspenTech.Hydraulics.PropertyUtilities.AHPPPWithAspenProperties")]
	[ClassInterface(ClassInterfaceType.None)]
	public class AHPPPWithAspenProperties : AHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase, IPPPWithAspenProperties, IAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase, IPhysicalPropertyPackage, IDisposable
	{
		protected bool mPropPkgSet;

		private IFlowStream refStream;

		[DllImport("Kernel32.dll")]
		public static extern bool SetEnvironmentVariable([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPStr)] string lpMyName, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPStr)] string lpMyValue);

		[DllImport("AspenPropertiesLink.dll")]
		private static extern void INITFROMPDFFILE([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPStr)] StringBuilder FileName, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int length);

		[DllImport("AspenPropertiesLink.dll")]
		private static extern void GETCOMPONENT(ref int index, ref double mw);

		[DllImport("AspenPropertiesLink.dll")]
		private static extern void GETNUMBEROFCOMPONENTS(ref int nComp);

		[DllImport("AspenPropertiesLink.dll")]
		private static extern void GETCOMPONENTNAME([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPStr)] StringBuilder Name, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int length);

		[DllImport("AspenPropertiesLink.dll")]
		private static extern void TESTSTRING([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPStr)] StringBuilder FileName, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.I4)] int length);

		[DllImport("AspenPropertiesLink.dll")]
		private unsafe static extern void CALCULATEPROPERTY(ref int PhaseType, ref int PropType, ref double t, ref double p, double* MoleFrac, ref double boy, ref int KBase, ref long ERR);

		[DllImport("AspenPropertiesLink.dll")]
		private unsafe static extern void CALCULATEPROPERTY2(ref int PhaseType, ref int PropType, ref double t, ref double p, double* MoleFrac, ref double boy, ref int KBase, ref long ERR);

		[DllImport("AspenPropertiesLink.dll")]
		private unsafe static extern void CALCULATEST(ref double t, ref double p, double* vapourMF, double* liquidMF, ref double boy, ref long ERR);

		[DllImport("AspenPropertiesLink.dll")]
		private static extern void FLSH_GETPFPROP(ref double solidPhaseFrac, ref double liquidPhaseFrac, ref double HV, ref double HL, ref double HL1, ref double HL2, ref double SV, ref double SL, ref double SL1, ref double SL2, ref double VV, ref double VL, ref double VL1, ref double VL2, ref double XMWV, ref double XMWL, ref double XMWL1, ref double XMWL2);

		[DllImport("AspenPropertiesLink.dll")]
		private unsafe static extern void DOFLASH(ref int flashType, ref double spec1, ref double spec2, double* moleFrac, ref int calculatedPhases, ref int whatPhase, ref double t, ref double p, ref double h, ref double vapourMoleFrac, ref double liquidMoleFrac, double* liquidMF, double* liquid1MF, double* liquid2MF, double* vapourMF, double* VLK, double* VLK2, ref int KBase, ref int error);

		public void InitFromFile(string fileName, IFlowStream stream)
		{
			try
			{
				initDefaults();
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(fileName);
				INITFROMPDFFILE(stringBuilder, stringBuilder.Length);
				getComponents(stream);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("InitFromFile failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public void LinkedFromHysys(IFlowStream stream)
		{
			try
			{
				initDefaults();
				getComponents(stream);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("LinkedFromHysys failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public override double CalculateBubblePoint(IFlowStream Stream, double Pressure)
		{
			try
			{
				return calculatAPropertyByFlashing(Stream, PhaseType.Overall, FlashType.PVFlash, Pressure, 0.0, PropertyType.Temperature);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("CalculateBubblePoint failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public override double CalculateDewPoint(IFlowStream Stream, double Pressure)
		{
			try
			{
				return calculatAPropertyByFlashing(Stream, PhaseType.Overall, FlashType.PVFlash, Pressure, 1.0, PropertyType.Temperature);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("CalculateDewPoint failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public override double CalculateProperty(IFlowStream Stream, PropertyType RT, PhaseType PT, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			try
			{
				return calculatAPropertyByFlashing(Stream, PT, FT, data1, data2, RT);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Calculate Property Failure", innerException);
			}
		}

		public override double CalculateSurfaceTension(IFlowStream Stream, PhaseType PT, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			try
			{
				return calculatAPropertyByFlashing(Stream, PT, FT, data1, data2, PropertyType.SurfaceTension);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("CalculateSurfaceTension failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public override void FlashStream(IFlowStream Stream, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			try
			{
				if (Stream.StreamID != mLastStreamID || mChangedComps == ChangedComponentsActionEnum.ReInitComThermo)
				{
					Check(Stream, Disable: true);
				}
				ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(Stream, FT, data1, data2);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("FlashStream Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		public override void ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(IFlowStream Stream, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			double num = -32767.0;
			int num2 = -32767;
			int num3 = -32767;
			try
			{
				base.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(Stream, FT, data1, data2);
				if (!mDoFlash)
				{
					return;
				}
				mLastStreamID = Stream.StreamID;
				DoFlash(Stream, FT, data1, data2);
				switch (FT)
				{
				case FlashType.PHFlash:
					num = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
					if (data2 != num)
					{
						if (Math.Abs(data2) > num)
						{
							if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / data2) > 0.001)
							{
								LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PH Flash");
							}
						}
						else if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / num) > 0.001)
						{
							LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PH Flash");
						}
					}
					Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value = data2;
					break;
				case FlashType.PSFlash:
					num = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Entropy.Value;
					if (data2 != num)
					{
						if (Math.Abs(data2) > Math.Abs(num))
						{
							if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / data2) > 0.001)
							{
								LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PS Flash");
							}
						}
						else if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / num) > 0.001)
						{
							LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PS Flash");
						}
					}
					Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Entropy.Value = data2;
					break;
				case FlashType.PVFlash:
					num = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarVapourFraction.Value;
					if (data2 != num)
					{
						if (num == 0.0)
						{
							if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / data2) > 0.001)
							{
								LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PV Flash");
							}
						}
						else if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / num) > 0.001)
						{
							LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PV Flash");
						}
					}
					Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarVapourFraction.Value = data2;
					break;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				if (mLog != null)
				{
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, "Flash Failure");
					string aMessage = "Flash Type = " + FT;
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					aMessage = "Data1 = " + data1.ToString("0.00000");
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					aMessage = "Data2 = " + data2.ToString("0.00000");
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					aMessage = "Calc = " + num.ToString("0.00000");
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					num3 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMoleFractions.Count;
					for (num2 = 0; num2 < num3; num2++)
					{
						aMessage = "Comp = " + (num2 + 1) + " : Mole Fraction = " + Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMoleFractions[num2].Value.ToString("0.000000000");
						LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					}
				}
				throw new Exception("ReflashStreamWithSameComposition Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected override void Check(IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			try
			{
				if (refStream == null)
				{
					refStream = new FlowStream();
					getComponents(refStream);
				}
				IPhase phaseByType = Stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall);
				IPhase phaseByType2 = refStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall);
				if (phaseByType.NumberOfCompounds != phaseByType2.NumberOfCompounds)
				{
					throw new Exception("The stream has different number of components to the initialised component list. ");
				}
				mReady = true;
			}
			finally
			{
				mCheckDisable = false;
			}
		}

		protected override void Check(IFlowStream Stream, bool Disable)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!mCheckDisable)
				{
					Check(Stream);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				mCheckDisable = false;
				if (Disable)
				{
					mCheckDisable = true;
				}
			}
		}

		private void getComponents(IFlowStream stream)
		{
			IPhase phaseByType = stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall);
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder("0                                                          ");
			int nComp = 0;
			double mw = 0.0;
			int num = 0;
			try
			{
				phaseByType.RemoveAllComponents();
				GETNUMBEROFCOMPONENTS(ref nComp);
				for (int i = 0; i < nComp; i++)
				{
					num = i + 1;
					GETCOMPONENT(ref num, ref mw);
					GETCOMPONENTNAME(stringBuilder, stringBuilder.Length);
					phaseByType.AddNewComponent(i + 1, stringBuilder.ToString().Trim(), mw);
					if (stringBuilder.ToString().Trim() == "WATER")
					{
						phaseByType.GetComponent(i).Alias = "Water";
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("GetComponents failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		private double[] convertFromRealValueArray(IFractionList list)
		{
			int count = list.Count;
			double[] array = new double[count];
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				array[i] = list[i].Value;
			}
			return array;
		}

		private unsafe double calculatAPropertyByFlashing(IFlowStream stream, PhaseType pt, FlashType ft, double data1, double data2, PropertyType proptype)
		{
			IPhase phaseByType = stream.GetPhaseByType(pt);
			int num = 0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			int numberOfCompounds = phaseByType.NumberOfCompounds;
			int num4 = 0;
			int whatPhase = 0;
			int PhaseType = (int)pt;
			int PropType = (int)proptype;
			double value = phaseByType.MolecularWeight.Value;
			double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			double t = 0.0;
			double p = 0.0;
			double h = 0.0;
			double vapourMoleFrac = 0.0;
			double liquidMoleFrac = 0.0;
			double[] array = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			double[] array2 = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			double[] array3 = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			double[] array4 = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			double[] array5 = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			double[] array6 = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			int error = 0;
			double solidPhaseFrac = 0.0;
			double liquidPhaseFrac = 0.0;
			double HV = 0.0;
			double HL = 0.0;
			double HL2 = 0.0;
			double HL3 = 0.0;
			double SV = 0.0;
			double SL = 0.0;
			double SL2 = 0.0;
			double SL3 = 0.0;
			double VV = 0.0;
			double VL = 0.0;
			double VL2 = 0.0;
			double VL3 = 0.0;
			double XMWV = 0.0;
			double XMWL = 0.0;
			double XMWL2 = 0.0;
			double XMWL3 = 0.0;
			double boy = 0.0;
			long ERR = 0L;
			int KBase = (int)thermoBase;
			Check(stream);
			num = ft switch
			{
				FlashType.PHFlash => 1, 
				FlashType.PTFlash => 2, 
				FlashType.PVFlash => 3, 
				FlashType.THFlash => 4, 
				FlashType.TVFlash => 5, 
				_ => throw new ArgumentException("Unsupported type", "FlashType"), 
			};
			if (num == 2)
			{
				num2 = data2;
				num3 = data1;
				p = data1;
				t = data2;
			}
			else
			{
				num2 = data1;
				num3 = ((num != 1 && num != 4) ? data2 : (data2 * phaseByType.MolecularWeight.Value));
			}
			double[] array7 = convertFromRealValueArray(phaseByType.ComponentMoleFractions);
			if (pt == AspenTech.Hydraulics.PropertyUtilities.PhaseType.Overall)
			{
				num4 = ((mCalculatedPhases != CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid && mCalculatedPhases != CalculatedPhasesEnum.Variable) ? 2 : 12);
			}
			else
			{
				num4 = 1;
				whatPhase = ((pt == AspenTech.Hydraulics.PropertyUtilities.PhaseType.Vapour) ? 1 : 2);
			}
			fixed (double* moleFrac = &array7[0])
			{
				fixed (double* liquidMF = &array[0])
				{
					fixed (double* liquid1MF = &array2[0])
					{
						fixed (double* liquid2MF = &array3[0])
						{
							fixed (double* vapourMF = &array4[0])
							{
								fixed (double* vLK = &array5[0])
								{
									fixed (double* vLK2 = &array6[0])
									{
										if (num != 2)
										{
											DOFLASH(ref num, ref num2, ref num3, moleFrac, ref num4, ref whatPhase, ref t, ref p, ref h, ref vapourMoleFrac, ref liquidMoleFrac, liquidMF, liquid1MF, liquid2MF, vapourMF, vLK, vLK2, ref KBase, ref error);
											if (error < 0)
											{
												throw new FlashFailedException(ft, data1, data2, "Flash failed in Aspen Properties.");
											}
										}
										if (num == 3)
										{
											result = t;
										}
										else
										{
											switch (proptype)
											{
											case PropertyType.SurfaceTension:
												if (pt == AspenTech.Hydraulics.PropertyUtilities.PhaseType.Vapour)
												{
													result = 0.0;
													break;
												}
												if (num == 2)
												{
													array = convertFromRealValueArray(phaseByType.ComponentMoleFractions);
													array4 = convertFromRealValueArray(stream.GetPhaseByType(AspenTech.Hydraulics.PropertyUtilities.PhaseType.Vapour).ComponentMoleFractions);
													CALCULATEST(ref num2, ref num3, vapourMF, liquidMF, ref boy, ref ERR);
												}
												else
												{
													CALCULATEST(ref t, ref p, vapourMF, liquidMF, ref boy, ref ERR);
												}
												result = boy;
												break;
											case PropertyType.Temperature:
												result = t;
												break;
											case PropertyType.Pressure:
												result = p;
												break;
											case PropertyType.Enthalpy:
											case PropertyType.Density:
											case PropertyType.Entropy:
											case PropertyType.MolecularWeight:
												FLSH_GETPFPROP(ref solidPhaseFrac, ref liquidPhaseFrac, ref HV, ref HL, ref HL2, ref HL3, ref SV, ref SL, ref SL2, ref SL3, ref VV, ref VL, ref VL2, ref VL3, ref XMWV, ref XMWL, ref XMWL2, ref XMWL3);
												if (pt == AspenTech.Hydraulics.PropertyUtilities.PhaseType.Vapour)
												{
													switch (proptype)
													{
													case PropertyType.Enthalpy:
														boy = HV / XMWV;
														break;
													case PropertyType.Entropy:
														boy = SV / XMWV;
														break;
													case PropertyType.MolecularWeight:
														boy = XMWV;
														break;
													case PropertyType.Density:
														boy = XMWV / VV;
														break;
													}
												}
												else if (mCalculatedPhases != CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid)
												{
													switch (proptype)
													{
													case PropertyType.Enthalpy:
														boy = HL / XMWL;
														break;
													case PropertyType.Entropy:
														boy = SL / XMWL;
														break;
													case PropertyType.MolecularWeight:
														boy = XMWL;
														break;
													case PropertyType.Density:
														boy = XMWL / VL;
														break;
													}
												}
												else if (pt == AspenTech.Hydraulics.PropertyUtilities.PhaseType.Liquid)
												{
													switch (proptype)
													{
													case PropertyType.Enthalpy:
														boy = HL2 / XMWL2;
														break;
													case PropertyType.Entropy:
														boy = SL2 / XMWL2;
														break;
													case PropertyType.MolecularWeight:
														boy = XMWL2;
														break;
													case PropertyType.Density:
														boy = XMWL2 / VL2;
														break;
													}
												}
												else
												{
													switch (proptype)
													{
													case PropertyType.Enthalpy:
														boy = HL3 / XMWL3;
														break;
													case PropertyType.Entropy:
														boy = SL3 / XMWL3;
														break;
													case PropertyType.MolecularWeight:
														boy = XMWL3;
														break;
													case PropertyType.Density:
														boy = XMWL3 / VL3;
														break;
													}
												}
												result = boy;
												break;
											case PropertyType.Cp:
											case PropertyType.Viscosity:
											case PropertyType.Conductivity:
												CALCULATEPROPERTY(ref PhaseType, ref PropType, ref t, ref p, moleFrac, ref boy, ref KBase, ref ERR);
												result = ((proptype != PropertyType.Cp) ? boy : (boy / value));
												break;
											case PropertyType.InternalEnergy:
											case PropertyType.Cv:
											case PropertyType.SonicVelocity:
											case PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure:
											case PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature:
											case PropertyType.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure:
											case PropertyType.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature:
												CALCULATEPROPERTY2(ref PhaseType, ref PropType, ref t, ref p, moleFrac, ref boy, ref KBase, ref ERR);
												switch (proptype)
												{
												case PropertyType.InternalEnergy:
												case PropertyType.Cv:
												case PropertyType.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure:
												case PropertyType.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature:
													result = boy / value;
													break;
												case PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure:
												case PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature:
													result = boy / value * 1000.0;
													break;
												case PropertyType.SonicVelocity:
													result = boy;
													break;
												}
												break;
											default:
												throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("proptype", proptype, "is not supported");
											}
										}
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private unsafe void DoFlash(IFlowStream stream, FlashType ft, double data1, double data2)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(data1) || double.IsNaN(data2))
			{
				throw new FlashFailedException(ft, data1, data2, "Flash failed with invalid target state.");
			}
			IPhase phaseByType = stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall);
			int num = 0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			int numberOfCompounds = phaseByType.NumberOfCompounds;
			int num4 = 0;
			int whatPhase = 0;
			double t = 0.0;
			double p = 0.0;
			double h = 0.0;
			double vapourMoleFrac = 0.0;
			double liquidMoleFrac = 0.0;
			double[] array = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			double[] array2 = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			double[] array3 = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			double[] array4 = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			double[] array5 = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			double[] array6 = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			int error = 0;
			int num5 = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			bool flag3 = false;
			double solidPhaseFrac = 0.0;
			double liquidPhaseFrac = 0.0;
			double HV = 0.0;
			double HL = 0.0;
			double HL2 = 0.0;
			double HL3 = 0.0;
			double SV = 0.0;
			double SL = 0.0;
			double SL2 = 0.0;
			double SL3 = 0.0;
			double VV = 0.0;
			double VL = 0.0;
			double VL2 = 0.0;
			double VL3 = 0.0;
			double XMWV = 0.0;
			double XMWL = 0.0;
			double XMWL2 = 0.0;
			double XMWL3 = 0.0;
			int KBase = (int)thermoBase;
			num = ft switch
			{
				FlashType.PHFlash => 1, 
				FlashType.PTFlash => 2, 
				FlashType.PVFlash => 3, 
				FlashType.THFlash => 4, 
				FlashType.TVFlash => 5, 
				FlashType.PSFlash => 6, 
				_ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("ft", ft, "Unsupported flash type"), 
			};
			if (num == 2)
			{
				num2 = data2;
				num3 = data1;
			}
			else
			{
				num2 = data1;
				num3 = ((num != 1 && num != 4 && num != 6) ? data2 : (data2 * phaseByType.MolecularWeight.Value));
			}
			double[] array7 = convertFromRealValueArray(phaseByType.ComponentMoleFractions);
			num4 = ((mCalculatedPhases != CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid && mCalculatedPhases != CalculatedPhasesEnum.Variable) ? 2 : 12);
			fixed (double* moleFrac = &array7[0])
			{
				fixed (double* ptr = &array[0])
				{
					fixed (double* ptr2 = &array2[0])
					{
						fixed (double* ptr3 = &array3[0])
						{
							fixed (double* ptr4 = &array4[0])
							{
								fixed (double* vLK = &array5[0])
								{
									fixed (double* vLK2 = &array6[0])
									{
										DOFLASH(ref num, ref num2, ref num3, moleFrac, ref num4, ref whatPhase, ref t, ref p, ref h, ref vapourMoleFrac, ref liquidMoleFrac, ptr, ptr2, ptr3, ptr4, vLK, vLK2, ref KBase, ref error);
										if (error < 0)
										{
											throw new FlashFailedException(ft, data1, data2, "Flash failed in Aspen Properties.");
										}
										FLSH_GETPFPROP(ref solidPhaseFrac, ref liquidPhaseFrac, ref HV, ref HL, ref HL2, ref HL3, ref SV, ref SL, ref SL2, ref SL3, ref VV, ref VL, ref VL2, ref VL3, ref XMWV, ref XMWL, ref XMWL2, ref XMWL3);
										switch (ft)
										{
										case FlashType.PHFlash:
											phaseByType.Temperature.Value = t;
											break;
										case FlashType.PTFlash:
											phaseByType.Enthalpy.Value = h / phaseByType.MolecularWeight.Value;
											break;
										case FlashType.PVFlash:
											phaseByType.Temperature.Value = t;
											phaseByType.Enthalpy.Value = h / phaseByType.MolecularWeight.Value;
											break;
										case FlashType.THFlash:
											phaseByType.Pressure.Value = p;
											phaseByType.Temperature.Value = t;
											break;
										case FlashType.TVFlash:
											phaseByType.Pressure.Value = p;
											phaseByType.Enthalpy.Value = h / phaseByType.MolecularWeight.Value;
											break;
										}
										phaseByType.MolarVapourFraction.Value = vapourMoleFrac;
										if (vapourMoleFrac > 0.0001)
										{
											flag = true;
											num5++;
										}
										if (vapourMoleFrac < 0.9999)
										{
											num5++;
											if (liquidMoleFrac > 0.9999)
											{
												flag2 = true;
											}
											else if (liquidMoleFrac < 0.0001)
											{
												flag3 = true;
											}
											else
											{
												flag2 = true;
												flag3 = true;
												num5++;
											}
										}
										CheckStreamPhases(stream, num5, flag, flag2, flag3);
										if (flag)
										{
											IPhase phaseByType2 = stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
											SetPhaseThermalProperty(phaseByType2, t, p, HV, SV, XMWV, VV, vapourMoleFrac, numberOfCompounds, array4);
											calculatePhaseProperties(phaseByType2, t, p, ptr4);
										}
										if (flag2)
										{
											switch (num4)
											{
											case 12:
											{
												double moleFrac3 = liquidMoleFrac * liquidPhaseFrac;
												double boy2 = 0.0;
												long ERR2 = 0L;
												IPhase phaseByType3 = stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid);
												SetPhaseThermalProperty(phaseByType3, t, p, HL2, SL2, XMWL2, VL2, moleFrac3, numberOfCompounds, array2);
												CALCULATEST(ref t, ref p, ptr4, ptr2, ref boy2, ref ERR2);
												phaseByType3.SurfaceTension.Value = boy2;
												calculatePhaseProperties(phaseByType3, t, p, ptr2);
												break;
											}
											case 2:
											{
												double moleFrac2 = 1.0 - vapourMoleFrac;
												double boy = 0.0;
												long ERR = 0L;
												IPhase phaseByType3 = stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid);
												SetPhaseThermalProperty(phaseByType3, t, p, HL, SL, XMWL, VL, moleFrac2, numberOfCompounds, array);
												CALCULATEST(ref t, ref p, ptr4, ptr, ref boy, ref ERR);
												phaseByType3.SurfaceTension.Value = boy;
												calculatePhaseProperties(phaseByType3, t, p, ptr);
												break;
											}
											}
										}
										if (flag3)
										{
											double moleFrac4 = (1.0 - liquidMoleFrac) * liquidPhaseFrac;
											double boy3 = 0.0;
											long ERR3 = 0L;
											IPhase phaseByType4 = stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Aqueous);
											if (!flag2)
											{
												phaseByType4 = stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid);
											}
											SetPhaseThermalProperty(phaseByType4, t, p, HL3, SL3, XMWL3, VL3, moleFrac4, numberOfCompounds, array3);
											CALCULATEST(ref t, ref p, ptr4, ptr3, ref boy3, ref ERR3);
											phaseByType4.SurfaceTension.Value = boy3;
											calculatePhaseProperties(phaseByType4, t, p, ptr3);
										}
										UpdateOverallPhase(stream, num5);
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private unsafe void calculatePhaseProperties(IPhase phs, double t, double p, double* moleFrac)
		{
			double boy = 0.0;
			long ERR = 0L;
			int PhaseType = (int)phs.PhaseType;
			int num = 0;
			double value = phs.MolecularWeight.Value;
			int KBase = (int)thermoBase;
			phs.InternalEnergy.Value = phs.Enthalpy.Value - p / phs.Density.Value;
			num = 6;
			CALCULATEPROPERTY2(ref PhaseType, ref num, ref t, ref p, moleFrac, ref boy, ref KBase, ref ERR);
			phs.Cv.Value = boy / value;
			num = 7;
			CALCULATEPROPERTY(ref PhaseType, ref num, ref t, ref p, moleFrac, ref boy, ref KBase, ref ERR);
			phs.Cp.Value = boy / value;
			if (flashLevel > FlashCalculationLevelEnum.Thermal)
			{
				num = 9;
				CALCULATEPROPERTY(ref PhaseType, ref num, ref t, ref p, moleFrac, ref boy, ref KBase, ref ERR);
				phs.Viscosity.Value = boy;
				num = 10;
				CALCULATEPROPERTY(ref PhaseType, ref num, ref t, ref p, moleFrac, ref boy, ref KBase, ref ERR);
				phs.Conductivity.Value = boy;
			}
			if (flashLevel > FlashCalculationLevelEnum.ThermalAndTransport)
			{
				num = 16;
				CALCULATEPROPERTY2(ref PhaseType, ref num, ref t, ref p, moleFrac, ref boy, ref KBase, ref ERR);
				phs.SonicVelocity.Value = boy;
			}
			if (flashLevel > FlashCalculationLevelEnum.PlusSonicVelocity)
			{
				num = 17;
				CALCULATEPROPERTY2(ref PhaseType, ref num, ref t, ref p, moleFrac, ref boy, ref KBase, ref ERR);
				phs.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure.Value = boy * value;
				num = 18;
				CALCULATEPROPERTY2(ref PhaseType, ref num, ref t, ref p, moleFrac, ref boy, ref KBase, ref ERR);
				phs.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature.Value = boy * value;
				num = 19;
				CALCULATEPROPERTY2(ref PhaseType, ref num, ref t, ref p, moleFrac, ref boy, ref KBase, ref ERR);
				phs.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure.Value = boy / value;
				num = 20;
				CALCULATEPROPERTY2(ref PhaseType, ref num, ref t, ref p, moleFrac, ref boy, ref KBase, ref ERR);
				phs.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature.Value = boy / value;
			}
		}

		private static void SetAPRSystemPath()
		{
			Version version = typeof(AHPPPWithAspenProperties).Assembly.GetName().Version;
			string text = version.Major + "." + version.Minor;
			string text2 = (Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\APrSystem\\" + text + "\\aplus") ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Can't find APrSystem key. APrSystem must be installed under AspenTech\\AprSystem. ")).GetValue("ASPTOP").ToString() + "\\Xeq\\";
			string environmentVariable = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH");
			string lpMyValue = text2 + ";" + environmentVariable;
			SetEnvironmentVariable("PATH", lpMyValue);
		}

		private static void SetOOMFPath()
		{
			Version version = typeof(AHPPPWithAspenProperties).Assembly.GetName().Version;
			string text = version.Major + "." + version.Minor;
			string text2 = (Registry.LocalMachine.OpenSubKey("SOFTWARE\\AspenTech\\OOMF\\" + text) ?? throw new InvalidOperationException("Can't find OOMF key. OOMF must be installed under AspenTech\\OOMF. ")).GetValue("OOMFTOP").ToString() + "\\Xeq\\";
			string environmentVariable = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("PATH");
			string lpMyValue = text2 + ";" + environmentVariable;
			SetEnvironmentVariable("PATH", lpMyValue);
		}

		private void initDefaults()
		{
			SetAPRSystemPath();
			SetOOMFPath();
		}
	}

		public override double CalculateProperty(IFlowStream Stream, PropertyType RT, PhaseType PT, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			//IL_00a5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ab: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			IEnumeratedPropertyContainer val = null;
			int num = -32767;
			double num2 = -32767.0;
			int num3 = -32767;
			double result = -32767.0;
			try
			{
				if (Stream.StreamID != mLastStreamID)
				{
					mReady = false;
				}
				else if (mChangedComps == ChangedComponentsActionEnum.ReInitComThermo)
				{
					Check(Stream);
				}
				if (!mReady)
				{
					setupPhaseCollection(Stream as IBlackOilStream);
				}
				DoFlash(FT, data1, data2);
				mLastStreamID = Stream.StreamID;
				num3 = mPhaseCollection.NumberOfActivePhases;
				for (num = 0; num < num3; num++)
				{
					ref IBasicPhase reference = ref mBasicPhase;
					object activePhase = mPhaseCollection.GetActivePhase(num);
					reference = (IBasicPhase)((activePhase is COTHPhase) ? activePhase : null);
					if ((int)mBasicPhase.PhaseType != (int)PT)
					{
						continue;
					}
					IBasicPhase obj = mBasicPhase;
					val = (IEnumeratedPropertyContainer)(object)((obj is IEnumeratedPropertyContainer) ? obj : null);
					switch (RT)
					{
					case PropertyType.Pressure:
						result = mBasicPhase.Pressure * 1000.0;
						break;
					case PropertyType.Temperature:
						result = mBasicPhase.Temperature;
						break;
					case PropertyType.MolecularWeight:
						result = num2;
						break;
					case PropertyType.Density:
						result = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_MassDensity);
						break;
					case PropertyType.Enthalpy:
						result = mBasicPhase.Enthalpy * 1000.0;
						break;
					case PropertyType.InternalEnergy:
						result = mBasicPhase.Enthalpy - mBasicPhase.Pressure / (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_MassDensity);
						break;
					case PropertyType.Cp:
						result = mBasicPhase.Cp * 1000.0;
						break;
					case PropertyType.Cv:
						try
						{
							result = mBasicPhase.Cp * 1000.0 / (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_HeatCapacityRatio);
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
							mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_Cp, (object)mBasicPhase);
							result = ((double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_Cp) - 8314.34) / num2 * 1000.0;
						}
						break;
					case PropertyType.Conductivity:
						result = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_ThermalConductivity);
						break;
					case PropertyType.Viscosity:
						result = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_Viscosity);
						break;
					default:
						throw new Exception("Property Not Supported");
					}
					break;
				}
				return result;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Calculate Property Failure", innerException);
			}
		}

		public override double CalculateSurfaceTension(IFlowStream Stream, PhaseType PT, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			//IL_006f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			if (Stream.StreamID != mLastStreamID)
			{
				mReady = false;
			}
			else if (mChangedComps == ChangedComponentsActionEnum.ReInitComThermo)
			{
				Check(Stream);
			}
			if (!mReady)
			{
				setupPhaseCollection(Stream as IBlackOilStream);
			}
			DoFlash(FT, data1, data2);
			mLastStreamID = Stream.StreamID;
			ref IBasicPhase reference = ref mBasicPhase;
			object obj = mPhaseCollection.LocatePhase((tagPhaseTypeEnum)1, 0);
			reference = (IBasicPhase)((obj is COTHPhase) ? obj : null);
			return (double)((IEnumeratedPropertyContainer)mBasicPhase).GetPropertyValue(mID_SurfaceTension);
		}

		protected override void EvaluatePhaseProperties(IFlowStream Stream, int PhaseID, bool isFullSet)
		{
			//IL_0318: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_031e: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_0326: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_032c: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00a7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b1: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			IEnumeratedPropertyContainer val = null;
			IPhase phase = null;
			try
			{
				phase = Stream.GetPhaseByID(PhaseID);
				IBasicPhase obj = mBasicPhase;
				val = (IEnumeratedPropertyContainer)(object)((obj is IEnumeratedPropertyContainer) ? obj : null);
				if (mBasicPhase.Pressure != RealValue.FEMPTY)
				{
					phase.Pressure.Value = mBasicPhase.Pressure * 1000.0;
				}
				else
				{
					phase.Pressure.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				}
				if (mBasicPhase.Temperature != RealValue.FEMPTY)
				{
					phase.Temperature.Value = mBasicPhase.Temperature + 273.15;
				}
				else
				{
					phase.Temperature.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				}
				phase.PhaseType = (PhaseType)mBasicPhase.PhaseType;
				phase.MassFraction.Value = mBasicPhase.PhaseMassFraction;
				if (phase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour)
				{
					phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
					phase.Entropy.Value = mBasicPhase.Entropy;
				}
				else
				{
					phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
				}
				if (phase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
				{
					phase.MolecularWeight.Value = 18.016;
				}
				phase.VolumeFraction.Value = mBasicPhase.PhaseFraction;
				if (mBasicPhase.Enthalpy != RealValue.FEMPTY)
				{
					phase.Enthalpy.Value = mBasicPhase.Enthalpy * 1000.0;
				}
				else
				{
					phase.Enthalpy.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				}
				phase.Density.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_MassDensity);
				if (!phase.Density.IsUnknown && phase.Enthalpy.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY)
				{
					phase.InternalEnergy.Value = phase.Enthalpy.Value - phase.Pressure.Value / phase.Density.Value;
				}
				phase.Cp.Value = mBasicPhase.Cp * 1000.0;
				if (mCvSupported)
				{
					try
					{
						mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_Cv, (object)mBasicPhase);
						phase.Cv.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_Cv) * 1000.0;
						if (phase.Cv.Value > phase.Cp.Value || phase.Cv.Value < 0.0)
						{
							string message = "Cv = " + phase.Cv.Value;
							phase.Cv.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
							throw new Exception(message);
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						mCvSupported = false;
					}
				}
				else if (phase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour)
				{
					phase.Cv.Value = mBasicPhase.Cp * 1000.0 / (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_HeatCapacityRatio);
				}
				else
				{
					phase.Cv.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				}
				if ((int)mBasicPhase.PhaseType == 4 || (int)mBasicPhase.PhaseType == 5)
				{
					mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_SurfaceTension, (object)mBasicPhase);
					phase.SurfaceTension.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_SurfaceTension) / 1000.0;
				}
				if (flashLevel >= FlashCalculationLevelEnum.ThermalAndTransport)
				{
					phase.Conductivity.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_ThermalConductivity) * 1000.0;
					phase.Viscosity.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_Viscosity) * 0.001;
				}
				if (flashLevel >= FlashCalculationLevelEnum.PlusSonicVelocity && mID_SV > 0)
				{
					mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_SV, (object)mBasicPhase);
					phase.SonicVelocity.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_SV);
				}
				if (flashLevel >= FlashCalculationLevelEnum.PlusDerivertive)
				{
					getDerivatives(phase);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("EvaluatePhaseProperties failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public override void CombinePhases(IPhase[] Phases, IPhase NewPhase)
		{
			int num = -32767;
			try
			{
				GetOverallStateParameters(Phases, NewPhase);
				double num2 = 0.0;
				double num3 = 0.0;
				for (num = 0; num < Phases.GetLength(0); num++)
				{
					num2 += Phases[num].MassFraction.Value * Phases[num].Cp.Value;
					num3 += Phases[num].MassFraction.Value * Phases[num].Cv.Value;
				}
				NewPhase.Cp.Value = num2 / NewPhase.MassFraction.Value;
				NewPhase.Cv.Value = num3 / NewPhase.MassFraction.Value;
				NewPhase.PhaseType = Phases[0].PhaseType;
				CombineTransportProperty(Phases, NewPhase);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("CombinePhases Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected override void GetOverallStateParameters(IPhase[] Phases, IPhase OverallPhase)
		{
			double num = -32767.0;
			double num2 = -32767.0;
			double num3 = -32767.0;
			int num4 = -32767;
			double num5 = -32767.0;
			int num6 = -32767;
			double num7 = -32767.0;
			num6 = Phases.GetLength(0);
			num2 = 0.0;
			num3 = 0.0;
			num5 = 0.0;
			num7 = 0.0;
			num = 0.0;
			for (num4 = 0; num4 < num6; num4++)
			{
				num2 += Phases[num4].Enthalpy.Value * Phases[num4].MassFraction.Value;
				num3 += Phases[num4].Entropy.Value * Phases[num4].MassFraction.Value;
				num5 += Phases[num4].InternalEnergy.Value * Phases[num4].MassFraction.Value;
				num7 += Phases[num4].MassFraction.Value;
				num += Phases[num4].MassFraction.Value / Phases[num4].Density.Value;
			}
			num2 /= num7;
			num3 /= num7;
			num5 /= num7;
			OverallPhase.Density.Value = num7 / num;
			OverallPhase.Enthalpy.Value = num2;
			OverallPhase.Entropy.Value = num3;
			OverallPhase.InternalEnergy.Value = num5;
			OverallPhase.MassFraction.Value = num7;
		}

		protected override void GetPropertyID()
		{
			base.GetPropertyID();
			mID_SpecificGravity = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).AddPropertyByName("Specific Gravity");
			mID_MassFlow = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).AddPropertyByName("Mass Flow");
			mID_HeComp = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).AddPropertyByName("Limited Composition - He");
			mID_O2Comp = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).AddPropertyByName("Limited Composition - 02");
			mID_N2Comp = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).AddPropertyByName("Limited Composition - N2");
			mID_CO2Comp = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).AddPropertyByName("Limited Composition - C02");

			mID_ViscTempMatrix = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).AddPropertyByName("Viscosity/Temperature Matrix");
			mID_WatsonK = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).AddPropertyByName("Watson K Factor");

			mID_HeatCapacityRatio = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).AddPropertyByName("HeatCapacityRatio");
