		public override bool Validate(IFlowStream stream)
		{
			//IL_0061: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_006b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0181: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_018b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_02e5: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_02ef: Expected O, but got Unknown
			IBlackOilStream blackOilStream = null;
			IEnumeratedPropertyContainer val = null;
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			double num4 = 0.0;
			double num5 = 0.0;
			try
			{
				if (stream is IBlackOilStream)
				{
					blackOilStream = stream as IBlackOilStream;
					for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++)
					{
						mBasicPhase = (IBasicPhase)mPhaseCollection.GetEquilibriumPhase(i);
						IBasicPhase obj = mBasicPhase;
						val = (IEnumeratedPropertyContainer)(object)((obj is IEnumeratedPropertyContainer) ? obj : null);
						num = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_MassFlow);
						num3 = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_SpecificGravity);
						switch (i)
						{
						case 0:
							num2 = blackOilStream.GasMassFlowrate.Value;
							num4 = blackOilStream.GasGravity.Value;
							break;
						case 1:
							num2 = blackOilStream.OilMassFlowrate.Value;
							num4 = blackOilStream.OilGravity.Value;
							break;
						case 2:
							num2 = blackOilStream.WaterMassFlowrate.Value;
							num4 = blackOilStream.WaterGravity.Value;
							break;
						}
						if (num2 != 0.0 && Math.Abs(num / num2 - 1.0) > 0.001)
						{
							return false;
						}
						if (num4 != 0.0 && Math.Abs(num3 / num4 - 1.0) > 0.001)
						{
							return false;
						}
					}
					mBasicPhase = (IBasicPhase)mPhaseCollection.LocatePhase((tagPhaseTypeEnum)1, 0);
					IBasicPhase obj2 = mBasicPhase;
					val = (IEnumeratedPropertyContainer)(object)((obj2 is IEnumeratedPropertyContainer) ? obj2 : null);
					num5 = blackOilStream.OilViscosityParamA.Value;
					if (num5 != 0.0 && Math.Abs(1.0 - (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_CoeffA) / num5) > 0.001)
					{
						return false;
					}
					num5 = blackOilStream.OilViscosityParamB.Value;
					if (num5 != 0.0 && Math.Abs(1.0 - (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_CoeffB) / num5) > 0.001)
					{
						return false;
					}
					num5 = blackOilStream.OilWatsonK.Value;
					if (num5 != 0.0 && Math.Abs(1.0 - (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_WatsonK) / num5) > 0.001)
					{
						return false;
					}
					num5 = blackOilStream.UserSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value;
					if (num5 != 0.0 && Math.Abs(1.0 - (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_SurfaceTension) / num5) > 0.001)
					{
						return false;
					}
					mBasicPhase = (IBasicPhase)mPhaseCollection.LocatePhase((tagPhaseTypeEnum)3, 0);
					int numberOfCompounds = mBasicPhase.NumberOfCompounds;
					if (numberOfCompounds != blackOilStream.GetPhaseByID(0).NumberOfCompounds)
					{
						return false;
					}
					IPhase phaseByID = blackOilStream.GetPhaseByID(0);
					object molarComposition = mBasicPhase.MolarComposition;
					IRealArrayDisp val2 = (IRealArrayDisp)((molarComposition is IRealArrayDisp) ? molarComposition : null);
					for (int j = 0; j < numberOfCompounds; j++)
					{
						num2 = phaseByID.ComponentMoleFractions[j].Value;
						num5 = val2[j];
						if (Math.Abs(num2 - num5) > 0.0001)
						{
							return false;
						}
					}
					return true;
				}
				throw new ArgumentException("A black oil stream is required. ", "stream");
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Validate failed for black oil stream. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected override void Check(IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!mCheckDisable && mValidateFlowStream && !Validate(Stream))
				{
					if (mChangedComps == ChangedComponentsActionEnum.ThrowException)
					{
						throw new Exception("Component List Has Changed");
					}
					mReady = false;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				mCheckDisable = false;
			}
		}

		public override void FlashStream(IFlowStream Stream, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(Stream, FT, data1, data2);
		}

		public override void ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(IFlowStream Stream, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			double num = -32767.0;
			try
			{
				base.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(Stream, FT, data1, data2);
				if (!mDoFlash)
				{
					return;
				}
				setupPhaseCollection(Stream as IBlackOilStream);
				DoFlash(FT, data1, data2);
				UpdateStream(Stream, IsFullSet: true);
				mLastStreamID = Stream.StreamID;
				if (FT != FlashType.PHFlash)
				{
					return;
				}
				num = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
				if (data2 != num)
				{
					if (Math.Abs(data2) > num)
					{
						if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / data2) > 0.001)
						{
							throw new Exception("PH Flash Failure");
						}
					}
					else if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / num) > 0.001)
					{
						throw new Exception("PH Flash Failure");
					}
				}
				Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value = data2;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("ReflashStreamWithSameComposition Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected override void SetParameters(FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			try
			{
				switch (FT)
				{
				case FlashType.PHFlash:
					num = data1;
					break;
				case FlashType.PTFlash:
					num = data1;
					num2 = data2;
					break;
				default:
					throw new ArgumentException("Unsupported flash type. ", "FlashType");
				}
				if (num < 0.0)
				{
					throw new Exception("Pressure can not be set less than zero. ");
				}
				if (num2 < 0.0)
				{
					throw new Exception("Temperature can not be set less than zero. ");
				}
				ref IBasicPhase reference = ref mBasicPhase;
				object obj = mPhaseCollection.LocatePhase((tagPhaseTypeEnum)1, 0);
				reference = (IBasicPhase)((obj is IBasicPhase) ? obj : null);
				switch (FT)
				{
				case FlashType.PHFlash:
					mBasicPhase.Pressure = 0.001 * data1;
					mBasicPhase.Enthalpy = 0.001 * data2;
					break;
				case FlashType.PTFlash:
					mBasicPhase.Pressure = 0.001 * data1;
					mBasicPhase.Temperature = data2 - 273.15;
					break;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("SetParameters Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected override void UpdateStream(IFlowStream Stream, bool IsFullSet)
		{
			int num = -32767;
			int num2 = -32767;
			int num3 = -37767;
			try
			{
				num3 = mPhaseCollection.MaximumNumberOfEquilibriumPhases;
				if (Stream.NumberOfPhases != 3)
				{
					Stream.NumberOfPhases = 3;
				}
				num2 = 0;
				for (num = 0; num < num3; num++)
				{
					ref IBasicPhase reference = ref mBasicPhase;
					object equilibriumPhase = mPhaseCollection.GetEquilibriumPhase(num);
					reference = (IBasicPhase)((equilibriumPhase is COTHPhase) ? equilibriumPhase : null);
					if (mBasicPhase.Pressure == RealValue.FEMPTY && mBasicPhase.PhaseMassFraction <= 0.0 && mCM != CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine)
					{
						Stream.NumberOfPhases--;
						continue;
					}
					num2++;
					EvaluatePhaseProperties(Stream, num2, IsFullSet);
				}
				UpdateOverallPhase(Stream, num2);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("UpdateStream Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected override void UpdateOverallPhase(IFlowStream Stream, int ActiveCount)
		{
			IPhase phase = null;
			IEnumeratedPropertyContainer val = null;
			IRealValue realValue = new RealValue();
			try
			{
				phase = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0);
				ref IBasicPhase reference = ref mBasicPhase;
				object obj = mPhaseCollection.LocatePhase((tagPhaseTypeEnum)1, 0);
				reference = (IBasicPhase)((obj is COTHPhase) ? obj : null);
				IBasicPhase obj2 = mBasicPhase;
				val = (IEnumeratedPropertyContainer)(object)((obj2 is IEnumeratedPropertyContainer) ? obj2 : null);
				if (mBasicPhase.Temperature == RealValue.FEMPTY || mBasicPhase.Pressure == RealValue.FEMPTY)
				{
					double num = RealValue.FEMPTY;
					double num2 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
					for (int i = 1; i <= Stream.NumberOfPhases; i++)
					{
						IPhase phaseByID = Stream.GetPhaseByID(i);
						num = phaseByID.Temperature.Value;
						num2 = phaseByID.Pressure.Value;
						if (num != RealValue.FEMPTY && num2 != RealValue.FEMPTY)
						{
							break;
						}
					}
					if (num == RealValue.FEMPTY || num2 == RealValue.FEMPTY)
					{
						throw new Exception("Temperature & Pressure are empty.");
					}
					phase.Temperature.Value = num;
					phase.Pressure.Value = num2;
				}
				else
				{
					phase.Temperature.Value = mBasicPhase.Temperature + 273.15;
					phase.Pressure.Value = mBasicPhase.Pressure * 1000.0;
				}
				phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Overall;
				phase.Enthalpy.Value = mBasicPhase.Enthalpy * 1000.0;
				phase.Entropy.Value = mBasicPhase.Entropy * 1000.0;
				phase.Cp.Value = mBasicPhase.Cp * 1000.0;
				phase.Density.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_MassDensity);
				phase.Viscosity.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_Viscosity) * 0.001;
				phase.Conductivity.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_ThermalCondMassBlend) * 1000.0;
				phase.SurfaceTension.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_SurfaceTension) * 0.001;
				phase.MassFraction.Value = 1.0;
				(Stream as IBlackOilStream).UserSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value = phase.SurfaceTension.Value;
				realValue.Value = (Stream as IBlackOilStream).OilWatsonK.Value;
				if (realValue.Value == 0.0 || realValue.Value == RealValue.FEMPTY)
				{
					(Stream as IBlackOilStream).OilWatsonK.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_WatsonK);
				}
				try
				{
					(Stream as IBlackOilStream).GasTc.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_CritTemp) + 273.15;
					(Stream as IBlackOilStream).GasPc.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_CritPressure) * 1000.0;
				}
				catch (Exception ex)
				{
					ex.ToString();
				}
				(Stream as IBlackOilStream).OilBubblePtCalSolnGor.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_SolutionGOR);
				(Stream as IBlackOilStream).OilBubblePtCalFvf.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_OilFormationVolumeFactor);
				if ((Stream as IBlackOilStream).OilViscosityParamA.Value == RealValue.FEMPTY || (Stream as IBlackOilStream).OilViscosityParamB.Value == RealValue.FEMPTY || ((Stream as IBlackOilStream).OilViscosityParamA.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY && (Stream as IBlackOilStream).DeadOilViscosityCorrelation != "Specify Coefficients"))
				{
					(Stream as IBlackOilStream).OilViscosityParamA.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_CoeffA);
					(Stream as IBlackOilStream).OilViscosityParamB.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_CoeffB);
				}
				if (mCM != CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine)
				{
					base.UpdateOverallPhase(Stream, ActiveCount);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("UpdateOverallPhase Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		internal override void getComponentsFromCOMThermo(IFlowStream stream)
		{
			try
			{
				mReady = false;
				mLastStreamID = stream.StreamID;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("getComponentsFromCOMThermo failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void SetPropertyPackage()
		{
			try
			{
				mFlashOptions = mThermoPath + "NeotechBlackOil_flash.xml";
				mLiquidPkg = mThermoPath + "NeotechBlackOil_liquid.xml";
				mVapourPkg = mThermoPath + "NeotechBlackOil_vapour.xml";
				PropertyPackageSetup();
				mIsPPSet = true;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("SetPropertyPackage failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void setupPhaseCollection(IBlackOilStream stream)
		{
			//IL_0023: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00c8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00ce: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_033f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0349: Expected O, but got Unknown
			IEnumeratedPropertyContainer val = null;
			int numberOfViscosityPoints = stream.NumberOfViscosityPoints;
			try
			{
				mPhaseCollection.ResetCollection(0);
				mBasicPhase = (IBasicPhase)mPhaseCollection.LocatePhase((tagPhaseTypeEnum)1, 0);
				mBasicPhase.ResetProperties(0);
				IBasicPhase obj = mBasicPhase;
				val = (IEnumeratedPropertyContainer)(object)((obj is IEnumeratedPropertyContainer) ? obj : null);
				double value = stream.OilViscosityParamA.Value;
				double value2 = stream.OilViscosityParamB.Value;
				string deadOilViscosityCorrelation = stream.DeadOilViscosityCorrelation;
				if (value == RealValue.FEMPTY && value2 == RealValue.FEMPTY)
				{
					switch (deadOilViscosityCorrelation)
					{
					case null:
					case "":
					case "Specify Coefficients":
						throw new ArgumentNullException("Viscosity correlation or coefficients has not been set properly. ");
					}
					val.PutPropertyValue(mID_DeadOilViscosityCorrelation, (object)deadOilViscosityCorrelation);
					val.PutPropertyValue(mID_ViscTempMatrixSize, (object)numberOfViscosityPoints);
					IRealMatrix val2 = (IRealMatrix)new COTHRealMatrixClass();
					val2.NumberOfColumns = 2;
					val2.NumberOfRows = numberOfViscosityPoints;
					for (int i = 0; i < numberOfViscosityPoints; i++)
					{
						val2[i, 0] = stream.ViscosityPointValue(i).Value;
						val2[i, 1] = stream.TemperatureAtViscosityPoint(i).Value;
					}
					val.PutPropertyValue(mID_ViscTempMatrix, (object)val2);
				}
				else
				{
					deadOilViscosityCorrelation = "Specify Coefficients";
					val.PutPropertyValue(mID_DeadOilViscosityCorrelation, (object)deadOilViscosityCorrelation);
					val.PutPropertyValue(mID_CoeffA, (object)value);
					val.PutPropertyValue(mID_CoeffB, (object)value2);
				}
				val.PutPropertyValue(mID_WatsonK, (object)stream.OilWatsonK.Value);
				val.PutPropertyValue(mID_SurfaceTension, (object)stream.UserSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value);
				val.PutPropertyValue(mID_BubblePressure, (object)stream.OilBubblePtCalPressure.Value);
				val.PutPropertyValue(mID_BubbleTemperature, (object)stream.OilBubblePtCalTemperature.Value);
				double num = ((stream.OilGravity.Value > 0.0) ? (stream.OilMassFlowrate.Value / stream.OilGravity.Value) : RealValue.FEMPTY);
				double num2 = ((stream.WaterGravity.Value > 0.0) ? (stream.WaterMassFlowrate.Value / stream.WaterGravity.Value) : RealValue.FEMPTY);
				val.PutPropertyValue(mID_SolutionGOR, (object)stream.OilBubblePtCalSolnGor.Value);
				val.PutPropertyValue(mID_OilFormationVolumeFactor, (object)stream.OilBubblePtCalFvf.Value);
				val.PutPropertyValue(mID_Viscosity, (object)stream.OilBubblePtCalLiveViscosity.Value);
				val.PutPropertyValue(mID_ProducedGOR, (object)stream.ProducedGOR.Value);
				double num3 = ((num2 != RealValue.FEMPTY && num != RealValue.FEMPTY) ? (100.0 * num2 / (num2 + num)) : RealValue.FEMPTY);
				val.PutPropertyValue(mID_WaterCut, (object)num3);
				double num4 = ((num2 != RealValue.FEMPTY && num != RealValue.FEMPTY) ? (num2 / num) : RealValue.FEMPTY);
				val.PutPropertyValue(mID_WOR, (object)num4);
				for (int j = 0; j < 3; j++)
				{
					val = null;
					mBasicPhase = null;
					mBasicPhase = (IBasicPhase)mPhaseCollection.GetEquilibriumPhase(j);
					mBasicPhase.ResetProperties(0);
					IBasicPhase obj2 = mBasicPhase;
					val = (IEnumeratedPropertyContainer)(object)((obj2 is IEnumeratedPropertyContainer) ? obj2 : null);
					switch (j)
					{
					case 0:
						mBasicPhase.PhaseType = (tagPhaseTypeEnum)3;
						val.PutPropertyValue(mID_SpecificGravity, (object)stream.GasGravity.Value);
						val.PutPropertyValue(mID_GasPropertySelection, (object)1.0);
						if (stream.IsUsingLimitedComposition)
						{
							IPhase phaseByType = stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall);
							int numberOfCompounds = phaseByType.NumberOfCompounds;
							for (int k = 0; k < numberOfCompounds; k++)
							{
								IComponent component = phaseByType.GetComponent(k);
								double value3 = phaseByType.ComponentMoleFractions[k].Value;
								switch (k)
								{
								case 0:
									if (component.Name == "Helium")
									{
										val.PutPropertyValue(mID_HeComp, (object)value3);
										break;
									}
									throw new Exception("Vapour phase composition mis-macthed");
								case 1:
									if (component.Name == "Oxygen")
									{
										val.PutPropertyValue(mID_O2Comp, (object)value3);
										break;
									}
									throw new Exception("Vapour phase composition mis-macthed");
								case 2:
									if (component.Name == "Nitrogen")
									{
										val.PutPropertyValue(mID_N2Comp, (object)value3);
										break;
									}
									throw new Exception("Vapour phase composition mis-macthed");
								case 3:
									if (component.Name == "CarbonDioxide")
									{
										val.PutPropertyValue(mID_CO2Comp, (object)value3);
										break;
									}
									throw new Exception("Vapour phase composition mis-macthed");
								case 4:
									if (component.Name == "HydrogenSulfide")
									{
										val.PutPropertyValue(mID_H2SComp, (object)value3);
										break;
									}
									throw new Exception("Vapour phase composition mis-macthed");
								case 5:
									if (component.Name == "Methane")
									{
										val.PutPropertyValue(mID_C1Comp, (object)value3);
										break;
									}
									throw new Exception("Vapour phase composition mis-macthed");
								case 6:
									if (component.Name == "Ethane")
									{
										val.PutPropertyValue(mID_C2Comp, (object)value3);
										break;
									}
									throw new Exception("Vapour phase composition mis-macthed");
								case 7:
									if (component.Name == "Propane")
									{
										val.PutPropertyValue(mID_C3Comp, (object)value3);
										break;
									}
									throw new Exception("Vapour phase composition mis-macthed");
								case 8:
									if (component.Name == "I-Butane")
									{
										val.PutPropertyValue(mID_IC4Comp, (object)value3);
										break;
									}
									throw new Exception("Vapour phase composition mis-macthed");
								case 9:
									if (component.Name == "n-Butane")
									{
										val.PutPropertyValue(mID_NC4Comp, (object)value3);
										break;
									}
									throw new Exception("Vapour phase composition mis-macthed");
								case 10:
									if (component.Name == "I-Pentane")
									{
										val.PutPropertyValue(mID_IC5Comp, (object)value3);
										break;
									}
									throw new Exception("Vapour phase composition mis-macthed");
								case 11:
									if (component.Name == "n-Pentane")
									{
										val.PutPropertyValue(mID_NC5Comp, (object)value3);
										break;
									}
									throw new Exception("Vapour phase composition mis-macthed");
								case 12:
									if (component.Name == "n-Hexane")
									{
										val.PutPropertyValue(mID_NC6Comp, (object)value3);
										break;
									}
									throw new Exception("Vapour phase composition mis-macthed");
								case 13:
									if (component.Name == "C7+")
									{
										val.PutPropertyValue(mID_C7_plusComp, (object)value3);
										break;
									}
									throw new Exception("Vapour phase composition mis-macthed");
								default:
									throw new Exception("Should never be here");
								}
							}
							val.PutPropertyValue(mID_C7_plusMoleWeight, (object)phaseByType.GetComponent(numberOfCompounds - 1).MolecularWeight.Value);
							double num5 = stream.C7PlusDensity.Value / 998.94;
							val.PutPropertyValue(mID_C7_plusSpecificGravity, (object)num5);
							val.PutPropertyValue(mID_GasPropertySelection, (object)2.0);
						}
						val.PutPropertyValue(mID_MassFlow, (object)stream.GasMassFlowrate.Value);
						break;
					case 1:
						mBasicPhase.PhaseType = (tagPhaseTypeEnum)4;
						val.PutPropertyValue(mID_SpecificGravity, (object)stream.OilGravity.Value);
						val.PutPropertyValue(mID_MassFlow, (object)stream.OilMassFlowrate.Value);
						break;
					case 2:
						mBasicPhase.PhaseType = (tagPhaseTypeEnum)5;
						val.PutPropertyValue(mID_SpecificGravity, (object)stream.WaterGravity.Value);
						val.PutPropertyValue(mID_MassFlow, (object)stream.WaterMassFlowrate.Value);
						break;
					}
				}
				mReady = true;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("setupPhaseCollection failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}
	}
	[Guid("F08F5D8E-096A-4AE2-8903-606DA33D645E")]
	public interface IPPPWithFlarenet
	{
		FlarenetPropertyPkgType PropertyPackage { get; set; }

		FlarenetEnthalpyMethodType EnthalpyMethod { get; set; }

		void Initialize(IFlowStream stream, double[,] kijPR, double[,] kijSRK);
	}
	[Guid("A931099F-24E5-4F03-AFB2-C79B535550E1")]
	[ProgId("AspenTech.Hydraulics.PropertyUtilities.AHPPPWithFlarenet")]
	[ClassInterface(ClassInterfaceType.None)]
	public class AHPPPWithFlarenet : AHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase, IPPPWithFlarenet, IDisposable
	{
		[StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
		private struct FlarenetFlashTolStructure
		{
			public double temp;

			public double enth;

			public double entr;

			public double vapf;
		}

		[StructLayout(LayoutKind.Sequential, Pack = 1)]
		private struct FlarenetCompStructure
		{
			public int id;

			public int type;

			public double molwt;

			public double stdden;

			public double nbp;

			public double watson;

			public double pc;

			public double tc;

			public double vc;

			public double vchar;

			public double ac;

			public double acsrk;

			public double entha;

			public double enthb;

			public double enthc;

			public double enthd;

			public double enthe;

			public double enthf;

			public double s;

			public double viscA;

			public double viscB;

			public bool CopyHydraComponent(IComponent hComp)
			{
				id = hComp.ID;
				type = hComp.ComponentType;
				molwt = hComp.MolecularWeight.Value;
				stdden = hComp.LiquidDensity.Value;
				nbp = hComp.NormalBoilingPoint.Value;
				watson = hComp.WatsonK.Value;
				pc = hComp.CriticalPressure.Value / 1000.0;
				tc = hComp.CriticalTemperature.Value;
				vc = hComp.CriticalVolume.Value;
				vchar = hComp.CostaldVolume.Value;
				ac = hComp.Acentricity.Value;
				acsrk = hComp.CostaldAcentricity.Value;
				entha = hComp.VapourEnthalpyCoeff(0).Value / 1000.0;
				enthb = hComp.VapourEnthalpyCoeff(1).Value / 1000.0;
				enthc = hComp.VapourEnthalpyCoeff(2).Value / 1000.0;
				enthd = hComp.VapourEnthalpyCoeff(3).Value / 1000.0;
				enthe = hComp.VapourEnthalpyCoeff(4).Value / 1000.0;
				enthf = hComp.VapourEnthalpyCoeff(5).Value / 1000.0;
				s = hComp.VapourEnthalpyCoeff(6).Value;
				viscA = hComp.ViscosityThetaA.Value;
				viscB = hComp.ViscosityThetaB.Value;
				return true;
			}
		}

		private FlarenetCompStructure[] mFlarenetComps;

		private FlarenetFlashTolStructure mFlashTol;

		private FlarenetPropertyPkgType mPPkgType;

		private FlarenetEnthalpyMethodType mEnthMethod;

		public FlarenetPropertyPkgType PropertyPackage
		{
			get
			{
				return mPPkgType;
			}
			set
			{
				mPPkgType = value;
			}
		}

		public FlarenetEnthalpyMethodType EnthalpyMethod
		{
			get
			{
				return mEnthMethod;
			}
			set
			{
				mEnthMethod = value;
			}
		}

		[DllImport("FnCalc32.dll")]
		private static extern void OpenProps(int nc1, FlarenetCompStructure[] cd1, double[] kij1, double[] kij2);

		[DllImport("FnCalc32.dll")]
		private static extern void CloseProps();

		[DllImport("FnCalc32.dll")]
		private static extern int Prop3(int prpack, int enpack, ref double t, ref double p, [In] double[] compf, [In][Out] double[] compv, [In][Out] double[] compl, [In][Out] double[] compw, [In][Out] double[] compm, [In][Out] int[] phase, [In][Out] double[] phfrac, [In][Out] double[] hi, [In][Out] double[] si, [In][Out] double[] cp, [In][Out] double[] vi, [In][Out] double[] mw, double guess, ref FlarenetFlashTolStructure ft, int db);

		[DllImport("FnCalc32.dll")]
		private static extern int ThermCondLiquid(double[] compl, double tk, ref double liqthermcond);

		[DllImport("FnCalc32.dll")]
		private static extern int ThermCondVapour(double[] compv, double tk, double density, ref double vapthermcond);

		[DllImport("FnCalc32.dll")]
		private static extern int SurfTenLiquid(double[] compl, double tk, ref double surften);

		[DllImport("FnCalc32.dll")]
		private static extern int ViscVapour(double[] compv, double tk, ref double vapvisc);

		[DllImport("FnCalc32.dll")]
		private static extern int ViscLiquid(double[] compl, double pr, double tk, double density, ref double liqvisc);

		public AHPPPWithFlarenet()
		{
			mPPkgType = FlarenetPropertyPkgType.PengRobinson;
			mEnthMethod = FlarenetEnthalpyMethodType.PengRobinson;
			mFlashTol.temp = 0.0001;
			mFlashTol.enth = 1.0;
			mFlashTol.entr = 0.01;
			mFlashTol.vapf = 1E-06;
		}

		public override double CalculateBubblePoint(IFlowStream Stream, double Pressure)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public override double CalculateDewPoint(IFlowStream Stream, double Pressure)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public override double CalculateProperty(IFlowStream Stream, PropertyType RT, PhaseType PT, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public override double CalculateSurfaceTension(IFlowStream Stream, PhaseType PT, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public override void FlashStream(IFlowStream Stream, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			if (Stream.StreamID != mLastStreamID || mChangedComps == ChangedComponentsActionEnum.ReInitComThermo)
			{
				Check(Stream, Disable: true);
			}
			ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(Stream, FT, data1, data2);
		}

		public override void ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(IFlowStream Stream, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			double num = -32767.0;
			int num2 = -32767;
			int num3 = -32767;
			try
			{
				base.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(Stream, FT, data1, data2);
				if (!mDoFlash)
				{
					return;
				}
				mLastStreamID = Stream.StreamID;
				DoFlash(Stream, FT, data1, data2);
				switch (FT)
				{
				case FlashType.PHFlash:
					num = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
					if (data2 != num)
					{
						if (Math.Abs(data2) > num)
						{
							if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / data2) > 0.001)
							{
								LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PH Flash");
							}
						}
						else if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / num) > 0.001)
						{
							LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PH Flash");
						}
					}
					Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value = data2;
					break;
				case FlashType.PSFlash:
					num = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Entropy.Value;
					if (data2 != num)
					{
						if (Math.Abs(data2) > Math.Abs(num))
						{
							if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / data2) > 0.001)
							{
								LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PS Flash");
							}
						}
						else if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / num) > 0.001)
						{
							LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PS Flash");
						}
					}
					Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Entropy.Value = data2;
					break;
				case FlashType.PVFlash:
					num = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarVapourFraction.Value;
					if (data2 != num)
					{
						if (num == 0.0)
						{
							if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / data2) > 0.001)
							{
								LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PV Flash");
							}
						}
						else if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / num) > 0.001)
						{
							LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PV Flash");
						}
					}
					Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarVapourFraction.Value = data2;
					break;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				if (mLog != null)
				{
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, "Flash Failure");
					string aMessage = "Flash Type = " + FT;
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					aMessage = "Data1 = " + data1.ToString("0.00000");
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					aMessage = "Data2 = " + data2.ToString("0.00000");
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					aMessage = "Calc = " + num.ToString("0.00000");
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					num3 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMoleFractions.Count;
					for (num2 = 0; num2 < num3; num2++)
					{
						aMessage = "Comp = " + (num2 + 1) + " : Mole Fraction = " + Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMoleFractions[num2].Value.ToString("0.000000000");
						LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					}
				}
				throw;
			}
		}

		protected override void Check(IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			try
			{
				if (mFlarenetComps == null)
				{
					throw new Exception("The Flarenet property package has not been set-up with any components.");
				}
				if (Stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall).NumberOfCompounds != mFlarenetComps.Length)
				{
					throw new Exception("The stream has different number of components to the initialised component list. ");
				}
				mReady = true;
			}
			finally
			{
				mCheckDisable = false;
			}
		}

		protected override void Check(IFlowStream Stream, bool Disable)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!mCheckDisable)
				{
					Check(Stream);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				mCheckDisable = false;
				if (Disable)
				{
					mCheckDisable = true;
				}
			}
		}

		internal void DoFlash(IFlowStream Stream, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			int prpack = (int)mPPkgType;
			int enpack = (int)mEnthMethod;
			double t = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			double p = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			int db = 0;
			double fEMPTY = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			IPhase phaseByID = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0);
			int numberOfCompounds = phaseByID.NumberOfCompounds;
			double[] array = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			double[] array2 = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			double[] array3 = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			double[] array4 = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			double[] array5 = new double[numberOfCompounds];
			for (int i = 0; i < numberOfCompounds; i++)
			{
				array[i] = phaseByID.ComponentMoleFractions[i].Value;
			}
			double[] array6 = new double[5];
			double[] array7 = new double[5];
			double[] array8 = new double[5];
			double[] array9 = new double[5];
			double[] array10 = new double[5];
			double[] array11 = new double[5];
			for (int j = 0; j < 5; j++)
			{
				array6[j] = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				array7[j] = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				array8[j] = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				array9[j] = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				array10[j] = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				array11[j] = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			}
			int[] array12 = new int[3];
			switch (FT)
			{
			case FlashType.PHFlash:
				p = data1 / 1000.0;
				array7[3] = data2 * phaseByID.MolecularWeight.Value / 1000.0;
				break;
			case FlashType.PTFlash:
				p = data1 / 1000.0;
				t = data2;
				break;
			case FlashType.PSFlash:
				p = data1 / 1000.0;
				array8[3] = data2 * phaseByID.MolecularWeight.Value / 1000.0;
				break;
			case FlashType.PVFlash:
				p = data1 / 1000.0;
				array6[0] = data2;
				break;
			case FlashType.TVFlash:
				t = data1;
				array6[0] = data2;
				break;
			}
			int num = Prop3(prpack, enpack, ref t, ref p, array, array2, array3, array4, array5, array12, array6, array7, array8, array10, array9, array11, fEMPTY, ref mFlashTol, db);
			switch (FT)
			{
			case FlashType.PHFlash:
				array7[3] = data2 * phaseByID.MolecularWeight.Value / 1000.0;
				break;
			case FlashType.PSFlash:
				array8[3] = data2 * phaseByID.MolecularWeight.Value / 1000.0;
				break;
			case FlashType.PVFlash:
				array6[0] = data2;
				break;
			case FlashType.TVFlash:
				array6[0] = data2;
				break;
			}
			if (num == 0)
			{
				ExtractFlashResults(Stream, t, p, array, array2, array3, array4, array5, array12, array6, array7, array8, array10, array9, array11);
				return;
			}
			throw new FlarenetFlashFailedException(num);
		}

		internal void ExtractFlashResults(IFlowStream stream, double tk, double pkPa, double[] molefrac, double[] vapMF, double[] lgtLiqMF, double[] hvyLiqMF, double[] mixLiqMF, int[] phases, double[] fractions, double[] hh, double[] ss, double[] cc, double[] vv, double[] mw)
		{
			int numberOfCompounds = stream.GetPhaseByID(0).NumberOfCompounds;
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			bool flag3 = false;
			int num = 0;
			if (fractions[0] > 0.0)
			{
				flag = true;
				num++;
			}
			if (fractions[1] > 0.0)
			{
				flag2 = true;
				num++;
			}
			if (fractions[2] > 0.0)
			{
				flag3 = true;
				num++;
			}
			CheckStreamPhases(stream, num, flag, flag2, flag3);
			if (flag)
			{
				IPhase phaseByType = stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
				SetPhaseThermalProperty(phaseByType, tk, 1000.0 * pkPa, 1000.0 * hh[0], 1000.0 * ss[0], mw[0], vv[0], fractions[0], numberOfCompounds, vapMF);
				phaseByType.Cp.Value = cc[0] * 1000.0 / mw[0];
				phaseByType.InternalEnergy.Value = (1000.0 * hh[0] - 1000.0 * pkPa * vv[0]) / mw[0];
				CalculatePhaseTransportProperties(phaseByType, tk, pkPa, vapMF);
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				IPhase phaseByType2 = stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid);
				SetPhaseThermalProperty(phaseByType2, tk, 1000.0 * pkPa, 1000.0 * hh[1], 1000.0 * ss[1], mw[1], vv[1], fractions[1], numberOfCompounds, lgtLiqMF);
				phaseByType2.Cp.Value = cc[1] * 1000.0 / mw[1];
				phaseByType2.InternalEnergy.Value = (1000.0 * hh[1] - 1000.0 * pkPa * vv[1]) / mw[1];
				CalculatePhaseTransportProperties(phaseByType2, tk, pkPa, lgtLiqMF);
			}
			if (flag3)
			{
				IPhase phaseByType3 = stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Aqueous);
				if (!flag2)
				{
					phaseByType3 = stream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid);
				}
				SetPhaseThermalProperty(phaseByType3, tk, 1000.0 * pkPa, 1000.0 * hh[2], 1000.0 * ss[2], mw[2], vv[2], fractions[2], numberOfCompounds, hvyLiqMF);
				phaseByType3.Cp.Value = cc[2] * 1000.0 / mw[2];
				phaseByType3.InternalEnergy.Value = (1000.0 * hh[2] - 1000.0 * pkPa * vv[2]) / mw[2];
				CalculatePhaseTransportProperties(phaseByType3, tk, pkPa, hvyLiqMF);
			}
			IPhase phaseByID = stream.GetPhaseByID(0);
			phaseByID.MolarVapourFraction.Value = fractions[0];
			phaseByID.Cp.Value = cc[3] * 1000.0 / mw[3];
			UpdateOverallPhase(stream, num);
		}

		private void CalculatePhaseTransportProperties(IPhase tPhase, double t, double pkPa, double[] molefrac)
		{
			double liqvisc = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			if (tPhase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour)
			{
				ViscVapour(molefrac, t, ref liqvisc);
				tPhase.Viscosity.Value = liqvisc / 1000.0;
				ThermCondVapour(molefrac, t, tPhase.Density.Value, ref liqvisc);
				tPhase.Conductivity.Value = liqvisc;
				tPhase.SurfaceTension.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			}
			else if (tPhase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid || tPhase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
			{
				ViscLiquid(molefrac, pkPa, t, tPhase.Density.Value, ref liqvisc);
				tPhase.Viscosity.Value = liqvisc / 1000.0;
				ThermCondLiquid(molefrac, t, ref liqvisc);
				tPhase.Conductivity.Value = liqvisc;
				SurfTenLiquid(molefrac, t, ref liqvisc);
				tPhase.SurfaceTension.Value = liqvisc;
			}
		}

		public void Initialize(IFlowStream stream, double[,] kijPR, double[,] kijSRK)
		{
			try
			{
				int numberOfCompounds = stream.GetPhaseByID(0).NumberOfCompounds;
				mFlarenetComps = new FlarenetCompStructure[numberOfCompounds];
				for (int i = 0; i < numberOfCompounds; i++)
				{
					IComponent component = stream.GetPhaseByID(0).GetComponent(i);
					mFlarenetComps[i].CopyHydraComponent(component);
				}
				if (kijPR.GetLength(0) != numberOfCompounds || kijPR.GetLength(1) != numberOfCompounds)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Array length is mismatched", "kijPR");
				}
				if (kijSRK.GetLength(0) != numberOfCompounds || kijSRK.GetLength(1) != numberOfCompounds)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("Array length is mismatched", "kijSRK");
				}
				double[] array = new double[numberOfCompounds * numberOfCompounds];
				double[] array2 = new double[numberOfCompounds * numberOfCompounds];
				for (int j = 0; j < numberOfCompounds; j++)
				{
					for (int k = 0; k < numberOfCompounds; k++)
					{
						array[j * numberOfCompounds + k] = kijPR[j, k];
						array2[j * numberOfCompounds + k] = kijSRK[j, k];
						if (j == k)
						{
							if (kijPR[j, k] == RealValue.FEMPTY)
							{
								array[j * numberOfCompounds + k] = 0.0;
							}
							if (kijSRK[j, k] == RealValue.FEMPTY)
							{
								array2[j * numberOfCompounds + k] = 0.0;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				OpenProps(numberOfCompounds, mFlarenetComps, array, array2);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Flarenet Property Package Initialization failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}
	}
	public class FlarenetFlashFailedException : Exception
	{
		public int Code { get; }

		public FlarenetFlashFailedException()
		{
		}

		public FlarenetFlashFailedException(string message)
			: base(message)
		{
		}

		public FlarenetFlashFailedException(int code, string message = null)
			: base(message)
		{
			Code = code;
		}
	}

		{
			Initialise(existing.m_Name, existing.m_ID, existing.m_MolecularWeight.Value, existing.Alias);
			copyHypotheticalParameters(existing);
		}

		private void Initialise(string aName, int anID, double aMolWt, string alias)
		{
			m_Name = aName;
			m_ID = anID;
			m_MolecularWeight = new RealValue(aMolWt);
			m_Alias = alias;
			InitialiseHypotheticalParameters();
		}

		private void InitialiseHypotheticalParameters()
		{
			try
			{
				m_IsHypothetical = false;
				m_NormalBoilingPoint = new RealValue();
				m_LiquidDensity = new RealValue();
				m_CriticalPressure = new RealValue();
				m_CriticalTemperature = new RealValue();
				m_CriticalVolume = new RealValue();
				m_Acentricity = new RealValue();
				m_CostaldAcentricity = new RealValue();
				m_CostaldVolume = new RealValue();
				m_VapourEnthalpyCoeff = new RealValueCollection();
				m_VapourPressureCoeff = new RealValueCollection();
				for (int i = 0; i <= 9; i++)
				{
					m_VapourEnthalpyCoeff.Add(new RealValue(0.0));
					m_VapourPressureCoeff.Add(new RealValue(0.0));
				}
				m_HeatOfFormation = new RealValue();
				m_ViscosityThetaA = new RealValue();
				m_ViscosityThetaB = new RealValue();
				m_WatsonK = new RealValue();
				m_CompType = 1;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("InitialiseHypotheticalParameters failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void copyHypotheticalParameters(IComponent refComp)
		{
			try
			{
				m_IsHypothetical = refComp.IsHypothetical;
				m_NormalBoilingPoint.Value = refComp.NormalBoilingPoint.Value;
				m_LiquidDensity.Value = refComp.LiquidDensity.Value;
				m_CriticalPressure.Value = refComp.CriticalPressure.Value;
				m_CriticalTemperature.Value = refComp.CriticalTemperature.Value;
				m_CriticalVolume.Value = refComp.CriticalVolume.Value;
				m_Acentricity.Value = refComp.Acentricity.Value;
				m_CostaldAcentricity.Value = refComp.CostaldAcentricity.Value;
				m_CostaldVolume.Value = refComp.CostaldVolume.Value;
				for (int i = 0; i <= 9; i++)
				{
					m_VapourEnthalpyCoeff.Item(i).Value = refComp.VapourEnthalpyCoeff(i).Value;
					m_VapourPressureCoeff.Item(i).Value = refComp.VapourPressureCoeff(i).Value;
				}
				m_HeatOfFormation.Value = refComp.HeatOfFormation.Value;
				m_ViscosityThetaA.Value = refComp.ViscosityThetaA.Value;
				m_ViscosityThetaB.Value = refComp.ViscosityThetaB.Value;
				m_WatsonK.Value = refComp.WatsonK.Value;
				m_CompType = refComp.ComponentType;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("copyHypotheticalParameters failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public IRealValue VapourEnthalpyCoeff(int index)
		{
			return m_VapourEnthalpyCoeff.Item(index);
		}

		public IRealValue VapourPressureCoeff(int index)
		{
			return m_VapourPressureCoeff.Item(index);
		}

		public void UpdateByValue(IComponent refComp)
		{
			try
			{
				m_ID = refComp.ID;
				m_Name = refComp.Name;
				m_MolecularWeight.Value = refComp.MolecularWeight.Value;
				m_Alias = refComp.Alias;
				copyHypotheticalParameters(refComp);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("UpdateByValue failed.", innerException);
			}
		}
	}
	internal class ConvertXY2Item
	{
		private int[,] lclTable;

		internal int this[long xIndex, long yIndex]
		{
			get
			{
				if (xIndex < 0 || xIndex >= XLength())
				{
					return -1;
				}
				if (yIndex < 0 || yIndex >= YLength())
				{
					return -1;
				}
				return lclTable[(int)checked((nint)xIndex), (int)checked((nint)yIndex)] - 1;
			}
			set
			{
				if (xIndex >= 0 && xIndex < XLength() && yIndex >= 0 && yIndex < YLength())
				{
					lclTable[(int)checked((nint)xIndex), (int)checked((nint)yIndex)] = value + 1;
				}
			}
		}

		internal ConvertXY2Item(int xSize, int ySize)
		{
			lclTable = new int[xSize, ySize];
		}

		internal void Clear()
		{
			long num = XLength();
			long num2 = YLength();
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < num2; j++)
				{
					lclTable[i, j] = 0;
				}
			}
		}

		internal int GetValue(long xIndex, long yIndex)
		{
			if (xIndex < 0 || xIndex >= XLength())
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (yIndex < 0 || yIndex >= YLength())
			{
				return -1;
			}
			return lclTable[(int)checked((nint)xIndex), (int)checked((nint)yIndex)] - 1;
		}

		internal void InsertX(long index)
		{
			if (index == -1)
			{
				return;
			}
			long num = XLength();
			long num2 = YLength();
			int[,] array = new int[(int)checked((nint)num), (int)checked((nint)num2)];
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < num2; j++)
				{
					if (j < index)
					{
						array[i, j] = lclTable[i, j];
					}
					if (j >= index)
					{
						array[i, j] = lclTable[i, j];
					}
				}
			}
			lclTable = new int[(int)checked((nint)unchecked(num + 1)), (int)checked((nint)num2)];
			for (int k = 0; k < num; k++)
			{
				for (int l = 0; l < num2; l++)
				{
					if (k < index)
					{
						lclTable[k, l] = array[k, l];
					}
					if (k >= index)
					{
						lclTable[k + 1, l] = array[k, l];
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal void InsertY(long index)
		{
			if (index == -1)
			{
				return;
			}
			long num = XLength();
			long num2 = YLength();
			int[,] array = new int[(int)checked((nint)num), (int)checked((nint)num2)];
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < num2; j++)
				{
					if (j < index)
					{
						array[i, j] = lclTable[i, j];
					}
					if (j >= index)
					{
						array[i, j] = lclTable[i, j];
					}
				}
			}
			lclTable = new int[(int)checked((nint)num), (int)checked((nint)unchecked(num2 + 1))];
			for (int k = 0; k < num; k++)
			{
				for (int l = 0; l < num2; l++)
				{
					if (l < index)
					{
						lclTable[k, l] = array[k, l];
					}
					if (l >= index)
					{
						lclTable[k, l + 1] = array[k, l];
					}
				}
			}
		}

		internal void SetValue(long xIndex, long yIndex, int value)
		{
			if (xIndex >= 0 && xIndex < XLength() && yIndex >= 0 && yIndex < YLength())
			{
				lclTable[(int)checked((nint)xIndex), (int)checked((nint)yIndex)] = value + 1;
			}
		}

		internal long XLength()
		{
			return lclTable.GetUpperBound(0) + 1;
		}

		internal long YLength()
		{
			return lclTable.GetUpperBound(1) + 1;
		}
	}
	[Guid("9CCED0CD-7C43-4896-8B1D-B0FC0FCDAC31")]
	[ProgId("Utility.FlowStream")]
	[ClassInterface(ClassInterfaceType.None)]
	public class FlowStream : IFlowStream, ICloneable, IDisposable, IBlackOilStream
	{
		protected IMessageLog log;

		protected int nPhases;

		protected int ID;

		protected ArrayList Phases;

		protected IRealValue massFlowrate;

		protected string flowRegime;

		protected string WaterflowRegime;

		protected bool mDisposed;

		protected IPhase mCombinedLiqPhase;

		private static Random rnd = new Random();

		private static HashSet<int> existingIDs = new HashSet<int>();

		private IRealValue oilMassFlowrate;

		private IRealValue oilGravity;

		private IRealValue oilViscosityParamA;

		private IRealValue oilViscosityParamB;

		private IRealValue oilWatsonK;

		private IRealValue oilBubblePtCalPressure;

		private IRealValue oilBubblePtCalTemperature;

		private IRealValue oilBubblePtCalSolnGor;

		private IRealValue oilBubblePtCalFvf;

		private IRealValue oilBubblePtCalLiveViscosity;

		private IRealValue gasMassFlowrate;

		private IRealValue gasGravity;

		private IRealValue gasPc;

		private IRealValue gasTc;

		private IRealValue waterMassFlowrate;

		private IRealValue waterGravity;

		private IRealValue userSpecifiedSurfaceTension;

		private int nPoints;

		private RealValueCollection visPoints;

		private RealValueCollection temPoints;

		private string visCorrelation;

		private bool isBlackOil;

		private bool isUsingLimitedComposition;

		private IRealValue c7PlusDensity;

		private IRealValue waterVF;

		private IRealValue gasSV;

		private IRealValue waterSV;

		public IRealValue MassFlowrate
		{
			get
			{
				return massFlowrate;
			}
			set
			{
				massFlowrate = value;
			}
		}

		public IPhase CombinedLiquidPhase
		{
			get
			{
				IPhase phase = mCombinedLiqPhase;
				if (phase == null)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < NumberOfPhases; i++)
					{
						IPhase phaseByID = GetPhaseByID(i + 1);
						if (phaseByID.PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid || phaseByID.PhaseType == PhaseType.Aqueous)
						{
							phase = phaseByID;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				return phase;
			}
			set
			{
				mCombinedLiqPhase = value;
			}
		}

		public int NumberOfPhases
		{
			get
			{
				return nPhases;
			}
			set
			{
				int num = -1;
				int num2 = -1;
				try
				{
					nPhases = value;
					num = Phases.Count;
					if (num == nPhases + 1)
					{
						return;
					}
					if (num > nPhases + 1)
					{
						for (num2 = num - 1; num2 > nPhases; num2--)
						{
							Phases.Remove(Phases[num2]);
						}
					}
					else
					{
						for (num2 = num - 1; num2 < nPhases; num2++)
						{
							Phases.Add(GetNewPhase(0));
						}
					}
				}
				catch (Exception innerException)
				{
					throw new Exception("Failed To Set Number Of Phases", innerException);
				}
			}
		}

		public string FlowRegime
		{
			get
			{
				return flowRegime;
			}
			set
			{
				flowRegime = value;
			}
		}

		public string WaterFlowRegime
		{
			get
			{
				return WaterflowRegime;
			}
			set
			{
				WaterflowRegime = value;
			}
		}

		public int StreamID
		{
			get
			{
				return ID;
			}
			set
			{
				ID = value;
			}
		}

		public IRealValue OilMassFlowrate
		{
			get
			{
				return oilMassFlowrate;
			}
			set
			{
				oilMassFlowrate = value;
				if (value.Value != 0.0 && value.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY)
				{
					isBlackOil = true;
				}
			}
		}

		public IRealValue OilGravity
		{
			get
			{
				return oilGravity;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY && value.Value < 0.0)
				{
					throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("value", "The oil gravity value should not be less than zero.");
				}
				oilGravity = value;
			}
		}

			}
			set
			{
				oilViscosityParamA = value;

		}

			}
			set
			{

			}

				return oilBubblePtCalLiveViscosity;
			}
			set
			{
				oilBubblePtCalLiveViscosity = value;
			}
		}

		public IRealValue GasMassFlowrate
		{
			get
			{
				return gasMassFlowrate;
			}
			set
			{
				gasMassFlowrate = value;
				if (value.Value != 0.0 && value.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY)
				{
					isBlackOil = true;
				}
			}
		}

					throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("value", "The gas gravity value should not be less than zero.");
				}
				gasGravity = value;
			}
		}

		public int NumberOfViscosityPoints
		{
			get
			{
				return nPoints;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != -32767 && value < 0)
				{
					throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("value", "The number of viscosity points should not be less than zero.");
				}
				nPoints = value;
				visPoints = new RealValueCollection();
				temPoints = new RealValueCollection();
				for (int i = 0; i < value; i++)
				{
					visPoints.Add(new RealValue(0.0));
					temPoints.Add(new RealValue(0.0));
				}
			}
		}

				if (oilMassFlowrate.Value > 0.0 || waterMassFlowrate.Value > 0.0 || gasMassFlowrate.Value > 0.0)
				{
					isBlackOil = true;
				}
				return isBlackOil;
			}
		}
