		protected static int GetNewStreamID()
		{
			int num;
			do
			{
				num = rnd.Next();
			}
			while (!existingIDs.Add(num));
			return num;
		}

		public FlowStream()
		{
			FlowStreamInit(1);
		}

		public FlowStream(Phase[] refPhases)
		{
			int length = refPhases.GetLength(0);
			FlowStreamInit(length);
			for (int i = 0; i <= nPhases; i++)
			{
				Phases[i] = refPhases[i];
			}
		}

		public FlowStream(int nphases)
		{
			FlowStreamInit(nphases);
		}

		~FlowStream()
		{
			Dispose(Disposing: false);
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(Disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool Disposing)
		{
			if (!mDisposed && Disposing)
			{
				int num = -1;
				int count = Phases.Count;
				for (num = 0; num < count; num++)
				{
					(Phases[num] as IDisposable).Dispose();
				}
			}
			mDisposed = true;
		}

		public void MixingStreams(StreamCollection streams, double[] massfrac)
		{
			mixingBOStreams(streams, massfrac);
		}

		public void MergeStreamCompositions(IFlowStream s1, int FlowDir1, IFlowStream s2, int FlowDir2)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			double[] array = null;
			int num = -32767;
			int[] array2 = null;
			int[] array3 = null;
			IComponent component = null;
			IComponent component2 = null;
			IComponent component3 = null;
			bool flag3 = false;
			int num2 = -32767;
			int num3 = -32767;
			int num4 = -32767;
			int num5 = -32767;
			int num6 = -32767;
			int num7 = -32767;
			IPhase phase = null;
			IPhase phase2 = null;
			IPhase phase3 = null;
			double num8 = -32767.0;
			double num9 = -32767.0;
			double num10 = -32767.0;
			double num11 = -32767.0;
			try
			{
				if (s1 == null || s2 == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException();
				}
				if (Math.Abs(FlowDir1) != 1 || Math.Abs(FlowDir2) != 1)
				{
					throw new Exception("Bad Flow Direction");
				}
				phase = s1.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall);
				phase2 = s2.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall);
				num8 = s1.MassFlowrate.Value;
				num9 = s2.MassFlowrate.Value;
				num10 = num8 * (double)FlowDir1 + num9 * (double)FlowDir2;
				if (s1.StreamID == s2.StreamID)
				{
					if (s1.StreamID == StreamID && !(s1 as IBlackOilStream).IsBlackOil)
					{
						massFlowrate.Value = num10;
						return;
					}
					UpdateStreamByValue(s1);
					massFlowrate.Value = num10;
					if (!(s1 as IBlackOilStream).IsBlackOil)
					{
						return;
					}
					IBlackOilStream blackOilStream;
					double num12;
					if (num8 * (double)FlowDir1 > 0.0)
					{
						blackOilStream = s1 as IBlackOilStream;
						num12 = Math.Abs(num10 / num8);
					}
					else
					{
						if (!(num9 * (double)FlowDir2 > 0.0))
						{
							throw new Exception("No mass flowrate.");
						}
						blackOilStream = s2 as IBlackOilStream;
						num12 = Math.Abs(num10 / num9);
					}
					oilMassFlowrate.Value = blackOilStream.OilMassFlowrate.Value * num12;
					gasMassFlowrate.Value = blackOilStream.GasMassFlowrate.Value * num12;
					waterMassFlowrate.Value = blackOilStream.WaterMassFlowrate.Value * num12;
					return;
				}
				phase3 = GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall);
				if (phase3 == null)
				{
					FlowStreamInit(1);
					phase3 = GetPhaseByID(0);
					phase3.PhaseType = PhaseType.Overall;
				}
				num = 0;
				num7 = phase3.NumberOfCompounds;
				num5 = phase.NumberOfCompounds;
				array2 = new int[num5];
				for (num2 = 0; num2 < num5; num2++)
				{
					component = phase.GetComponent(num2);
					flag3 = false;
					for (num4 = 0; num4 < num7; num4++)
					{
						component3 = phase3.GetComponent(num4);
						if (component.ID == component3.ID)
						{
							flag3 = true;
							array2[num2] = num4;
							if (num2 != num4 && num == 0)
							{
								num = 2;
							}
							break;
						}
					}
					if (!flag3)
					{
						phase3.AddComponent(component);
						array2[num2] = phase3.NumberOfCompounds - 1;
						num = 1;
					}
				}
				num7 = phase3.NumberOfCompounds;
				num6 = phase2.NumberOfCompounds;
				array3 = new int[num6];
				for (num3 = 0; num3 < num6; num3++)
				{
					component2 = phase2.GetComponent(num3);
					flag3 = false;
					for (num4 = 0; num4 < num7; num4++)
					{
						component3 = phase3.GetComponent(num4);
						if (component2.ID == component3.ID)
						{
							flag3 = true;
							array3[num3] = num4;
							if (num3 != num4 && num == 0)
							{
								num = 2;
							}
							break;
						}
					}
					if (!flag3)
					{
						phase3.AddComponent(component2);
						array3[num3] = phase3.NumberOfCompounds - 1;
						num = 1;
					}
				}
				num7 = phase3.NumberOfCompounds;
				array = new double[num7];
				for (num2 = 0; num2 < num5; num2++)
				{
					array[array2[num2]] = num8 * (double)FlowDir1 * phase.ComponentMassFractions[num2].Value;
				}
				for (num3 = 0; num3 < num6; num3++)
				{
					array[array3[num3]] += num9 * (double)FlowDir2 * phase2.ComponentMassFractions[num3].Value;
				}
				flag = true;
				for (num4 = 0; num4 < num7; num4++)
				{
					if (array[num4] != 0.0)
					{
						flag = false;
						break;
					}
				}
				flag2 = false;
				if (num7 > 0)
				{
					for (num4 = 1; num4 < num7; num4++)
					{
						if (array[num4] * array[num4 - 1] < 0.0)
						{
							flag2 = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (!flag && !flag2)
				{
					for (num4 = 0; num4 < num7; num4++)
					{
						num11 = array[num4] / num10;
						phase3.ComponentMassFractions[num4].Value = num11;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					for (num4 = 0; num4 < num7; num4++)
					{
						phase3.ComponentMassFractions[num4].Value = 0.0;
					}
					for (num2 = 0; num2 < num5; num2++)
					{
						phase3.ComponentMassFractions[array2[num2]].Value = phase.ComponentMassFractions[num2].Value;
					}
				}
				MassFlowrate.Value = num10;
				if (num != 0)
				{
					StreamID = GetNewStreamID();
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					throw new Exception("Bad Merged Stream Composition");
				}
				if ((s1 as IBlackOilStream).IsBlackOil || (s2 as IBlackOilStream).IsBlackOil)
				{
					MergeBOParameters(s1 as IBlackOilStream, FlowDir1, s2 as IBlackOilStream, FlowDir2);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("MergeStreamCompositions Failure", innerException);
			}
		}

		public void SplitStream(IFlowStream s1, IFlowStream s2)
		{
			if (StreamID != s1.StreamID || StreamID != s2.StreamID)
			{
				IPhase phaseByType = GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall);
				s1.SetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall, (IPhase)phaseByType.Clone());
				s2.SetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall, (IPhase)phaseByType.Clone());
				int streamID = (s2.StreamID = StreamID);
				s1.StreamID = streamID;
			}
		}

		public void SplitStreamByPhase(IFlowStream s, IFlowStream liquidStream, IFlowStream vapourStream)
		{
			IPhase phase = null;
			IPhase phase2 = null;
			try
			{
				if (liquidStream == null)
				{
					liquidStream = new FlowStream(1);
				}
				else
				{
					liquidStream.NumberOfPhases = 1;
				}
				if (vapourStream == null)
				{
					vapourStream = new FlowStream(1);
				}
				else
				{
					vapourStream.NumberOfPhases = 1;
				}
				if (s.NumberOfPhases > 1)
				{
					phase = s.CombinedLiquidPhase;
					IPhase phaseByID = liquidStream.GetPhaseByID(0);
					liquidStream.SetPhaseByID(1, phase);
					phaseByID.CopyStateParameters(phase);
					phaseByID.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
					mCombinedLiqPhase.CopyStateParameters(s.CombinedLiquidPhase);
					phase2 = s.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
					IPhase phaseByID2 = vapourStream.GetPhaseByID(0);
					vapourStream.SetPhaseByID(1, phase2);
					phaseByID2.CopyStateParameters(phase2);
					phaseByID.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
					liquidStream.MassFlowrate.Value = s.MassFlowrate.Value * phase.MassFraction.Value;
					vapourStream.MassFlowrate.Value = s.MassFlowrate.Value * phase2.MassFraction.Value;
					return;
				}
				throw new Exception("The stream only has one phase. ");
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("SplitStreamByPhase failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			FlowStreamInit(1);
		}

		public void Erase()
		{
			massFlowrate.Erase();
			for (int i = 0; i < nPhases; i++)
			{
				GetPhaseByID(i).Erase();
			}
		}

		public virtual void AvarageFlowStream(IFlowStream flowStream1, IFlowStream flowStream2, double localDistance, double totalDistance, bool overrideExistingData)
		{
			try
			{
				IPhase phase = null;
				IPhase phase2 = null;
				int num = 0;
				int i = 0;
				for (num = flowStream1.NumberOfPhases + 1; i < num; i++)
				{
					phase = flowStream1.GetPhaseByID(i);
					phase2 = flowStream2.GetPhaseByID(i);
					GetPhaseByID(i).AvaragePhase(phase, phase2, localDistance, totalDistance, overrideExistingData);
				}
				mCombinedLiqPhase.AvaragePhase(flowStream1.CombinedLiquidPhase, flowStream2.CombinedLiquidPhase, localDistance, totalDistance, overrideExistingData);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("AvarageFlowStream failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		public virtual IPhase GetNewPhase(int nCompounds)
		{
			if (nCompounds == 0)
			{
				return new Phase();
			}
			return new Phase(nCompounds);
		}

		public IPhase GetPhaseByID(int nPhase)
		{
			try
			{
				return Phases[nPhase] as IPhase;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				if (log != null)
				{
					log.Add(MessageLogType.Warning, "Phase " + ex.Message);
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public IPhase GetPhaseByType(PhaseType MyTagPhaseTypeEnumerator)
		{
			int i = 0;
			if (MyTagPhaseTypeEnumerator == PhaseType.UnknownPhase)
			{
				if (log != null)
				{
					log.Add(MessageLogType.Warning, "User Loading phase tagged as Unknown");
				}
				return null;
			}
			for (; i <= nPhases; i++)
			{
				IPhase phase = Phases[i] as IPhase;
				if (phase.PhaseType == MyTagPhaseTypeEnumerator)
				{
					return phase;
				}
			}
			if (log != null)
			{
				log.Add(MessageLogType.Warning, "User trying to access non present phase");
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void SetMessageLog(ref IMessageLog MymessageLog)
		{
			log = MymessageLog;
		}

		public virtual object Clone()
		{
			if (isBlackOil)
			{
				return CloneBO();
			}
			IFlowStream flowStream = new FlowStream(nPhases);
			flowStream.StreamID = ID;
			flowStream.MassFlowrate = new RealValue(massFlowrate);
			flowStream.NumberOfPhases = nPhases;
			for (int i = 0; i <= nPhases; i++)
			{
				if (Phases[i] != null)
				{
					IPhase phase = Phases[i] as IPhase;
					flowStream.SetPhaseByID(i, (IPhase)phase.Clone());
				}
			}
			flowStream.FlowRegime = flowRegime;
			flowStream.CombinedLiquidPhase = mCombinedLiqPhase;
			return flowStream;
		}

		public virtual void UpdateStreamByValue(IFlowStream refStream)
		{
			int num = -32767;
			try
			{
				ID = refStream.StreamID;
				massFlowrate.AssignFrom(refStream.MassFlowrate);
				NumberOfPhases = refStream.NumberOfPhases;
				for (num = 0; num <= NumberOfPhases; num++)
				{
					((IPhase)Phases[num]).UpdatePhaseByValue(refStream.GetPhaseByID(num));
				}
				flowRegime = refStream.FlowRegime;
				mCombinedLiqPhase = refStream.GetCmbLiqPhPtr();
				if ((refStream as IBlackOilStream).IsBlackOil)
				{
					UpdateBOStreamByValue(refStream as IBlackOilStream);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("UpdateStreamByValue Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		public IPhase GetCmbLiqPhPtr()
		{
			return mCombinedLiqPhase;
		}

		public void SetPhaseByID(int nPhase, IPhase refPhase)
		{
			Phases[nPhase] = refPhase;
		}

		public void SetPhaseByType(PhaseType phaseType, IPhase refPhase)
		{
			if (phaseType != refPhase.PhaseType)
			{
				throw new Exception("Your reference phase does not match the required phase type.");
			}
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < Phases.Count; i++)
			{
				if ((Phases[i] as IPhase).PhaseType == phaseType)
				{
					Phases[i] = refPhase;
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				throw new Exception("Your stream does not contain required phase.");
			}
		}

		public void SetPhaseValueByID(int nPhase, IPhase refPhase)
		{
			((IPhase)Phases[nPhase]).UpdatePhaseByValue(refPhase);
		}

		public void SetPhaseValueByType(PhaseType phaseType, IPhase refPhase)
		{
			if (phaseType != refPhase.PhaseType)
			{
				throw new Exception("Your reference phase does not match the required phase type.");
			}
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < Phases.Count; i++)
			{
				IPhase phase = Phases[i] as IPhase;
				if (phase.PhaseType == phaseType)
				{
					phase.UpdatePhaseByValue(refPhase);
					flag = true;
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				throw new Exception("Your stream does not contain required phase.");
			}
		}

		protected void FlowStreamInit(int NoOfPhases)
		{
			if (NoOfPhases > 4)
			{
				throw new Exception("Cannot Have More Than 4 Equilibrium Phases");
			}
			log = null;
			nPhases = NoOfPhases;
			ID = GetNewStreamID();
			Phases = new ArrayList(5);
			for (int i = 0; i <= nPhases; i++)
			{
				Phases.Add(GetNewPhase(0));
			}
			(Phases[0] as IPhase).PhaseType = PhaseType.Overall;
			massFlowrate = new RealValue();
			flowRegime = "";
			InitialiseBO(nPhases);
		}

		public IRealValue ViscosityPointValue(int index)
		{
			if (index >= nPoints)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("index", index.ToString(), " is out of range " + nPoints + " - 1");
			}
			return visPoints.Item(index);
		}

		public IRealValue TemperatureAtViscosityPoint(int index)
		{
			if (index >= nPoints)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("index", index.ToString(), " is out of range " + nPoints + " - 1");
			}
			return temPoints.Item(index);
		}

		public void CopyStreamComposition(IFlowStream refStream)
		{
			IPhase phase = null;
			IPhase phase2 = null;
			try
			{
				phase = Phases[0] as IPhase;
				phase2 = refStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall);
				IComponent component = null;
				if (phase.NumberOfCompounds == phase2.NumberOfCompounds)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < phase2.NumberOfCompounds; i++)
					{
						component = phase2.GetComponent(i);
						phase.SetComponentValues(i, component.ID, component.Name, component.MolecularWeight.Value);
						phase.ComponentMoleFractions[i].Value = phase2.ComponentMoleFractions[i].Value;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					phase.RemoveAllComponents();
					for (int j = 0; j < phase2.NumberOfCompounds; j++)
					{
						component = phase2.GetComponent(j);
						phase.AddNewComponent(component.ID, component.Name, component.MolecularWeight.Value);
						phase.ComponentMoleFractions[j].Value = phase2.ComponentMoleFractions[j].Value;
					}
				}
				phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Overall;
				if ((refStream as IBlackOilStream).IsBlackOil)
				{
					UpdateBOStreamByValue(refStream as IBlackOilStream);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("CopyStreamComposition failed.", innerException);
			}
		}

		public void CopyBlackOilParameters(IBlackOilStream refStream)
		{
			double num = 1.0;
			if (massFlowrate.Value == RealValue.FEMPTY)
			{
				num = 1.0;
				massFlowrate.Value = refStream.MassFlowrate.Value;
			}
			else if (refStream.MassFlowrate.Value != 0.0)
			{
				num = Math.Abs(massFlowrate.Value / refStream.MassFlowrate.Value);
			}
			oilMassFlowrate.Value = refStream.OilMassFlowrate.Value * num;
			oilGravity.Value = refStream.OilGravity.Value;
			oilViscosityParamA.Value = refStream.OilViscosityParamA.Value;
			oilViscosityParamB.Value = refStream.OilViscosityParamB.Value;
			oilWatsonK.Value = refStream.OilWatsonK.Value;
			oilBubblePtCalPressure.Value = refStream.OilBubblePtCalPressure.Value;
			oilBubblePtCalTemperature.Value = refStream.OilBubblePtCalTemperature.Value;
			oilBubblePtCalSolnGor.Value = refStream.OilBubblePtCalSolnGor.Value;
			oilBubblePtCalFvf.Value = refStream.OilBubblePtCalFvf.Value;
			oilBubblePtCalLiveViscosity.Value = refStream.OilBubblePtCalLiveViscosity.Value;
			gasMassFlowrate.Value = refStream.GasMassFlowrate.Value * num;
			gasGravity.Value = refStream.GasGravity.Value;
			gasPc.Value = refStream.GasPc.Value;
			gasTc.Value = refStream.GasTc.Value;
			waterMassFlowrate.Value = refStream.WaterMassFlowrate.Value * num;
			waterGravity.Value = refStream.WaterGravity.Value;
			userSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value = refStream.UserSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value;
			visCorrelation = refStream.DeadOilViscosityCorrelation;
			isBlackOil = refStream.IsBlackOil;
			isUsingLimitedComposition = refStream.IsUsingLimitedComposition;
			producedGOR.Value = refStream.ProducedGOR.Value;
			c7PlusDensity.Value = refStream.C7PlusDensity.Value;
			visEqName = refStream.OilViscosityEquationName;
			volumeFlowrate.Value = refStream.VolumeFlowrate.Value * num;
			oilVF.Value = refStream.OilVolumeFraction.Value;
			gasVF.Value = refStream.GasVolumeFraction.Value;
			waterVF.Value = refStream.WaterVolumeFraction.Value;
			oilSV.Value = refStream.OilSpecificVolume.Value;
			gasSV.Value = refStream.GasSpecificVolume.Value;
			waterSV.Value = refStream.WaterSpecificVolume.Value;
			if (!refStream.IsUsingLimitedComposition)
			{
				return;
			}
			IPhase phase = null;
			IPhase phase2 = null;
			phase = Phases[0] as IPhase;
			phase2 = refStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall);
			IComponent component = null;
			if (phase.NumberOfCompounds == phase2.NumberOfCompounds)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < phase2.NumberOfCompounds; i++)
				{
					component = phase2.GetComponent(i);
					phase.SetComponentValues(i, component.ID, component.Name, component.MolecularWeight.Value);
					phase.ComponentMoleFractions[i].Value = phase2.ComponentMoleFractions[i].Value;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				phase.RemoveAllComponents();
				for (int j = 0; j < phase2.NumberOfCompounds; j++)
				{
					component = phase2.GetComponent(j);
					phase.AddNewComponent(component.ID, component.Name, component.MolecularWeight.Value);
					phase.ComponentMoleFractions[j].Value = phase2.ComponentMoleFractions[j].Value;
				}
			}
			phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Overall;
		}

		private object CloneBO()
		{
			IBlackOilStream blackOilStream = new FlowStream(nPhases);
			blackOilStream.StreamID = ID;
			blackOilStream.MassFlowrate = new RealValue(massFlowrate);
			blackOilStream.NumberOfPhases = nPhases;
			for (int i = 0; i <= nPhases; i++)
			{
				if (Phases[i] != null)
				{
					IPhase phase = Phases[i] as IPhase;
					blackOilStream.SetPhaseByID(i, (IPhase)phase.Clone());
				}
			}
			blackOilStream.FlowRegime = flowRegime;
			blackOilStream.CombinedLiquidPhase = mCombinedLiqPhase;
			blackOilStream.OilMassFlowrate.Value = oilMassFlowrate.Value;
			blackOilStream.OilGravity.Value = oilGravity.Value;
			blackOilStream.OilViscosityParamA.Value = oilViscosityParamA.Value;
			blackOilStream.OilViscosityParamB.Value = oilViscosityParamB.Value;
			blackOilStream.OilWatsonK.Value = oilWatsonK.Value;
			blackOilStream.OilBubblePtCalPressure.Value = oilBubblePtCalPressure.Value;
			blackOilStream.OilBubblePtCalTemperature.Value = oilBubblePtCalTemperature.Value;
			blackOilStream.OilBubblePtCalSolnGor.Value = oilBubblePtCalSolnGor.Value;
			blackOilStream.OilBubblePtCalFvf.Value = oilBubblePtCalFvf.Value;
			blackOilStream.OilBubblePtCalLiveViscosity.Value = oilBubblePtCalLiveViscosity.Value;
			blackOilStream.GasMassFlowrate.Value = gasMassFlowrate.Value;
			blackOilStream.GasGravity.Value = gasGravity.Value;
			blackOilStream.GasPc.Value = gasPc.Value;
			blackOilStream.GasTc.Value = gasTc.Value;
			blackOilStream.WaterMassFlowrate.Value = waterMassFlowrate.Value;
			blackOilStream.WaterGravity.Value = waterGravity.Value;
			blackOilStream.UserSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value = userSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value;
			blackOilStream.NumberOfViscosityPoints = nPoints;
			for (int j = 0; j < nPoints; j++)
			{
				blackOilStream.ViscosityPointValue(j).Value = visPoints.Item(j).Value;
				blackOilStream.TemperatureAtViscosityPoint(j).Value = temPoints.Item(j).Value;
			}
			blackOilStream.DeadOilViscosityCorrelation = visCorrelation;
			blackOilStream.IsUsingLimitedComposition = isUsingLimitedComposition;
			blackOilStream.ProducedGOR.Value = producedGOR.Value;
			blackOilStream.C7PlusDensity.Value = c7PlusDensity.Value;
			blackOilStream.VolumeFlowrate.Value = volumeFlowrate.Value;
			blackOilStream.OilVolumeFraction.Value = oilVF.Value;
			blackOilStream.GasVolumeFraction.Value = gasVF.Value;
			blackOilStream.WaterVolumeFraction.Value = waterVF.Value;
			blackOilStream.OilSpecificVolume.Value = oilSV.Value;
			blackOilStream.GasSpecificVolume.Value = gasSV.Value;
			blackOilStream.WaterSpecificVolume.Value = waterSV.Value;
			return blackOilStream;
		}

		private void InitialiseBO(int NoOfPhases)
		{
			oilMassFlowrate = new RealValue();
			oilGravity = new RealValue();
			oilViscosityParamA = new RealValue();
			oilViscosityParamB = new RealValue();
			oilWatsonK = new RealValue();
			oilBubblePtCalPressure = new RealValue();
			oilBubblePtCalTemperature = new RealValue();
			oilBubblePtCalSolnGor = new RealValue();
			oilBubblePtCalFvf = new RealValue();
			oilBubblePtCalLiveViscosity = new RealValue();
			gasMassFlowrate = new RealValue();
			gasGravity = new RealValue();
			gasPc = new RealValue();
			gasTc = new RealValue();
			waterMassFlowrate = new RealValue();
			waterGravity = new RealValue();
			userSpecifiedSurfaceTension = new RealValue();
			visCorrelation = null;
			isBlackOil = false;
			isUsingLimitedComposition = false;
			producedGOR = new RealValue();
			c7PlusDensity = new RealValue();
			visEqName = null;
			volumeFlowrate = new RealValue();
			oilVF = new RealValue();
			gasVF = new RealValue();
			waterVF = new RealValue();
			oilSV = new RealValue();
			gasSV = new RealValue();
			waterSV = new RealValue();
		}

		private void UpdateBOStreamByValue(IBlackOilStream refStream)
		{
			try
			{
				oilMassFlowrate.Value = refStream.OilMassFlowrate.Value;
				oilGravity.Value = refStream.OilGravity.Value;
				oilViscosityParamA.Value = refStream.OilViscosityParamA.Value;
				oilViscosityParamB.Value = refStream.OilViscosityParamB.Value;
				oilWatsonK.Value = refStream.OilWatsonK.Value;
				oilBubblePtCalPressure.Value = refStream.OilBubblePtCalPressure.Value;
				oilBubblePtCalTemperature.Value = refStream.OilBubblePtCalTemperature.Value;
				oilBubblePtCalSolnGor.Value = refStream.OilBubblePtCalSolnGor.Value;
				oilBubblePtCalFvf.Value = refStream.OilBubblePtCalFvf.Value;
				oilBubblePtCalLiveViscosity.Value = refStream.OilBubblePtCalLiveViscosity.Value;
				gasMassFlowrate.Value = refStream.GasMassFlowrate.Value;
				gasGravity.Value = refStream.GasGravity.Value;
				gasPc.Value = refStream.GasPc.Value;
				gasTc.Value = refStream.GasTc.Value;
				waterMassFlowrate.Value = refStream.WaterMassFlowrate.Value;
				waterGravity.Value = refStream.WaterGravity.Value;
				userSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value = refStream.UserSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value;
				NumberOfViscosityPoints = refStream.NumberOfViscosityPoints;
				for (int i = 0; i < nPoints; i++)
				{
					visPoints.Item(i).Value = refStream.ViscosityPointValue(i).Value;
					temPoints.Item(i).Value = refStream.TemperatureAtViscosityPoint(i).Value;
				}
				visCorrelation = refStream.DeadOilViscosityCorrelation;
				isUsingLimitedComposition = refStream.IsUsingLimitedComposition;
				producedGOR.Value = refStream.ProducedGOR.Value;
				c7PlusDensity.Value = refStream.C7PlusDensity.Value;
				isBlackOil = true;
				volumeFlowrate.Value = refStream.VolumeFlowrate.Value;
				oilVF.Value = refStream.OilVolumeFraction.Value;
				gasVF.Value = refStream.GasVolumeFraction.Value;
				waterVF.Value = refStream.WaterVolumeFraction.Value;
				oilSV.Value = refStream.OilSpecificVolume.Value;
				gasSV.Value = refStream.GasSpecificVolume.Value;
				waterSV.Value = refStream.WaterSpecificVolume.Value;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Update Black Oil Stream by Value failed.", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void MergeBOParameters(IBlackOilStream s1, int flowDir1, IBlackOilStream s2, int flowDir2)
		{
			double num = -32767.0;
			double num2 = -32767.0;
			double num3 = -32767.0;
			try
			{
				num = s1.MassFlowrate.Value;
				num2 = s2.MassFlowrate.Value;
				num3 = num * (double)flowDir1 + num2 * (double)flowDir2;
				if (Math.Sign(num * (double)flowDir1) != Math.Sign(num2 * (double)flowDir2))
				{
					throw new ArgumentException("The two streams must flow on the same direction.");
				}
				if (Math.Sign(num * (double)flowDir1) > 0 && Math.Sign(num2 * (double)flowDir2) > 0)
				{
					massFlowrate.Value = num3;
					oilMassFlowrate.Value = s1.OilMassFlowrate.Value + s2.OilMassFlowrate.Value;
					if (oilMassFlowrate.Value > 0.0)
					{
						oilGravity.Value = oilMassFlowrate.Value / (s1.OilMassFlowrate.Value / s1.OilGravity.Value + s2.OilMassFlowrate.Value / s2.OilGravity.Value);
						isBlackOil = true;
					}
					else
					{
						oilGravity.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
					}
					gasMassFlowrate.Value = s1.GasMassFlowrate.Value + s2.GasMassFlowrate.Value;
					if (gasMassFlowrate.Value > 0.0)
					{
						gasGravity.Value = gasMassFlowrate.Value / (s1.GasMassFlowrate.Value / s1.GasGravity.Value + s2.GasMassFlowrate.Value / s2.GasGravity.Value);
					}
					else
					{
						gasGravity.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
					}
					waterMassFlowrate.Value = s1.WaterMassFlowrate.Value + s2.WaterMassFlowrate.Value;
					if (waterMassFlowrate.Value > 0.0)
					{
						waterGravity.Value = waterMassFlowrate.Value / (s1.WaterMassFlowrate.Value / s1.WaterGravity.Value + s2.WaterMassFlowrate.Value / s2.WaterGravity.Value);
					}
					else
					{
						waterGravity.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
					}
					double[] array = new double[3];
					double[] array2 = new double[3];
					array[0] = s1.GasMassFlowrate.Value / (s1.GasGravity.Value * 1.23031271157076);
					array[1] = s1.OilMassFlowrate.Value / (s1.OilGravity.Value * 998.94);
					array[2] = s1.WaterMassFlowrate.Value / (s1.WaterGravity.Value * 998.94);
					array2[0] = s2.GasMassFlowrate.Value / (s2.GasGravity.Value * 1.23031271157076);
					array2[1] = s2.OilMassFlowrate.Value / (s2.OilGravity.Value * 998.94);
					array2[2] = s2.WaterMassFlowrate.Value / (s2.WaterGravity.Value * 998.94);
					double num4 = array[1] + array2[1];
					_ = array[0];
					_ = array[1];
					_ = array[2];
					_ = array2[0];
					_ = array2[1];
					_ = array2[2];
					if (s1.UserSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY && s2.UserSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY)
					{
						userSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value = (s1.UserSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value * array[1] + s2.UserSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value * array2[1]) / num4;
					}
					else
					{
						userSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
					}
					if (s1.OilWatsonK.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY && s2.OilWatsonK.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY)
					{
						double num5 = Math.Pow(s1.OilWatsonK.Value * s1.OilGravity.Value, 3.0);
						double num6 = Math.Pow(s2.OilWatsonK.Value * s2.OilGravity.Value, 3.0);
						double x = (array[1] * num5 + array2[1] * num6) / num4;
						double num7 = (array[1] * s1.OilGravity.Value + array2[1] * s2.OilGravity.Value) / num4;
						oilWatsonK.Value = Math.Pow(x, 1.0 / 3.0) / num7;
					}
					else
					{
						oilWatsonK.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
					}
					if (s1.OilViscosityParamA.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY && s1.OilViscosityParamB.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY && s2.OilViscosityParamA.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY && s2.OilViscosityParamB.Value != RealValue.FEMPTY)
					{
						if (array[1] > 0.0 && array2[1] > 0.0)
						{
							string text = ((s1.OilViscosityEquationName != null) ? s1.OilViscosityEquationName : s2.OilViscosityEquationName);
							double value = 1.0;
							double num8 = 0.0;
							switch (text)
							{
							case "General Equation":
							case "Eyring Equation":
								value = Math.Pow(s1.OilViscosityParamA.Value, array[1] / num4) * Math.Pow(s2.OilViscosityParamA.Value, array2[1] / num4);
								num8 = Math.Pow(s1.OilViscosityParamB.Value, array[1] / num4) * Math.Pow(s2.OilViscosityParamB.Value, array2[1] / num4);
								break;
							case "ASTM Equation":
							{
								double num9 = Math.Log10(560.0);
								double num10 = Math.Log10(672.0);
								double y = Math.Pow(10.0, s1.OilViscosityParamA.Value - s1.OilViscosityParamB.Value * num9);
								y = Math.Pow(10.0, y) - 0.7;
								double y2 = Math.Pow(10.0, s1.OilViscosityParamA.Value - s1.OilViscosityParamB.Value * num10);
								y2 = Math.Pow(10.0, y2) - 0.7;
								double y3 = Math.Pow(10.0, s2.OilViscosityParamA.Value - s2.OilViscosityParamB.Value * num9);
								y3 = Math.Pow(10.0, y3) - 0.7;
								double y4 = Math.Pow(10.0, s2.OilViscosityParamA.Value - s2.OilViscosityParamB.Value * num10);
								y4 = Math.Pow(10.0, y4) - 0.7;
								double num11 = Math.Pow(y, array[1] / num4) * Math.Pow(y3, array2[1] / num4);
								double num12 = Math.Pow(y2, array[1] / num4) * Math.Pow(y4, array2[1] / num4);
								double num13 = Math.Log10(Math.Log10(num11 + 0.7));
								double num14 = Math.Log10(Math.Log10(num12 + 0.7));
								num8 = (num13 - num14) / (num10 - num9);
								value = num13 + num9 * num8;
								break;
							}
							}
							oilViscosityParamA.Value = value;
							oilViscosityParamB.Value = num8;
							OilViscosityEquationName = text;
							DeadOilViscosityCorrelation = "Specify Coefficients";
						}
						else if (array[1] <= 0.0 && array2[1] > 0.0)
						{
							oilViscosityParamA.Value = s2.OilViscosityParamA.Value;
							oilViscosityParamB.Value = s2.OilViscosityParamB.Value;
							OilViscosityEquationName = s2.OilViscosityEquationName;
							DeadOilViscosityCorrelation = s2.DeadOilViscosityCorrelation;
						}
						else
						{
							oilViscosityParamA.Value = s1.OilViscosityParamA.Value;
							oilViscosityParamB.Value = s1.OilViscosityParamB.Value;
							OilViscosityEquationName = s1.OilViscosityEquationName;
							DeadOilViscosityCorrelation = s1.DeadOilViscosityCorrelation;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						oilViscosityParamA.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
						oilViscosityParamB.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
						string oilViscosityEquationName = ((s1.OilViscosityEquationName != null) ? s1.OilViscosityEquationName : s2.OilViscosityEquationName);
						OilViscosityEquationName = oilViscosityEquationName;
						DeadOilViscosityCorrelation = s1.DeadOilViscosityCorrelation;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (flowDir1 >= 0 || flowDir2 >= 0)
					{
						throw new ArgumentNullException("Unknown flow direction.");
					}
					CopyStreamComposition(s1);
					massFlowrate.Value = num3;
					double num15 = Math.Abs(num3 / num);
					oilMassFlowrate.Value = s1.OilMassFlowrate.Value * num15;
					gasMassFlowrate.Value = s1.GasMassFlowrate.Value * num15;
					waterMassFlowrate.Value = s1.WaterMassFlowrate.Value * num15;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Merge Black Oil Parameters failed.", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void mixingBOStreams(StreamCollection streams, double[] massfrac)
		{
			IBlackOilStream blackOilStream = null;
			IPhase phase = null;
			int num = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			double num4 = 0.0;
			double num5 = 0.0;
			double num6 = 0.0;
			double num7 = 0.0;
			double num8 = 0.0;
			double num9 = 0.0;
			double num10 = 0.0;
			double num11 = 0.0;
			double num12 = 0.0;
			double num13 = 0.0;
			double num14 = 0.0;
			double num15 = 0.0;
			double num16 = 0.0;
			double num17 = 0.0;
			try
			{
				num = streams.Count;
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					blackOilStream = streams.Item(i) as IBlackOilStream;
					num4 = massfrac[i];
					num2 += num4;
					if (Math.Abs(num4 - 1.0) < 0.0001)
					{
						CopyBlackOilParameters(blackOilStream);
						return;
					}
					if (blackOilStream.IsUsingLimitedComposition)
					{
						if (i == 0)
						{
							flag = true;
						}
						else if (!flag)
						{
							throw new ArgumentException("If using limited composition, all streams should use. ", "streams");
						}
					}
				}
				if (Math.Abs(num2 - 1.0) > 0.0001)
				{
					throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("massfrac ", num2, " is not equal to 1.0");
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					blackOilStream = streams.Item(i) as IBlackOilStream;
					num17 = Math.Abs(blackOilStream.MassFlowrate.Value);
					num14 = blackOilStream.OilMassFlowrate.Value / num17 * massfrac[i];
					num15 = blackOilStream.GasMassFlowrate.Value / num17 * massfrac[i];
					num16 = blackOilStream.WaterMassFlowrate.Value / num17 * massfrac[i];
					num5 += num14;
					num6 += num15;
					num7 += num16;
					num8 += num14 / blackOilStream.OilGravity.Value;
					num9 += num15 / blackOilStream.GasGravity.Value;
					num10 += num16 / blackOilStream.WaterGravity.Value;
				}
				num2 = num5 + num6 + num7;
				if (Math.Abs(num2 - 1.0) > 0.0001)
				{
					throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("mass fractions of Phases  ", num2, " are not equal to 1.0");
				}
				num17 = Math.Abs(massFlowrate.Value);
				if (num5 >= 0.0)
				{
					oilMassFlowrate.Value = num5 * num17;
					if (num6 >= 0.0)
					{
						gasMassFlowrate.Value = num6 * num17;
						if (num7 >= 0.0)
						{
							waterMassFlowrate.Value = num7 * num17;
							num11 = num5 / num8;
							num12 = num6 / num9;
							num13 = num7 / num10;
							if (num11 >= 0.0)
							{
								oilGravity.Value = num11;
								if (num12 >= 0.0)
								{
									gasGravity.Value = num12;
									if (num13 >= 0.0)
									{
										waterGravity.Value = num13;
										if (flag)
										{
											isUsingLimitedComposition = true;
											phase = Phases[0] as IPhase;
											num2 = 0.0;
											double[] array = new double[phase.NumberOfCompounds];
											double[] array2 = new double[num];
											num17 = Math.Abs(massFlowrate.Value);
											for (int i = 0; i < phase.NumberOfCompounds; i++)
											{
												array[i] = 0.0;
												for (int j = 0; j < num; j++)
												{
													blackOilStream = streams.Item(j) as IBlackOilStream;
													array2[j] = num17 * massfrac[j] * blackOilStream.GasMassFlowrate.Value / Math.Abs(blackOilStream.MassFlowrate.Value);
													num3 = blackOilStream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMassFractions[i].Value;
													if (num3 < 0.0)
													{
														throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("Component Mass Fraction " + i, num3, " has not been set properly. ");
													}
													array[i] += array2[j] * num3;
												}
												phase.ComponentMassFractions[i].Value = array[i] / gasMassFlowrate.Value;
												num2 += array[i] / gasMassFlowrate.Value;
											}
											if (Math.Abs(num2 - 1.0) > 0.001)
											{
												throw new Exception("The sum of component mass fractions is not equal to one. Sum = " + num2);
											}
										}
										else
										{
											IsUsingLimitedComposition = false;
										}
										double[] array3 = new double[num];
										double num18 = 0.0;
										for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
										{
											blackOilStream = streams.Item(i) as IBlackOilStream;
											array3[i] = blackOilStream.OilMassFlowrate.Value / Math.Abs(blackOilStream.MassFlowrate.Value) * massfrac[i] * Math.Abs(massFlowrate.Value) / (blackOilStream.OilGravity.Value * 998.94);
											num18 += array3[i];
										}
										double num19 = 0.0;
										double num20 = 0.0;
										double num21 = 1.0;
										double num22 = 0.0;
										string oilViscosityEquationName = blackOilStream.OilViscosityEquationName;
										double num23 = Math.Log10(560.0);
										double num24 = Math.Log10(672.0);
										double[,] array4 = new double[num, 2];
										double num25 = 1.0;
										double num26 = 1.0;
										for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
										{
											blackOilStream = streams.Item(i) as IBlackOilStream;
											num19 += blackOilStream.UserSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value * array3[i];
											num20 += Math.Pow(blackOilStream.OilWatsonK.Value * blackOilStream.OilGravity.Value, 3.0) * array3[i];
											switch (oilViscosityEquationName)
											{
											case "General Equation":
											case "Eyring Equation":
												num21 *= Math.Pow(blackOilStream.OilViscosityParamA.Value, array3[i] / num18);
												num22 += Math.Pow(blackOilStream.OilViscosityParamB.Value, array3[i] / num18);
												break;
											case "ASTM Equation":
												array4[i, 0] = Math.Pow(10.0, blackOilStream.OilViscosityParamA.Value - blackOilStream.OilViscosityParamB.Value * num23);
												array4[i, 0] = Math.Pow(10.0, array4[i, 0]) - 0.7;
												array4[i, 1] = Math.Pow(10.0, blackOilStream.OilViscosityParamA.Value - blackOilStream.OilViscosityParamB.Value * num24);
												array4[i, 1] = Math.Pow(10.0, array4[i, 1]) - 0.7;
												num25 *= Math.Pow(array4[i, 0], array3[i] / num18);
												num26 *= Math.Pow(array4[i, 1], array3[i] / num18);
												break;
											}
										}
										num19 /= num18;
										num20 /= num18;
										userSpecifiedSurfaceTension.Value = num19;
										oilWatsonK.Value = Math.Pow(num20, 1.0 / 3.0) / oilGravity.Value;
										if (oilViscosityEquationName == "ASTM Equation")
										{
											double num27 = Math.Log10(Math.Log10(num25 + 0.7));
											double num28 = Math.Log10(Math.Log10(num26 + 0.7));
											num22 = (num27 - num28) / (num24 - num23);
											num21 = num27 + num23 * num22;
										}
										oilViscosityParamA.Value = num21;
										oilViscosityParamB.Value = num22;
										OilViscosityEquationName = oilViscosityEquationName;
										DeadOilViscosityCorrelation = "Specify Coefficients";
										return;
									}
									waterGravity.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
									throw new Exception("Can't calculate water specfic gravity. ");
								}
								gasGravity.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
								throw new Exception("Can't calculate gas specfic gravity. ");
							}
							oilGravity.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
							throw new Exception("Can't calculate oil specfic gravity. ");
						}
						waterMassFlowrate.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
						throw new Exception("Can't calculate water mass flow rate. ");
					}
					gasMassFlowrate.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
					throw new Exception("Can't calculate gas mass flow rate. ");
				}
				oilMassFlowrate.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				throw new Exception("Can't calculate oil mass flow rate. ");
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("mixing black oil streams failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}
	}

	[Guid("8F398DFA-E7FE-45A9-9C3F-A076DE62B718")]
	public interface IFractionList : IList, ICollection, IEnumerable, ICloneable
	{
		new IRealValueWithCallBack this[int idx] { get; set; }

		void Normalise();
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class FractionList : ArrayList, IFractionList, IList, ICollection, IEnumerable, ICloneable
	{
		public new IRealValueWithCallBack this[int Index]
		{
			get
			{
				return (IRealValueWithCallBack)base[Index];
			}
			set
			{
				this[Index] = value;
			}
		}

		public override int Add(object Item)
		{
			if (Item == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("Item");
			}
			if (Item is IRealValueWithCallBack)
			{
				return base.Add(Item);
			}
			throw new ArgumentException("Item", "Object Of Type Not Derived From IRealValueWithCallBack");
		}

		public void Normalise()
		{
			int num = -32767;
			int num2 = -32767;
			IRealValueWithCallBack realValueWithCallBack = null;
			double num3 = -32767.0;
			if (!HasEmpty())
			{
				num3 = 0.0;
				num2 = Count;
				for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
				{
					realValueWithCallBack = this[num];
					num3 += realValueWithCallBack.Value;
				}
				if (num3 <= 0.0)
				{
					throw new Exception("Unable To Normalise Composition");
				}
				for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
				{
					this[num].Value /= num3;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool HasEmpty()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < Count; i++)
			{
				if (this[i].Value == -32767.0)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

		{

					initWrapperMembers();

			}

		public void InitFromCTF(object HysysSecurity, string ctfPath, IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!isInitialised)
				{
					initWrapperMembers();
				}
				if (pMultiflash == null)
				{
					packageSwitch();
				}
				pBase.HysysSecurity = HysysSecurity;
				pBase.InitFromCTF(ctfPath, Stream);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("InitFromCTF failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public void Copy(object hysysSecurity, object propertyManager, object compoundCollection, IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			try
			{
				if (pMultiflash == null)
				{
					packageSwitch();
				}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			try
			{
				if (pCOMThermo != null)
				{
					(pCOMThermo as ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoPureComp).Dispose();
				}
				if (pMultiflash != null)
				{
					(pMultiflash as ImpIPPPWithMultiflashThroughCOMThermo).Dispose();
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		private void initWrapperMembers()
		{
			try
			{
				if (pCOMThermo == null)
				{
					pCOMThermo = new ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoPureComp();
				}
				pBase = pCOMThermo;
				changedComponentsAction = ChangedComponentsActionEnum.ThrowException;
				combinationMethod = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.HysysMethodForWater_Oil;
				flashLevel = FlashCalculationLevelEnum.FullSet;
				licenseCookie = 0;
				mLog = null;
				isInitialised = true;
				mCalculatedPhases = CalculatedPhasesEnum.Variable;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("initWrapperMembers failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void packageSwitch()
		{
			try
			{
				if (pCOMThermo == null && pMultiflash != null)
				{
					pMultiflash = null;
					pCOMThermo = new ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoPureComp();
					pBase = pCOMThermo;
				}
				else
				{
					if (pMultiflash != null || pCOMThermo == null)
					{
						throw new Exception("Can't do switch. ");
					}
					pCOMThermo = null;
					pMultiflash = new ImpIPPPWithMultiflashThroughCOMThermo();
					pBase = pMultiflash;
				}
				pBase.LicenseCookie = licenseCookie;
				pBase.Initialise();
				pBase.ChangedComponentsAction = changedComponentsAction;
				pBase.LiquidPhaseCombination = combinationMethod;
				pBase.FlashLevel = flashLevel;
				pBase.Log = mLog;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("packageSwitch failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}
	}

		public override double CalculateProperty(IFlowStream Stream, PropertyType RT, PhaseType PT, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			//IL_009b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a1: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			IEnumeratedPropertyContainer val = null;
			int num = -32767;
			double num2 = -32767.0;
			int num3 = -32767;

			double num4 = -32767.0;
			double num5 = -32767.0;
			double num6 = -32767.0;
			double result = -32767.0;
			try
			{
				Check(Stream);
				phase = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0);
				SetComposition(phase);
				SetFlash(FT, data1, data2);
				num3 = mPhaseCollection.NumberOfActivePhases;
				for (num = 0; num < num3; num++)
				{

					{

					}

					}
					else
					{

					num2 = mBasicPhase.MolecularWeight;
					switch (RT)
					{
					case PropertyType.Pressure:

						result = mBasicPhase.Pressure * 1000.0;
						break;

						result = mBasicPhase.Temperature;
						break;

						break;
					case PropertyType.Density:
						mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_MV, (object)mBasicPhase);
						result = num2 / (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_MV);
						break;
					case PropertyType.Enthalpy:
						mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_H, (object)mBasicPhase);
						result = mBasicPhase.Enthalpy / num2 * 1000.0;
						break;
					case PropertyType.Entropy:
						mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_S, (object)mBasicPhase);
						result = mBasicPhase.Entropy / num2 * 1000.0;
						break;
					case PropertyType.InternalEnergy:
						mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_MV, (object)mBasicPhase);
						num4 = num2 / (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_MV);
						mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_P, (object)mBasicPhase);
						num5 = mBasicPhase.Pressure * 1000.0;
						mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_H, (object)mBasicPhase);
						num6 = mBasicPhase.Enthalpy / num2 * 1000.0;
						result = ((num4 != -32767.0 && num6 != -32767.0 && num6 != -32767.0) ? (-32767.0) : (num6 - num4 * num5));
						break;
					case PropertyType.Cp:
						mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_Cp, (object)mBasicPhase);
						result = mBasicPhase.Cp / num2 * 1000.0;
						break;
					case PropertyType.Cv:
						try
						{
							mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_Cv, (object)mBasicPhase);
							result = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_Cv) / num2 * 1000.0;
						}
						catch (Exception)
						{
							mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_Cp, (object)mBasicPhase);
							result = ((double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_Cp) - 8314.34) / num2 * 1000.0;
						}
						break;
					case PropertyType.Conductivity:
						mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_TC, (object)mBasicPhase);
						result = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_TC);
						break;
					case PropertyType.Viscosity:
						mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_VIS, (object)mBasicPhase);
						result = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_VIS);
						break;
					case PropertyType.SonicVelocity:
						if (mID_SV > 0)
						{
							mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_SV, (object)mBasicPhase);
							result = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_SV);
							break;
						}
						throw new Exception("Sonic velocity is not Supported");
					default:
						throw new Exception("Property Not Supported");
					}
					break;
				}
				return result;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Calculate Property Failure", innerException);
			}
		}

			//IL_006a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

				SetComposition(phase);
				if (PT == PhaseType.Liquid)

					for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)

						object activePhase = mPhaseCollection.GetActivePhase(num);
						reference = (IBasicPhase)((activePhase is COTHPhase) ? activePhase : null);
						if ((int)mBasicPhase.PhaseType == (int)PT)
						{
							mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_ST, (object)mBasicPhase);
							IBasicPhase obj = mBasicPhase;
							result = (double)((IEnumeratedPropertyContainer)((obj is IEnumeratedPropertyContainer) ? obj : null)).GetPropertyValue(mID_ST);
							break;

				}
				return result;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)

			}
		}

		public override void FlashStream(IFlowStream Stream, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			IPhase phase = null;
			try
			{
				if (Stream.StreamID != mLastStreamID || mChangedComps == ChangedComponentsActionEnum.ReInitComThermo)
				{
					Check(Stream, Disable: true);
				}
				phase = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0);
				SetComposition(phase);
				ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(Stream, FT, data1, data2);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("FlashStream Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		public override void ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(IFlowStream Stream, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			double num = -32767.0;
			int num2 = -32767;
			int num3 = -32767;
			try
			{
				base.ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(Stream, FT, data1, data2);
				if (!mDoFlash || (Stream as IBlackOilStream).IsBlackOil)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (!mReady)
				{
					SetComposition(Stream.GetPhaseByID(0));
				}
				DoFlash(FT, data1, data2);
				UpdateStream(Stream, IsFullSet: true);
				mLastStreamID = Stream.StreamID;
				switch (FT)
				{
				case FlashType.PHFlash:
					num = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value;
					if (data2 != num)
					{
						if (Math.Abs(data2) > num)
						{
							if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / data2) > 0.001)
							{
								LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PH Flash");
							}
						}
						else if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / num) > 0.001)
						{
							LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PH Flash");
						}
					}
					Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Enthalpy.Value = data2;
					break;
				case FlashType.PSFlash:
					num = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Entropy.Value;
					if (data2 != num)
					{
						if (Math.Abs(data2) > Math.Abs(num))
						{
							if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / data2) > 0.001)
							{
								LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PS Flash");
							}
						}
						else if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / num) > 0.001)
						{
							LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PS Flash");
						}
					}
					Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).Entropy.Value = data2;
					break;
				case FlashType.PVFlash:
					num = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarVapourFraction.Value;
					if (data2 != num)
					{
						if (num == 0.0)
						{
							if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / data2) > 0.001)
							{
								LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PV Flash");
							}
						}
						else if (Math.Abs((num - data2) / num) > 0.001)
						{
							LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "Inaccurate PV Flash");
						}
					}
					Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).MolarVapourFraction.Value = data2;
					break;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				if (mLog != null)
				{
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, "Flash Failure");
					string aMessage = "Flash Type = " + FT;
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					aMessage = "Data1 = " + data1.ToString("0.00000");
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					aMessage = "Data2 = " + data2.ToString("0.00000");
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					aMessage = "Calc = " + num.ToString("0.00000");
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					num3 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMoleFractions.Count;
					for (num2 = 0; num2 < num3; num2++)
					{
						aMessage = "Comp = " + (num2 + 1) + " : Mole Fraction = " + Stream.GetPhaseByID(0).ComponentMoleFractions[num2].Value.ToString("0.000000000");
						LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					}
				}
				throw new Exception("ReflashStreamWithSameComposition Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected void nullify()
		{
			mPropertyManager = null;
			mFlashManager = null;
			mThermoProperty = null;
			mIPM = null;
			mPhaseCollection = null;
			mCompoundCollection = null;
			mIsInitFromStrings = false;
			ccPopulated = false;
		}

		protected void setCompoundInfo(IFlowStream s)
		{
			COTHCompound val = null;
			int num = 0;
			int numberOfCompounds = mCompoundCollection.NumberOfCompounds;
			if (mComponents == null)
			{
				mComponents = new IComponent[numberOfCompounds];
			}
			double num2 = 0.0;
			try
			{
				IPhase phaseByType = s.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall);
				for (int i = 0; i < numberOfCompounds; i++)
				{
					object obj = mCompoundCollection[i];
					val = (COTHCompound)((obj is COTHCompound) ? obj : null);
					num2 = ((IBasicCompound)val)[273.15, 101325.0, (tagExtrapolationEnum)0, ref num];
					phaseByType.GetComponent(i).MolecularWeight.Value = num2;
					if (mComponents[i] == null)
					{
						mComponents[i] = new Component();
					}
					mComponents[i].MolecularWeight.Value = num2;
					if (phaseByType.GetComponent(i).ID != ((IBasicCompound)val).HyprotechID)
					{
						throw new Exception("Components in flow stream do not match those in stored compound collection.");
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("setCompoundInfo failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected string AppendToThermoPath(string FileName)
		{
			return Path.Combine(mThermoPath, FileName);
		}

		protected override void Check(IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!mCheckDisable && mValidateFlowStream && !Validate(Stream))
				{
					if (mChangedComps == ChangedComponentsActionEnum.ThrowException)
					{
						throw new Exception("Component List Has Changed");
					}
					InitComponents(Stream);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				mCheckDisable = false;
			}
		}

		protected override void Check(IFlowStream Stream, bool Disable)
		{
			try
			{
				if (mLastStreamID == Stream.StreamID && !mCheckDisable && mValidateFlowStream && !Validate(Stream))
				{
					if (mChangedComps == ChangedComponentsActionEnum.ThrowException)
					{
						throw new Exception("Component List Has Changed");
					}
					InitComponents(Stream);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				mCheckDisable = false;
				if (Disable)
				{
					mCheckDisable = true;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void DoFlash(FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			int num = -32767;
			double num2 = -32767.0;
			int num3 = -32767;
			IRealArrayDisp val = null;
			try
			{
				if (mReady)
				{
					SetParameters(FT, data1, data2);
					mFlashManager.Flash((object)mPhaseCollection);
					return;
				}
				throw new Exception("Composition Not Ready");
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				if (mLog != null)
				{
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, "Flash Failure");
					string aMessage = "Flash Type = " + FT;
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					aMessage = "Data1 = " + data1.ToString("0.000");
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					aMessage = "Data2 = " + data2.ToString("0.000");
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					object molarComposition = mBasicPhase.MolarComposition;
					val = (IRealArrayDisp)((molarComposition is IRealArrayDisp) ? molarComposition : null);
					num3 = val.Size;
					for (num = 0; num < num3; num++)
					{
						num2 = val[num];
						aMessage = "Comp = " + (num + 1) + " : Mole Fraction = " + num2.ToString("0.000000000");
						LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					}
				}
				throw new Exception("DoFlash Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void EvaluatePhaseProperties(IFlowStream Stream, int PhaseID, bool isFullSet)
		{
			//IL_0463: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0469: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			//IL_00f1: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00fb: Expected I4, but got Unknown
			IEnumeratedPropertyContainer val = null;
			int num = -32767;
			double num2 = -32767.0;
			double num3 = -32767.0;
			int num4 = -32767;
			IPhase phase = null;
			IRealArrayDisp val2 = null;
			double num5 = -32767.0;
			double num6 = 0.0;
			try
			{
				phase = Stream.GetPhaseByID(PhaseID);
				IBasicPhase obj = mBasicPhase;
				val = (IEnumeratedPropertyContainer)(object)((obj is IEnumeratedPropertyContainer) ? obj : null);
				object molarComposition = mBasicPhase.MolarComposition;
				val2 = (IRealArrayDisp)((molarComposition is IRealArrayDisp) ? molarComposition : null);
				num4 = phase.NumberOfCompounds;
				for (num = 0; num < num4; num++)
				{
					num2 = val2[num];
					phase.ComponentMoleFractions[num].Value = num2;
				}
				num3 = phase.MolecularWeight.Value;
				mBasicPhase.MolecularWeight = num3;
				phase.Pressure.Value = mBasicPhase.Pressure * 1000.0;
				phase.Temperature.Value = mBasicPhase.Temperature;
				phase.PhaseType = (PhaseType)mBasicPhase.PhaseType;
				phase.MolarFraction.Value = mBasicPhase.PhaseFraction;
				if (phase.PhaseType == PhaseType.Vapour)
				{
					phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
				}
				else
				{
					phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
				}
				if (!isFullSet)
				{
					return;
				}
				mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_MV, (object)mBasicPhase);
				num5 = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_MV);
				if (num5 != -32767.0)
				{
					phase.Density.Value = num3 / num5;
				}
				else
				{
					phase.Density.Erase();
				}
				((ICOMExtensionHandler)mPropertyManager).GetPropCalcBase("Enthalpy", ref whatBase);
				if (checkHBase == BooleanSwitchEnum.On)
				{
					for (num = 0; num < num4; num++)
					{
						num2 = val2[num];
						num6 += num2 * hfArray[num];
					}
				}
				else
				{
					num6 = 0.0;
				}
				mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_H, (object)mBasicPhase);
				if (base.ThermoBase == ThermoBaseEnum.NotIncludeHF && whatBase != 0)
				{
					phase.Enthalpy.Value = (mBasicPhase.Enthalpy - num6) / num3 * 1000.0;
				}
				else if (base.ThermoBase == ThermoBaseEnum.IncludeHF && whatBase == 0)
				{
					phase.Enthalpy.Value = (mBasicPhase.Enthalpy + num6) / num3 * 1000.0;
				}
				else
				{
					phase.Enthalpy.Value = mBasicPhase.Enthalpy / num3 * 1000.0;
				}
				mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_S, (object)mBasicPhase);
				phase.Entropy.Value = mBasicPhase.Entropy / num3 * 1000.0;
				if (!phase.Density.IsUnknown)
				{
					phase.InternalEnergy.Value = phase.Enthalpy.Value - phase.Pressure.Value / phase.Density.Value;
				}
				mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_Cp, (object)mBasicPhase);
				phase.Cp.Value = mBasicPhase.Cp / num3 * 1000.0;
				if (mCvSupported && flashLevel < FlashCalculationLevelEnum.PlusDerivertive)
				{
					try
					{
						mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_Cv, (object)mBasicPhase);
						phase.Cv.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_Cv) / num3 * 1000.0;
						if (phase.Cv.Value > phase.Cp.Value || phase.Cv.Value < 0.0)
						{
							string message = "Cv = " + phase.Cv.Value;
							phase.Cv.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
							throw new Exception(message);
						}
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
						mCvSupported = false;
					}
				}
				else if (flashLevel >= FlashCalculationLevelEnum.PlusDerivertive)
				{
					phase.Cv.Value = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				}
				else
				{
					phase.Cv.Value = phase.Cp.Value - 8314.34 / num3;
				}
				if ((int)mBasicPhase.PhaseType == 4)
				{
					mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_ST, (object)mBasicPhase);
					phase.SurfaceTension.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_ST) / 1000.0;
				}
				if (flashLevel >= FlashCalculationLevelEnum.ThermalAndTransport)
				{
					mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_TC, (object)mBasicPhase);
					phase.Conductivity.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_TC);
					mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_VIS, (object)mBasicPhase);
					phase.Viscosity.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_VIS) / 1000.0;
				}
				if (flashLevel >= FlashCalculationLevelEnum.PlusSonicVelocity && mID_SV > 0)
				{
					mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_SV, (object)mBasicPhase);
					phase.SonicVelocity.Value = (double)val.GetPropertyValue(mID_SV);
				}
				if (flashLevel >= FlashCalculationLevelEnum.PlusDerivertive)
				{
					getDerivatives(phase);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("EvaluatePhaseProperties failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void getDerivatives(IPhase phs)
		{
			IDerivativeHandler val = (IDerivativeHandler)(object)mPropertyManager;
			IBasicPhase obj = mBasicPhase;
			IDerivativeProp val2 = (IDerivativeProp)(object)((obj is IDerivativeProp) ? obj : null);
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = 0;
			int num4 = 0;
			double num5 = 0.0;
			double num6 = 0.0;
			double num7 = 0.0;
			double num8 = 0.0;
			double num9 = 0.0;
			double num10 = 0.0;
			try
			{
				val.TurnOnDerivative((byte)1);
				val2.ClearDerivativesProps();
				val2.AddDerivativeProp(mID_MV, mID_dPressure, (tagResultTypeEnum)1, (tagResultTypeEnum)1, ref num);
				val2.AddDerivativeProp(mID_MV, mID_dTemperature, (tagResultTypeEnum)1, (tagResultTypeEnum)1, ref num2);
				val2.AddDerivativeProp(mID_H, mID_dPressure, (tagResultTypeEnum)1, (tagResultTypeEnum)1, ref num3);
				val2.AddDerivativeProp(mID_H, mID_dTemperature, (tagResultTypeEnum)1, (tagResultTypeEnum)1, ref num4);
				val2.SetNeedDerivative(num, (byte)0);
				val2.SetNeedDerivative(num2, (byte)0);
				val2.SetNeedDerivative(num3, (byte)0);
				val2.SetNeedDerivative(num4, (byte)0);
				val2.SetNeedDerivative(num, (byte)1);
				val2.SetNeedDerivative(num2, (byte)1);
				mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_MV, (object)mBasicPhase);
				val2.GetPointDerivative(num, ref num5);
				val2.GetPointDerivative(num2, ref num6);
				val2.SetNeedDerivative(num, (byte)0);
				val2.SetNeedDerivative(num2, (byte)0);
				val2.SetNeedDerivative(num3, (byte)1);
				val2.SetNeedDerivative(num4, (byte)1);
				mThermoProperty.EvaluatePhaseProperty(mID_H, (object)mBasicPhase);
				val2.GetPointDerivative(num3, ref num7);
				val2.GetPointDerivative(num4, ref num8);
				val2.SetNeedDerivative(num3, (byte)0);
				val2.SetNeedDerivative(num4, (byte)0);
				num9 = mBasicPhase.MolecularWeight;
				num10 = mBasicPhase.MolarVolume;
				phs.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure.Value = (0.0 - num9) * num5 / (1000.0 * num10 * num10);
				phs.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature.Value = (0.0 - num9) * num6 / (num10 * num10);
				phs.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure.Value = (num7 - mBasicPhase.Pressure * num5 - num10) / num9;
				if (phs.Cp.Value == RealValue.FEMPTY)
				{
					phs.Cp.Value = num8 * 1000.0 / num9;
				}
				if (phs.Cv.Value == RealValue.FEMPTY || !mCvSupported)
				{
					phs.Cv.Value = phs.Cp.Value + phs.Temperature.Value * Math.Pow(phs.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature.Value / phs.Density.Value, 2.0) / (0.0 - phs.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure.Value);
				}
				phs.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature.Value = phs.Cp.Value + phs.Pressure.Value * phs.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature.Value / Math.Pow(phs.Density.Value, 2.0);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("getDerivatives failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected double GetPhaseMolWt()
		{
			//IL_0023: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			IComponent component = null;
			double num = -32767.0;
			int num2 = -32767;
			int num3 = -32767;
			IRealArrayDisp val = (IRealArrayDisp)mBasicPhase.MolarComposition;
			num3 = mComponents.GetUpperBound(0) + 1;
			num = 0.0;
			for (num2 = 0; num2 < num3; num2++)
			{
				component = mComponents[num2];
				num += val[num2] * component.MolecularWeight.Value;
			}
			return num;
		}

		protected virtual void GetPropertyID()
		{
			mID_T = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("Temperature");
			mID_P = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("Pressure");
			mID_H = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("Enthalpy");
			mID_S = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("Entropy");
			mID_Cp = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("Cp");
			mID_Cv = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("Cv");
			mID_MV = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("MolarVolume");
			mID_MW = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("MolecularWeight");
			mID_VIS = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("Viscosity");
			mID_TC = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("ThermalConductivity");
			mID_ST = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("SurfaceTension");
			mID_SV = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("SonicVelocity");
			mID_dTemperature = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("dT");
			mID_dPressure = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("dP");
		}

		protected void InitComThermoObjects()
		{
			//IL_0038: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)

		protected void PropertyPackageSetup()
		{
			//IL_000c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
