					mFlashManager.Initialize((object)mPropertyManager);
					mThermoProperty = (IThermoProperty)(object)mPropertyManager;
					mPropertyManager.SetPropertyModel(mVapourPkg, (byte)1);
					mPropertyManager.SetPropertyModel(mLiquidPkg, (byte)0);
					mPropertyManager.SetPropModelCreatePropObjects(mFlashOptions, (byte)0);
					if (mCompoundCollection.NumberOfCompounds > 0)
					{
						mIPM.CreateIPs((object)mCompoundCollection, mDatabasePath);
						mIPM.LoadIPObjects((object)mCompoundCollection);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					mPropertyManager.SetPropModelCreatePropObjects(mLiquidPkg, (byte)1);
					mPropertyManager.SetPropModelCreatePropObjects(mVapourPkg, (byte)0);
				}
				GetPropertyID();
				mPropertyManager.HasModified((NotifyChange)1);
				mPropPkgSet = true;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("PropertyPackageSetup Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected void SetComposition(IPhase Phs)
		{
			int num = -32767;
			double num2 = -32767.0;
			int num3 = -32767;
			IRealArrayDisp val = null;
			double num4 = -32767.0;
			try
			{
				ref IBasicPhase reference = ref mBasicPhase;
				object obj = mPhaseCollection.LocatePhase((tagPhaseTypeEnum)1, 0);
				reference = (IBasicPhase)((obj is IBasicPhase) ? obj : null);
				mBasicPhase.ResetProperties(127);
				object molarComposition = mBasicPhase.MolarComposition;
				val = (IRealArrayDisp)((molarComposition is IRealArrayDisp) ? molarComposition : null);
				num3 = Phs.NumberOfCompounds;
				num4 = 0.0;
				for (num = 0; num < num3; num++)
				{
					num2 = (val[num] = Phs.ComponentMoleFractions[num].Value);
					num4 += num2;
					if (Math.Abs(Phs.GetComponent(num).MolecularWeight.Value - 18.016) < 0.001)
					{
						Phs.GetComponent(num).Alias = "Water";
					}
				}
				if (Math.Abs(num4 - 1.0) < 0.001)
				{
					mBasicPhase.MolarComposition = val;
					mReady = true;
					if (checkHBase != BooleanSwitchEnum.On)
					{
						return;
					}
					ICOMExtensionHandler val2 = null;
					try
					{
						val2 = (ICOMExtensionHandler)(object)mPropertyManager;
					}
					catch (Exception ex)
					{
						LogMessage(MessageLogType.Note, "PP is not extensioned. " + ex.Message);
						val2 = null;
					}
					if (val2 != null)
					{
						val2.GetPropCalcBase("Enthalpy", ref whatBase);
						hfArray = new double[num3];
						hf = 0.0;
						COTHCompound val3 = null;
						int num5 = default(int);
						for (num = 0; num < num3; num++)
						{
							mCompoundCollection.GetBasicCompound(num, ref val3);
							hfArray[num] = ((IBasicCompound)val3)[298.15, 101.3, (tagExtrapolationEnum)0, ref num5];
							hf += hfArray[num] * Phs.ComponentMoleFractions[num].Value;
						}
					}
					return;
				}
				throw new Exception("Sum Of Mole Fractions Not 1");
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				if (mLog != null)
				{
					LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, "SetComposition Failure");
					num3 = Phs.NumberOfCompounds;
					for (num = 0; num < num3; num++)
					{
						num2 = val[num];
						string aMessage = "Comp = " + (num + 1) + " : Mole Fraction = " + Phs.ComponentMoleFractions[num].Value.ToString("0.000000000");
						LogMessage(MessageLogType.Error, aMessage);
					}
				}
				throw new Exception("SetComposition Failure", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected void SetFlash(FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			try
			{
				SetParameters(FT, data1, data2);
				if (mReady)
				{
					mPhaseCollection.ResetCollection(0);
					mFlashManager.Flash((object)mPhaseCollection);
					return;
				}
				throw new Exception("Composition Not Ready");
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("SetFlash Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void SetParameters(FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			double num = -32767.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			try
			{
				switch (FT)
				{
				case FlashType.PHFlash:
				case FlashType.PSFlash:
				case FlashType.PVFlash:
					num2 = data1;
					break;
				case FlashType.THFlash:
				case FlashType.TSFlash:
				case FlashType.TVFlash:
					num3 = data1;
					break;
				case FlashType.PTFlash:
					num2 = data1;
					num3 = data2;
					break;
				}
				if (num2 < 0.0)
				{
					throw new Exception("Pressure can not be set less than zero. ");
				}
				if (num3 < 0.0)
				{
					throw new Exception("Temperature can not be set less than zero. ");
				}
				ref IBasicPhase reference = ref mBasicPhase;
				object obj = mPhaseCollection.LocatePhase((tagPhaseTypeEnum)1, 0);
				reference = (IBasicPhase)((obj is IBasicPhase) ? obj : null);
				mBasicPhase.ResetProperties(128);
				if (FT != FlashType.PTFlash)
				{
					num = GetPhaseMolWt();
				}
				switch (FT)
				{
				case FlashType.PHFlash:
					mBasicPhase.Pressure = 0.001 * data1;
					mBasicPhase.Enthalpy = 0.001 * data2 * num;
					break;
				case FlashType.PTFlash:
					mBasicPhase.Pressure = 0.001 * data1;
					mBasicPhase.Temperature = data2;
					break;
				case FlashType.HVFlash:
					mBasicPhase.Enthalpy = 0.001 * data2 * num;
					mBasicPhase.PhaseFraction = data2;
					break;
				case FlashType.PSFlash:
					mBasicPhase.Pressure = 0.001 * data1;
					mBasicPhase.Entropy = 0.001 * data2 * num;
					break;
				case FlashType.PVFlash:
					mBasicPhase.Pressure = 0.001 * data1;
					mBasicPhase.PhaseFraction = data2;
					break;
				case FlashType.THFlash:
					mBasicPhase.Temperature = data1;
					mBasicPhase.Enthalpy = 0.001 * data2 * num;
					break;
				case FlashType.TSFlash:
					mBasicPhase.Temperature = data1;
					mBasicPhase.Entropy = 0.001 * data2 * num;
					break;
				case FlashType.TVFlash:
					mBasicPhase.Temperature = data1;
					mBasicPhase.PhaseFraction = data2;
					break;
				}
				if (checkHBase == BooleanSwitchEnum.On && whatBase == 0 && (FT == FlashType.PHFlash || FT == FlashType.THFlash || FT == FlashType.HVFlash))
				{
					IBasicPhase obj2 = mBasicPhase;
					obj2.Enthalpy -= hf;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("SetParameters Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void AdjustEquilibriumPhases1(IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			int num = -32767;
			int num2 = -32767;
			IPhase phase = null;
			IPhase[] array = null;
			PhaseType phaseType = PhaseType.UnknownPhase;
			try
			{
				IPhase[] array2 = new Phase[1];
				array = array2;
				array[0] = (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(1).Clone();
				phaseType = array[0].PhaseType;
				if (mCalculatedPhases == CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquid)
				{
					Stream.NumberOfPhases = 2;
				}
				else
				{
					Stream.NumberOfPhases = 3;
				}
				num2 = Stream.NumberOfPhases;
				for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
				{
					phase = Stream.GetPhaseByID(num + 1);
					phase.UpdatePhaseByValue(array[0]);
					switch (num)
					{
					case 0:
						phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
						if (phaseType != PhaseType.Vapour)
						{
							phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Vapour;
						}
						break;
					case 1:
						phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
						switch (phaseType)
						{
						case PhaseType.Vapour:
							phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
							break;
						case PhaseType.Aqueous:
							if (mCalculatedPhases != CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquid)
							{
								phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
								phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
							}
							break;
						}
						break;
					case 2:
						phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
						switch (phaseType)
						{
						case PhaseType.Vapour:
							phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
							break;
						case PhaseType.Liquid:
							phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
							phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
							break;
						}
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("AdjustEquilibriumPhases1", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void AdjustEquilibriumPhases2(IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases combinationMethodForLiquidPhases = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine;
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			IPhase phase = null;
			IPhase[] array = null;
			PhaseType[] array2 = null;
			try
			{
				IPhase[] array3 = new Phase[2];
				array = array3;
				array[0] = (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(1).Clone();
				array[1] = (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(2).Clone();
				array2 = new PhaseType[2]
				{
					array[0].PhaseType,
					array[1].PhaseType
				};
				if (mCalculatedPhases == CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquid && array2[0] != PhaseType.Vapour && array2[1] != PhaseType.Vapour)
				{
					combinationMethodForLiquidPhases = mCM;
					mCM = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.HysysMethodForWater_Oil;
					phase = new Phase();
					phase = (IPhase)array[0].Clone();
					CombinePhases(array, phase);
					phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
					phase.MolarFraction.Value = 1.0;
					phase.MassFraction.Value = 1.0;
					phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, phase);
					mCM = combinationMethodForLiquidPhases;
					phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Vapour;
					phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, phase);
				}
				if (mCalculatedPhases != CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid)
				{
					return;
				}
				Stream.NumberOfPhases = 3;
				if (array2[0] != PhaseType.Vapour && array2[1] != PhaseType.Vapour)
				{
					if (array[0].Density.Value < array[1].Density.Value && array[0].PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid)
					{
						num = 0;
						phase = (IPhase)array[0].Clone();
					}
					else
					{
						num = 1;
						phase = (IPhase)array[1].Clone();
					}
					phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Vapour;
					phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 1.0;
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, phase);
					phase = array[num];
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, phase);
					phase = array[1 - num];
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(3, phase);
					return;
				}
				if (array2[0] == PhaseType.Vapour)
				{
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, array[0]);
					num2 = 1;
				}
				else if (array2[1] == PhaseType.Vapour)
				{
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, array[1]);
					num2 = 0;
				}
				if (array2[num2] == PhaseType.Liquid)
				{
					phase = array[num2];
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, phase);
					phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(3, phase);
				}
				else if (array2[num2] == PhaseType.Aqueous)
				{
					phase = array[num2];
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(3, phase);
					phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
					phase.MolarFraction.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MassFraction.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, phase);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("AdjustEquilibriumPhases2", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void AdjustEquilibriumPhases3(IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases combinationMethodForLiquidPhases = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine;
			int num = 0;
			int[] array = new int[2];
			int num2 = 0;
			IPhase[] array2 = new IPhase[2];
			int num3 = 0;
			int num4 = 0;
			IPhase phase = null;
			IPhase[] array3 = new IPhase[3];
			PhaseType[] array4 = new PhaseType[3];
			try
			{
				for (num = 0; num < 3; num++)
				{
					array3[num] = (IPhase)Stream.GetPhaseByID(num + 1).Clone();
					array4[num] = array3[num].PhaseType;
				}
				for (num = 0; num < 3; num++)
				{
					if (array4[num] == PhaseType.Vapour)
					{
						num4++;
						num2 = num;
					}
					else if (array4[num] == PhaseType.Liquid || array4[num] == PhaseType.Aqueous)
					{
						array[num3] = num;
						array2[num3] = array3[num];
						num3++;
					}
				}
				if (num4 != 1 || num3 != 2)
				{
					throw new Exception("Unexpected Phase Type In 3 Phases");
				}
				if (mCalculatedPhases == CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquid)
				{
					Stream.NumberOfPhases = 2;
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, array3[num2]);
					combinationMethodForLiquidPhases = mCM;
					mCM = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.HysysMethodForWater_Oil;
					phase = new Phase();
					phase = (IPhase)array3[0].Clone();
					CombinePhases(array2, phase);
					phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
					phase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = 0.0;
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, phase);
					mCM = combinationMethodForLiquidPhases;
				}
				else if (mCalculatedPhases == CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid)
				{
					Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(1, array3[num2]);
					if (array2[0].Density.Value < array2[1].Density.Value && array2[0].PhaseType == PhaseType.Liquid)
					{
						Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, array2[0]);
						Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(3, array2[1]);
					}
					else
					{
						Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(2, array2[1]);
						Stream.SetPhaseValueByID(3, array2[0]);
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("AdjustEquilibriumPhases3", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateStream(IFlowStream Stream, bool IsFullSet)
		{
			//IL_0084: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_008a: Invalid comparison between Unknown and I4
			int num = -32767;
			int num2 = -32767;
			int num3 = -32767;
			int num4 = -32767;
			int num5 = -32767;
			int num6 = -37767;
			IPhase phase = null;
			IPhase phase2 = null;
			try
			{
				num6 = mPhaseCollection.MaximumNumberOfEquilibriumPhases;
				num5 = mPhaseCollection.NumberOfActivePhases;
				if (Stream.NumberOfPhases != num5)
				{
					Stream.NumberOfPhases = num5;
				}
				phase = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0);
				num2 = 0;
				for (num = 0; num < num6; num++)
				{
					ref IBasicPhase reference = ref mBasicPhase;
					object equilibriumPhase = mPhaseCollection.GetEquilibriumPhase(num);
					reference = (IBasicPhase)((equilibriumPhase is COTHPhase) ? equilibriumPhase : null);
					if ((int)mBasicPhase.PhaseStatus == 1)
					{
						num2++;
						phase2 = Stream.GetPhaseByID(num2);
						phase2.RemoveAllComponents();
						num4 = phase.NumberOfCompounds;
						for (num3 = 0; num3 < num4; num3++)
						{
							phase2.AddComponent(phase.GetComponent(num3));
						}
						EvaluatePhaseProperties(Stream, num2, IsFullSet);
					}
				}
				UpdateOverallPhase(Stream, num5);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("UpdateStream Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		protected int getPropertyIDByType(PropertyType propType)
		{
			return propType switch
			{
				PropertyType.Enthalpy => mID_H, 
				PropertyType.Entropy => mID_S, 
				PropertyType.Cp => mID_Cp, 
				PropertyType.Cv => mID_Cv, 
				PropertyType.Density => mID_MV, 
				PropertyType.SurfaceTension => mID_ST, 
				PropertyType.Conductivity => mID_TC, 
				PropertyType.Viscosity => mID_VIS, 
				PropertyType.SonicVelocity => mID_SV, 
				PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure => mID_dPressure, 
				PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature => mID_dTemperature, 
				_ => throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("propType", "Unsupported property type."), 
			};
		}

		internal string getEncrptedLicenceName(string name)
		{
			string text = null;
			char[] array = name.ToCharArray();
			int num = array.Length;
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				int num2 = array[i] - 15;
				array[i] = (char)num2;
				text += array[i];
			}
			return text;
		}

		internal bool checkLicense(string name)
		{
			string error = "";
			string text = null;
			int cookie = 0;
			try
			{
				text = getEncrptedLicenceName(name);
				bool num = server.RegisterSoftLicense(text, "", 0, licenseCookie, out cookie);
				if (!num || cookie == 0)
				{
					throw new Exception(server.GenerateErrorMessageFromLastError(error));
				}
				if (name == "AEA_Thermo_FlashMgr")
				{
					flashManagerCookie = cookie;
				}
				else
				{
					propManagerCookie = cookie;
				}
				return num;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("checkLicense failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		internal virtual void getComponentsFromCOMThermo(IFlowStream stream)
		{
			IPhase phase = null;
			int num = 0;
			COTHCompound val = null;
			int num2 = -32726;
			string text = null;
			double num3 = -32767.0;
			int num4 = -32767;
			string text2 = null;
			try
			{
				if (stream == null)
				{
					throw new ArgumentNullException();
				}
				phase = stream.GetPhaseByID(0);
				num = mCompoundCollection.NumberOfCompounds;
				mComponents = new IComponent[num];
				if (num != phase.NumberOfCompounds)
				{
					throw new Exception("Component Count Mismatch");
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
				{
					mCompoundCollection.GetBasicCompound(i, ref val);
					text = ((COTHCompoundClass)((val is COTHCompoundClass) ? val : null)).Name;
					num2 = ((IBasicCompound)val).HyprotechID;
					num3 = ((IBasicCompound)val)[273.15, 100000.0, (tagExtrapolationEnum)0, ref num4];
					text2 = ((num2 != 19 && !(Math.Abs(num3 - 18.016) < 0.001)) ? "" : "Water");
					mComponents[i] = new Component(text, num2, num3, text2);
					phase.GetComponent(i).UpdateByValue(mComponents[i]);
				}
				mReady = false;
				mLastStreamID = stream.StreamID;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("getComponentsFromCOMThermo failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public override void Dispose()
		{
			mBasicPhase = null;
			mCompoundCollection = null;
			mFlashManager = null;
			mIPM = null;
			mPER = null;
			mPhaseCollection = null;
			mPropertyManager = null;
			mThermoProperty = null;
			base.Dispose();
		}
	}

	public class ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoPureComp : ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoBase, IPPPWithCOMThermoPureComp, IPPPWithCOMThermoBase, IAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase, IPhysicalPropertyPackage, IDisposable
	{

		private double mPressureEstimate;

		public string FlashOptions
		{
			get
			{
				return mFlashOptions;
			}
			set
			{
				//IL_0039: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0043: Expected O, but got Unknown
				if (File.Exists(value) || checkFileNameFromFileList(mFlashFileList, value))
				{
					mFlashOptions = mThermoPath + getFileNameFromFileList(mFlashFileList, value);
					mPropertyManager = new COTHPropertyManagerClass();
					mPropertyManager.SetPropertyEnumerationRegistry((object)mPER);
					mIPM.SetPropertyManager((object)mPropertyManager);
					mFlashManager.Initialize((object)mPropertyManager);
					mThermoProperty = (IThermoProperty)(object)mPropertyManager;
					mPropertyManager.SetPropertyModel(mVapourPkg, (byte)1);
					mPropertyManager.SetPropertyModel(mLiquidPkg, (byte)0);
					mPropertyManager.SetPropModelCreatePropObjects(mFlashOptions, (byte)0);
					if (mCompoundCollection.NumberOfCompounds > 0)
					{
						mIPM.CreateIPs((object)mCompoundCollection, AppendToThermoPath("databases"));
						mIPM.LoadIPObjects((object)mCompoundCollection);
					}
					mPropertyManager.HasModified((NotifyChange)1);
					return;
				}
				throw new FileNotFoundException("Flash Definition Does Not Exist");
			}
		}

		public override void Initialise()
		{
			int num = -32767;
			double min = -32767.0;
			double max = -32767.0;
			base.Initialise();
			getFileList();
			if (mPackageFileList.Count > 0)
			{
				mPropertyPackage = mThermoPath + mPackageFileList[0];
			}
			else
			{
				LogMessage(MessageLogType.Warning, "No Property Packages");
			}
			if (mLiquidFileList.Count > 0)
			{
				mLiquidPkg = mThermoPath + mLiquidFileList[0];
				if (mVapourFileList.Count > 0)
				{
					mVapourPkg = mThermoPath + mVapourFileList[0];
					if (mFlashControl != null)
					{
						num = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("Pressure");
						mFlashControl.GetVariable(num, out mDefPressureEstimate, out min, out max, out mDefPressureStep, out mDefPressureTol);
						num = ((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("Temperature");
						mFlashControl.GetVariable(num, out mDefTemperatureEstimate, out min, out max, out mDefTemperatureStep, out mDefTemperatureTol);
						mPressureEstimate = mDefPressureEstimate;
						mPressureStep = mDefPressureStep;
						mPressureTol = mDefPressureTol;
						mTemperatureEstimate = mDefTemperatureEstimate;
						mTemperatureStep = mDefTemperatureStep;
						mTemperatureTol = mDefTemperatureTol;
						mUsePressureEstimate = false;
						mUseTemperatureEstimate = false;
						HysysPPName = null;
						nPanes = 0;
						return;
					}
					throw new InvalidOperationException("Flash Control Not Initialised");
				}
				LogMessage(MessageLogType.Fatal, "No Vapour File");
				throw new InvalidOperationException("No Vapour File");
			}
			LogMessage(MessageLogType.Fatal, "No Liquid File");
			throw new InvalidOperationException("No liquid file");
		}

		public override void InitFromCTF(string ctfPath, IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			string[] array = ctfPath.Split('\\');
			string text = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length - 1; i++)
			{
				text = text + array[i] + "\\";
			}
			string propMgrPath = "";
			string compCollPath = "";
			string extPkgPath = "";
			string extFlashPath = "";
			try
			{
				XmlTextReader xmlTextReader = new XmlTextReader(ctfPath);
				xmlTextReader.MoveToContent();
				while (!xmlTextReader.EOF)
				{
					if (xmlTextReader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
					{
						switch (xmlTextReader.Name)
						{
						case "PropertyManager":
							propMgrPath = text + xmlTextReader.GetAttribute("XMLFileName");
							break;
						case "CompoundCollection":
							compCollPath = text + xmlTextReader.GetAttribute("XMLFileName");
							break;
						case "IExtendedPkg":
							extPkgPath = text + xmlTextReader.GetAttribute("FileName");
							break;
						case "IExtendedFlsh":
							extFlashPath = text + xmlTextReader.GetAttribute("FileName");
							break;
						}
						xmlTextReader.Read();
					}
					else
					{
						xmlTextReader.Read();
					}
				}
				xmlTextReader = null;
				ccPopulated = false;
				initFromFiles(mHysysSecurity, propMgrPath, compCollPath, extPkgPath, extFlashPath);
				GetPropertyID();
				InitComponents(Stream);
				setCompoundInfo(Stream);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("InitFromCTF failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public override void SetPropertyPackageByEnum(PropertyPackage PP)
		{
			if (mPackageFileList.Count > 0)
			{
				string propertyPackageFileName = getPropertyPackageFileName(PP);
				SetPropertyPackageByName(propertyPackageFileName);
				return;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("No Property package files. ");
		}

		public void SetPropertyPackageByEnumForHysysClients(HysysPropertyPackage PP)
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			try
			{
				switch (PP)
				{
				case HysysPropertyPackage.Antoine:
					text = AppendToThermoPath("antoine_liquid.xml");
					text2 = AppendToThermoPath("antoine_vapor.xml");
					HysysPPName = null;
					nPanes = 0;
					break;
				case HysysPropertyPackage.BraunK10:
					text = AppendToThermoPath("braunk10_liquid.xml");
					text2 = AppendToThermoPath("braunk10_vapor.xml");
					HysysPPName = null;
					nPanes = 0;
					break;
				case HysysPropertyPackage.Chien_Ideal:
					text = AppendToThermoPath("cn_liquid.xml");
					text2 = AppendToThermoPath("ideal_vapor.xml");
					HysysPPName = null;
					nPanes = 0;
					break;
				case HysysPropertyPackage.Essotabular:
					text = AppendToThermoPath("essotabular_liquid.xml");
					text2 = AppendToThermoPath("essotabular_vapor.xml");
					HysysPPName = null;
					break;
				case HysysPropertyPackage.Extended:
					text = AppendToThermoPath("ext_liquid.xml");
					text2 = AppendToThermoPath("ext_vapor.xml");
					HysysPPName = null;
					nPanes = 0;
					break;
				case HysysPropertyPackage.HysysPR:
					text = AppendToThermoPath("hysyspr_liquid.xml");
					text2 = AppendToThermoPath("hysyspr_vapor.xml");
					HysysPPName = "PR";
					nPanes = 1;
					break;
				case HysysPropertyPackage.KD:
					text = AppendToThermoPath("kd_liquid.xml");
					text2 = AppendToThermoPath("kd_vapor.xml");
					HysysPPName = null;
					nPanes = 0;
					break;
				case HysysPropertyPackage.LeeKeslerPlocker:
					text = AppendToThermoPath("lkp_liquid.xml");
					text2 = AppendToThermoPath("lkp_vapor.xml");
					HysysPPName = "LKP";
					nPanes = 1;
					break;
				case HysysPropertyPackage.NRTL_Ideal:
					text = AppendToThermoPath("nrtl_liquid.xml");
					text2 = AppendToThermoPath("ideal_vapor.xml");
					HysysPPName = "NRTL";
					nPanes = 7;
					break;
				case HysysPropertyPackage.Margules_Ideal:
					text = AppendToThermoPath("margules_liquid.xml");
					text2 = AppendToThermoPath("ideal_vapor.xml");
					HysysPPName = "MARGULES";
					nPanes = 2;
					break;
				case HysysPropertyPackage.PengRobinson:
					text = AppendToThermoPath("pr_liquid.xml");
					text2 = AppendToThermoPath("pr_vapor.xml");
					HysysPPName = "PR";
					nPanes = 1;
					break;
				case HysysPropertyPackage.PRSV:
					text = AppendToThermoPath("prsv_liquid.xml");
					text2 = AppendToThermoPath("prsv_vapor.xml");
					HysysPPName = "PRSV";
					nPanes = 1;

					nPanes = 1;

				case HysysPropertyPackage.Uniquac_Ideal:
					text = AppendToThermoPath("uniquac_liquid.xml");
					text2 = AppendToThermoPath("ideal_vapor.xml");
					HysysPPName = "UNIQUAC";
					nPanes = 2;
					break;
				case HysysPropertyPackage.VanLaar_Ideal:

				default:
					throw new Exception("Property Package Not Supported");
				}
				SetPropertyPackage(text, text2);
				if (PP == HysysPropertyPackage.HysysPR || PP == HysysPropertyPackage.SRK)
				{
					SetModelOption(PhaseType.Liquid, PropertyType.Density, "Costald Molar Volume");
				}
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("SetPropertyPackageByEnumForHysysClients Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		public override void SetPropertyPackageByName(string name)
		{
			string Liquid = "";
			string Vapour = "";
			string Flash = "";
			GetPhaseXmlFiles(name, ref Liquid, ref Vapour, ref Flash);
			if (checkFileNameFromFileList(mFlashFileList, Flash))
			{
				mFlashOptions = mThermoPath + Flash;
				SetPropertyPackage(Liquid, Vapour);
				return;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException(Flash + " is not found in " + mThermoPath);
		}

		public void SetPropertyPackage(string LPP, string VPP)
		{
			if (File.Exists(LPP))
			{
				mLiquidPkg = LPP;
			}
			else
			{
				if (!checkFileNameFromFileList(mLiquidFileList, LPP))
				{
					throw new FileNotFoundException("Liquid Phase Property Package Definition Does Not Exist");
				}
				mLiquidPkg = mThermoPath + getFileNameFromFileList(mLiquidFileList, LPP);
			}
			if (File.Exists(VPP))
			{
				mVapourPkg = VPP;
			}
			else
			{
				if (!checkFileNameFromFileList(mVapourFileList, VPP))
				{
					throw new FileNotFoundException("Vapour Phase Property Package Definition Does Not Exist");
				}
				mVapourPkg = mThermoPath + getFileNameFromFileList(mVapourFileList, VPP);
			}
			PropertyPackageSetup();
			mIsPPSet = true;
		}

		[ComVisible(false)]
		public ArrayList GetModelOption(PhaseType phsType, PropertyType propType)
		{
			ArrayList classList = new ArrayList();
			return GetModelOption(phsType, propType, classList);
		}

		public void SetModelOption(PhaseType phsType, PropertyType propType, string optionName)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
			string text = "";
			try
			{
				ArrayList modelOption = GetModelOption(phsType, propType, arrayList);
				if (modelOption.Count < 2)
				{
					return;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < modelOption.Count; i++)
				{
					if ((string)modelOption[i] == optionName)
					{
						text = (string)arrayList[i];
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					int propertyIDByType = getPropertyIDByType(propType);
					IThermoPropertyManager1 val = (IThermoPropertyManager1)(object)mPropertyManager;
					if (phsType == PhaseType.Liquid)
					{
						val.SetDefaultPropertyMethodCreateObject(propertyIDByType, 4, text, (object)mCompoundCollection, mDatabasePath, (object)mIPM);
					}
					else
					{
						val.SetDefaultPropertyMethodCreateObject(propertyIDByType, 3, text, (object)mCompoundCollection, mDatabasePath, (object)mIPM);
					}
					mPropertyManager.HasModified((NotifyChange)1);
					return;
				}
				throw new Exception("The option name specified is not available.");
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("SetModelOption failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public void PassBinaryInteractionParameters(double[,] bipArray)
		{
			//IL_0048: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			COTHInteractionParametersClass val = null;
			int num = 0;
			try
			{
				if (HysysPPName != null && nPanes == 1)
				{
					num = mComponents.Length;
					if (num != bipArray.GetLength(0) || num != bipArray.GetLength(1))
					{
						throw new ArgumentException("Array length is mismatched", "bipArray");
					}
					val = new COTHInteractionParametersClass();
					val.ModelName = HysysPPName;
					val.NumberOfPanes = nPanes;
					val.Dimension = num;
					for (int i = 0; i < nPanes; i++)
					{
						for (int j = 0; j < num; j++)
						{
							for (int k = 0; k < num; k++)
							{
								val[i, j, k] = bipArray[j, k];
							}
						}
					}
					mCompoundCollection.InteractionParameters = val;
					return;
				}
				throw new Exception("Unsupported Property Package model for this method. ");
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("PassBinaryInteractionParameters failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		[ComVisible(false)]
		public void NEL40Utility(IFlowStream Stream, FlashType FT)
		{
			GetData(Stream, FT, out var data, out var data2);
			NEL40Utility(Stream, FT, data, data2);
		}

		[ComVisible(false)]
		public void NEL40Utility(IFlowStream Stream, FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			Check(Stream);
			IPhase phaseByID = Stream.GetPhaseByID(0);
			SetComposition(phaseByID);
			DoFlash(FT, data1, data2);
			UpdateStream(Stream, IsFullSet: false);
		}

		private void initFromFiles(object hysysSecurity, string propMgrPath, string compCollPath, string extPkgPath, string extFlashPath)
		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Expected O, but got Unknown
			XMLParser val = (XMLParser)new HyprotechStreamingXMLParserClass();
			ICOMExtensionHandler val2 = (ICOMExtensionHandler)(object)mPropertyManager;
			try
			{
				nullify();
				InitComThermoObjects();
				if (propMgrPath != "")
				{
					COTHPropertyManagerClass obj = mPropertyManager;
					XMLParserClient val3 = (XMLParserClient)(object)((obj is XMLParserClient) ? obj : null);
					val.ReadFile(propMgrPath, val3, false, false);
					val3 = null;
				}
				ccPopulated = false;
				if (compCollPath != "")
				{
					COTHCompoundCollectionClass obj2 = mCompoundCollection;
					XMLParserClient val3 = (XMLParserClient)(object)((obj2 is XMLParserClient) ? obj2 : null);
					val.ReadFile(compCollPath, val3, false, false);
					val3 = null;
					ccPopulated = true;
				}
				if (extPkgPath != "")
				{
					bool flag = false;
					string text;
					using (StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(extPkgPath))
					{
						text = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
					}
					val2.PkgRecall(text, ref flag);
				}
				if (extFlashPath != "")
				{
					string text2;
					using (StreamReader streamReader2 = new StreamReader(extFlashPath))
					{
						text2 = streamReader2.ReadToEnd();
					}
					val2.FlashRecall(text2);
				}
				((ICOTHHysysSecurity)mPropertyManager).PutApplicationInterface(hysysSecurity);
				byte b = 0;
				byte b2 = 0;
				val2.UseExtendedPropPkg(ref b);
				val2.UseExtendedFlash(ref b2);
				bool num = b == 1;
				bool flag2 = b2 == 1;
				if (num)
				{
					int num2 = 0;
					val2.IsPkgSetup(ref num2);
					if (num2 != 0)
					{
						throw new Exception("Property package not properly set up");
					}
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					object obj3 = new object();
					val2.GetExtendedFlash(ref obj3);
					object obj4 = ((obj3 is IExtendedFlash) ? obj3 : null);
					int num3 = 0;
					((IExtendedFlash)obj4).SetupFlash(0, (object)null, ref num3);
					if (num3 != 0)
					{
						throw new Exception("Error in setting up flash.");
					}
					int num4 = 0;
					val2.IsFlashSetup(ref num4);
					if (num4 != 0)
					{
						throw new Exception("Flash not properly set up");
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				throw ex;
			}
		}

		private void GetPhaseXmlFiles(string PPName, ref string Liquid, ref string Vapour, ref string Flash)
		{
			string text = "";
			try
			{
				if (checkFileNameFromFileList(mPackageFileList, PPName))
				{
					mPropertyPackage = mThermoPath + getFileNameFromFileList(mPackageFileList, PPName);
					FileStream fileStream = new FileStream(mPropertyPackage, FileMode.Open);
					XmlTextReader xmlTextReader = new XmlTextReader(fileStream);
					while (xmlTextReader.Read())
					{
						if (xmlTextReader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
						{
							continue;
						}
						for (int i = 0; i < xmlTextReader.AttributeCount; i++)
						{
							text = xmlTextReader.GetAttribute(i);
							if (text.EndsWith("liquid.xml"))
							{
								Liquid = text;
							}
							else if (text.EndsWith("vapor.xml"))
							{
								Vapour = text;
							}
							else if (text.EndsWith("flash.xml"))
							{
								Flash = text;
							}
						}
					}
					xmlTextReader.Close();
					fileStream.Close();
					return;
				}
				throw new Exception("The property package file does not exist." + " Property package files can be copied or installed by installing a HTFS product.");
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("GetPhaseXmlFiles Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void SetupPressureSearch(double Pstart, double Pstep, double Ptol)
		{
			double defaultValue = -32767.0;
			double min = -32767.0;
			double max = -32767.0;
			double maxInc = -32767.0;
			double tol = -32767.0;
			try
			{
				((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("Pressure");
				mFlashControl.GetVariable(mID_P, out defaultValue, out min, out max, out maxInc, out tol);
				mFlashControl.PutVariable(mID_P, Pstart, min, max, Pstep, Ptol);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("SetupPressureSearch Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void SetupTemperatureSearch(double Tstart, double Tstep, double Ttol)
		{
			double defaultValue = -32767.0;
			double min = -32767.0;
			double max = -32767.0;
			double maxInc = -32767.0;
			double tol = -32767.0;
			try
			{
				((IPropertyEnumerationRegistry)mPER).GetPropertyIDByName("Temperature");
				mFlashControl.GetVariable(mID_T, out defaultValue, out min, out max, out maxInc, out tol);
				mFlashControl.PutVariable(mID_T, Tstart, min, max, Tstep, Ttol);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("SetupTemperatureSearch Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void getFileList()
		{
			mPackageFileList = new ArrayList();
			mFlashFileList = new ArrayList();
			mLiquidFileList = new ArrayList();
			mVapourFileList = new ArrayList();
			DirectoryInfo directoryInfo = new DirectoryInfo(mThermoPath);
			if (!directoryInfo.Exists)
			{
				throw new DirectoryNotFoundException("Folder not found: " + mThermoPath);
			}
			FileInfo[] files = directoryInfo.GetFiles();
			for (int i = 0; i < files.Length; i++)
			{
				string text = files[i].Name.ToString();
				if (text.EndsWith("pkg.xml"))
				{
					mPackageFileList.Add(text);
				}
				else if (text.EndsWith("flash.xml"))
				{
					mFlashFileList.Add(text);
				}
				else if (text.EndsWith("liquid.xml"))
				{
					mLiquidFileList.Add(text);
				}
				else if (text.EndsWith("vapor.xml") || text.EndsWith("vapour.xml"))
				{
					mVapourFileList.Add(text);
				}
			}
			getHTFSpkgFile();
		}

		private void getHTFSpkgFile()
		{
			DirectoryInfo directoryInfo = new DirectoryInfo(mThermoPath + "HTFSPkgs");
			if (!directoryInfo.Exists)
			{
				return;
			}
			FileInfo[] files = directoryInfo.GetFiles();
			for (int i = 0; i < files.Length; i++)
			{
				string text = files[i].Name.ToString();
				if (text.EndsWith("pkg.xml"))
				{
					mPackageFileList.Add("HTFSPkgs\\" + text);
				}
			}
		}

		private bool checkFileNameFromFileList(ArrayList list, string fileName)
		{
			bool result = false;
			string[] array = fileName.Split('\\');
			string text = array[array.Length - 1];
			for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
			{
				if (list[i].ToString() == text)
				{
					result = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string getFileNameFromFileList(ArrayList list, string fileName)
		{
			string result = null;
			string[] array = fileName.Split('\\');
			string text = array[array.Length - 1];
			for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
			{
				if (text == list[i].ToString())
				{
					result = list[i].ToString();
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string getPropertyPackageFileName(PropertyPackage PP)
		{
			try
			{
				return PP switch
				{
					PropertyPackage.Ideal_Ideal => "HTFSidealpkg.xml", 
					PropertyPackage.Ideal_Ideal_LK => "HTFSidealLKpkg.xml", 
					PropertyPackage.NRTL_Ideal => "HTFShnrtl-idealpkg.xml", 
					PropertyPackage.NRTL_Ideal_LK => "HTFShnrtl-idealLKpkg.xml", 
					PropertyPackage.PengRobinson => "HTFSprpkg.xml", 
					PropertyPackage.PengRobinson_LK => "HTFSprLKpkg.xml", 
					PropertyPackage.PengRobinsonStryjekVera => "HTFSprsvpkg.xml", 
					PropertyPackage.PengRobinsonStryjekVera_LK => "HTFSprsvLKpkg.xml", 
					PropertyPackage.SoaveRedlichKwong => "HTFSsrkpkg.xml", 
					PropertyPackage.SoaveRedlichKwong_LK => "HTFSsrkLKpkg.xml", 
					_ => throw new Exception("Property Package Not Supported"), 
				};
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("get property package name Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		private ArrayList GetModelOption(PhaseType phsType, PropertyType propType, ArrayList classList)
		{
			if (phsType == PhaseType.Liquid || phsType == PhaseType.Vapour)
			{
				ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList();
				string text = "";
				text = ((phsType != PhaseType.Liquid) ? mVapourPkg : mLiquidPkg);
				FileStream fileStream = new FileStream(text, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);
				XmlTextReader xmlTextReader = new XmlTextReader(fileStream);
				string propertyNameByType = getPropertyNameByType(propType);
				string text2 = "";
				while (xmlTextReader.Read())
				{
					string value = "";
					if (xmlTextReader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
					{
						continue;
					}
					for (int i = 0; i < xmlTextReader.AttributeCount; i++)
					{
						text2 = xmlTextReader.GetAttribute(i);
						if (text2.EndsWith("Class"))
						{
							value = text2;
						}
						if (text2 != propertyNameByType && text2 != "Solid " + propertyNameByType && text2.EndsWith(propertyNameByType))
						{
							classList.Add(value);
							arrayList.Add(text2);
						}
					}
				}
				xmlTextReader.Close();
				fileStream.Close();
				return arrayList;
			}
			throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("phsType", "Phase type must be either Liquid or Vapour.");
		}

		protected override void DoFlash(FlashType FT, double data1, double data2)
		{
			if (mUsePressureEstimate)
			{
				SetupPressureSearch(mPressureEstimate, mPressureStep, mPressureTol);
			}
			if (mUseTemperatureEstimate)
			{
				SetupTemperatureSearch(mTemperatureEstimate, mTemperatureStep, mTemperatureTol);
			}
			base.DoFlash(FT, data1, data2);
			if (mUsePressureEstimate)
			{
				SetupPressureSearch(mDefPressureEstimate, mDefPressureStep, mDefPressureTol);
			}
			if (mUseTemperatureEstimate)
			{
				SetupTemperatureSearch(mDefTemperatureEstimate, mDefTemperatureStep, mDefTemperatureTol);
			}
		}
	}

	public class ImpIPPPWithMultiflashThroughCOMThermo : ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoBase, IPPPWithMultiflashThroughCOMThermo, IPPPWithCOMThermoBase, IAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase, IPhysicalPropertyPackage, IDisposable
	{
		private int hydraulicsCookie;

		private string version;

		private MultiflashThermodynamicPropertyModelEnum thermoModel;

		private MultiflashTransportPropertyModelEnum transportModel;

		private bool isInitialised;

		{
			set
			{
				version = value;
			}
		}

			{
				return thermoModel;
			}
			set

				thermoModel = value;
			}
		}

		public override void InitFromCTF(string ctfPath, IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			string[] array = ctfPath.Split('\\');
			string text = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length - 1; i++)
			{
				text = text + array[i] + "\\";
			}
			string propMgrPath = "";
			string compCollPath = "";
			string extPkgPath = "";
			string extFlashPath = "";
			try
			{
				if (!isInitialised)
				{
					setDefault();
				}
				XmlTextReader xmlTextReader = new XmlTextReader(ctfPath);
				xmlTextReader.MoveToContent();
				while (!xmlTextReader.EOF)
				{
					if (xmlTextReader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
					{
						switch (xmlTextReader.Name)
						{
						case "PropertyManager":
							propMgrPath = text + xmlTextReader.GetAttribute("XMLFileName");
							break;
						case "CompoundCollection":
							compCollPath = text + xmlTextReader.GetAttribute("XMLFileName");
							break;
						case "IExtendedPkg":
							extPkgPath = text + xmlTextReader.GetAttribute("FileName");
							break;
						case "IExtendedFlsh":
							extFlashPath = text + xmlTextReader.GetAttribute("FileName");
							break;
						}
						xmlTextReader.Read();
					}
					else
					{
						xmlTextReader.Read();
					}
				}
				xmlTextReader = null;
				ccPopulated = false;
				initFromFiles(mHysysSecurity, propMgrPath, compCollPath, extPkgPath, extFlashPath);
				GetPropertyID();
				InitComponents(Stream);
				setCompoundInfo(Stream);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("InitFromCTF failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public void Copy(object hysysSecurity, object propertyManager, object compoundCollection, IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			string text = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), Path.GetRandomFileName());
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = "";

			{

				streamWriter.Close();

				{
					IXMLStore val2 = (IXMLStore)((compoundCollection is IXMLStore) ? compoundCollection : null);
					text2 = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), Path.GetRandomFileName());
					StreamWriter streamWriter2 = new StreamWriter(text2, append: false);
					streamWriter2.Write(val2.StoreAsXML());
					streamWriter2.Flush();
					streamWriter2.Close();
				}
				ICOMExtensionHandler val3 = (ICOMExtensionHandler)((propertyManager is ICOMExtensionHandler) ? propertyManager : null);
				byte b = 0;
				byte b2 = 0;
				val3.UseExtendedPropPkg(ref b);
				val3.UseExtendedFlash(ref b2);
				int num = ((b != 0) ? 1 : 0);
				bool flag = ((b2 != 0) ? true : false);
				if (num != 0)
				{
					text3 = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), Path.GetRandomFileName());
					object obj = new object();

					object obj2 = ((obj is IExtendedPropPackage) ? obj : null);
					string value = "";

					StreamWriter streamWriter3 = new StreamWriter(text3, append: false);
					streamWriter3.Write(value);
					streamWriter3.Flush();
					streamWriter3.Close();

				{
					text4 = Path.Combine(Path.GetTempPath(), Path.GetRandomFileName());

				ccPopulated = false;

			}
		}

		public override void InitFromStream(IFlowStream Stream)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!isInitialised)
				{
					setDefault();
				}
				initMultiflash(Stream);
				mIsPPSet = true;
				base.InitFromStream(Stream);
				GetPropertyID();
				setCompoundInfo(Stream);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Initialisation of Multiflash failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		}

		}

		{
			//IL_0000: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0006: Expected O, but got Unknown

				}

				{

					val.ReadFile(compCollPath, val3, false, false);

				}
				if (extPkgPath != "")
				{
					bool flag = false;
					string text;

					{
						text = streamReader.ReadToEnd();
					}
					val2.PkgRecall(text, ref flag);
				}
				if (extFlashPath != "")
				{
					string text2;
					using (StreamReader streamReader2 = new StreamReader(extFlashPath))
					{
						text2 = streamReader2.ReadToEnd();
					}
					val2.FlashRecall(text2);
				}
				((ICOTHHysysSecurity)mPropertyManager).PutApplicationInterface(hysysSecurity);
				byte b = 0;
				byte b2 = 0;
				val2.UseExtendedPropPkg(ref b);
				val2.UseExtendedFlash(ref b2);
				bool num = b == 1;
				bool flag2 = b2 == 1;
				if (num)
				{
					int num2 = 0;
					val2.IsPkgSetup(ref num2);
					if (num2 != 0)
					{
						throw new Exception("Property package not properly set up");
					}
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					object obj3 = new object();
					val2.GetExtendedFlash(ref obj3);
					object obj4 = ((obj3 is IExtendedFlash) ? obj3 : null);
					int num3 = 0;
					((IExtendedFlash)obj4).SetupFlash(0, (object)null, ref num3);
					if (num3 != 0)
					{
						throw new Exception("Error in setting up flash.");
					}
					int num4 = 0;
					val2.IsFlashSetup(ref num4);
					if (num4 != 0)
					{
						throw new Exception("Flash not properly set up");
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)

			}
		}

		private string mfProps(IFlowStream myStream)
		{
			//IL_0042: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0048: Expected O, but got Unknown
			string text = "";
			try
			{
				text += getMFLFileFirstPart();
				string text2 = "puredata INFODATA ; components ";
				IPhase phaseByID = myStream.GetPhaseByID(0);
				COTHCompound val = null;
				int num = 0;
				while (num < phaseByID.NumberOfCompounds)
				{
					num++;
					if (!phaseByID.GetComponent(num - 1).IsHypothetical)
					{
						val = (COTHCompound)new COTHCompoundClass();
						((ICompoundInitFromHypDB)((val is ICompoundInitFromHypDB) ? val : null)).Init(mDatabasePath, phaseByID.GetComponent(num - 1).ID);
						text2 = text2 + num + " " + ((ICompoundID)((val is ICompoundID) ? val : null)).CASN + " ";
						text = text + text2 + "\r\r\n";
						text2 = " ; puredata INFODATA ; components ";
						continue;
					}
					double value = phaseByID.GetComponent(num - 1).MolecularWeight.Value;
					double value2 = phaseByID.GetComponent(num - 1).CriticalTemperature.Value;
					double num2 = phaseByID.GetComponent(num - 1).CriticalPressure.Value * 1000.0;
					double value3 = phaseByID.GetComponent(num - 1).Acentricity.Value;
					double value4 = phaseByID.GetComponent(num - 1).NormalBoilingPoint.Value;
					double num3 = phaseByID.GetComponent(num - 1).LiquidDensity.Value / 1000.0;
					text += "; chardata INFOCHAR TBSOEREIDE ;\r\r\n";
					text = text + "petrofracs " + num + " CUT" + num + " normal data  MOLECULARWEIGHT   " + value + " TCRIT   " + value2 + " PCRIT " + num2 + " ACENTRICFACTOR  " + value3 + " TBOIL   " + value4 + " SPGRAVITY  " + num3 + " ; ;\r\r\n";
				}
				if (!phaseByID.GetComponent(phaseByID.NumberOfCompounds - 1).IsHypothetical)
				{
					text += "; chardata INFOCHAR TBSOEREIDE ;\r\r\n";
				}
				text += "bipset PRABIP 1 constant ;\r\r\n";
				text += "bipdata INFOBIPS OILANDGAS4 ;\r\r\n";
				text += "model MPRA PRA PSAT LDEN VDW PRABIP;\r\r\n";
				text += "model MLBCMPRA LBC LFIT MPRA;\r\r\n";
				text += "model MCLSMPRA CLS MPRA;\r\r\n";
				text += "model MMCSMPRA MCS MPRA;\r\r\n";
				text += "pd GAS gas MPRA MPRA MPRA MLBCMPRA MCLSMPRA;\r\r\n";
				text += "pd LIQUID1 liquid MPRA MPRA MPRA MLBCMPRA MCLSMPRA MMCSMPRA;\r\r\n";
				text += "key LIQUID1 not 007732-18-5;\r\r\n";
				text += "pd WATER liquid MPRA MPRA MPRA MLBCMPRA MCLSMPRA MMCSMPRA;\r\r\n";
				text += "key WATER 007732-18-5;\r\r\n";
				text += "units temperature K pressure Pa enthalpy J/mol entropy J/mol/K volume m3/mol\r\r\n";
				text += "amounts mole viscosity Pas thcond W/m/K;\r\r\n";
				text += "set physprops 2T;\r\r\n";
				return text + " \r\r\n\r\n";
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("mfProps failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		private string mfFlash()
		{
			return "Save Multiflash Flash\r\r\n";
		}

		private string mfNames(int hypCompoundIndex)
		{
			string[] array = new string[26]
			{
				null, "METHANE", "ETHANE", "PROPANE", "ISOBUTANE", "BUTANE", "ISOPENTANE", "PENTANE", "HEXANE", "HEPTANE",
				"OCTANE", "NONANE", "DECANE", "NITROGEN", "CO2", "H2S", null, null, null, "WATER",
				null, null, null, null, null, null
			};
			if (array[hypCompoundIndex] == null || array[hypCompoundIndex] == "")
			{
				throw new Exception("Compound " + hypCompoundIndex + " not found in list of known Multiflash compound identifiers.");
			}
			return array[hypCompoundIndex];
		}

		private string getMFLFileFirstPart()
		{
			string text = "";
			try
			{
				DateTime dateTime = default(DateTime);
				dateTime = DateTime.Now;
				string text2 = "# Multiflash version: " + version + "   #\r\r\n";
				text += text2;
				string text3 = "# Date: " + dateTime.ToLongDateString() + "   Time: " + dateTime.ToLongTimeString() + " #\r\r\n";
				text += text3;
				text += "remove all;\r\r\n";
				text += "units temperature K pressure Pa enthalpy J/mol entropy J/mol/K volume m3/mol\r\r\n";
				return text + "amounts mol viscosity Pas thcond W/m/K;\r\r\n";
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Creat MFL file string first part failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		private string getMFLFileModelPart()
		{
			string text = "";
			try
			{
				return text + "\r\r\n";
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Creat MFL file string first part failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		private string getMFLFileLastPart()
		{
			string text = "";
			string text2 = "";
			string text3 = "";
			try
			{
				switch (thermoModel)
				{
				case MultiflashThermodynamicPropertyModelEnum.RKS:
					text2 = "RKS";
					break;
				case MultiflashThermodynamicPropertyModelEnum.RKSAPI:
					text2 = "API";
					break;
				case MultiflashThermodynamicPropertyModelEnum.RKSAdvanced:
					text2 = "RKSA";
					break;
				case MultiflashThermodynamicPropertyModelEnum.RKSAdvancedInfochem:
					text2 = "RKSA";
					break;
				case MultiflashThermodynamicPropertyModelEnum.PR:
					text2 = "PR";
					break;
				case MultiflashThermodynamicPropertyModelEnum.Default:
				case MultiflashThermodynamicPropertyModelEnum.PRAdvanced:
					text2 = "PRA";
					break;
				case MultiflashThermodynamicPropertyModelEnum.LKP:
					text2 = "LPK";
					break;
				case MultiflashThermodynamicPropertyModelEnum.BWRS:
					text2 = "BWRS";
					break;
				case MultiflashThermodynamicPropertyModelEnum.PSRK:
					text2 = "PSRK";
					break;
				case MultiflashThermodynamicPropertyModelEnum.AssociationCPAInfochem:
					text2 = "ASSOC";
					break;
				case MultiflashThermodynamicPropertyModelEnum.LCVM:
					text2 = "LCVM";
					break;
				default:
					throw new Exception("Undefined package model");
				}
				text += "units temperature K pressure Pa enthalpy J/mol entropy J/mol/K volume m3/mol\r\r\n";
				text += "amounts mole viscosity Pas thcond W/m/K;\r\r\n";
				text += "set physprops 1;\r\r\n";
				text += " \r\r\n\r\n";
				text += text2;
				return text + text3;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Creat MFL file string first part failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		private void setDefault()
		{
			version = "3.3.40   October 2003";
			thermoModel = MultiflashThermodynamicPropertyModelEnum.Default;
			transportModel = MultiflashTransportPropertyModelEnum.Default;
			isInitialised = true;
		}

		public override void Dispose()
		{
			try
			{
				if (hydraulicsCookie != 0 && server != null)
				{
					server.CheckIn(hydraulicsCookie);
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			base.Dispose();
		}
	}

			}
			set
			{
				if (lclpppType < PropertyPackage.Ideal_Ideal && value >= PropertyPackage.Ideal_Ideal && value <= PropertyPackage.SoaveRedlichKwong_LK)
				{
					lclpppType = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public string CTFPath
		{
			get

				return ctfPath;
			}
			set

				ctfPath = value;
			}
		}

		public int Xlength => lclxData.Length();

					packageBase = value;
					return;
				}
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("value", "Unsupported package");
			}
		}
