		public ImpIPPPWithTableInterpolator()
		{
			lclExtendMode = false;
			lclInitialised = false;
			myHysysSecurity = null;
		}

		public ImpIPPPWithTableInterpolator(IMessageLog thisLog, string CTFFilePath, InterpolatorOptions typeOfInterpolator, PropertyType typeOfxProperty, double[] xValue, double xGrid, PropertyType typeOfyProperty, double[] yValue, double yGrid, IFlowStream workStream)
			: this()
		{
			lclInitialised = false;
			lclMessageLog = thisLog;
			Initialise(CTFFilePath, typeOfInterpolator, typeOfxProperty, xValue, xGrid, typeOfyProperty, yValue, yGrid, workStream);
			GenerateTable();
		}

		public ImpIPPPWithTableInterpolator(IMessageLog thisLog, PropertyPackage typeOfPPP, InterpolatorOptions typeOfInterpolator, PropertyType typeOfxProperty, double[] xValue, double xGrid, PropertyType typeOfyProperty, double[] yValue, double yGrid, IFlowStream workStream)
			: this()
		{
			lclInitialised = false;
			lclMessageLog = thisLog;
			Initialise(typeOfPPP, typeOfInterpolator, typeOfxProperty, xValue, xGrid, typeOfyProperty, yValue, yGrid, workStream);
			GenerateTable();
		}

		public ImpIPPPWithTableInterpolator(IMessageLog thisLog, string fName, string fWaterName)
			: this()
		{
			lclInitialised = false;
			lclMessageLog = thisLog;
			ReadADMtable(fName, fWaterName);
		}

		public void Initialise(object HysysSecurity, InterpolatorOptions typeOfInterpolator, PropertyType typeOfxProperty, double[] xValue, double xGrid, PropertyType typeOfyProperty, double[] yValue, double yGrid, IFlowStream workStream)
		{
			try
			{
				myHysysSecurity = HysysSecurity;
				if (packageBase == IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.Unknown)
				{
					packageBase = IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.Default;
				}
				StreamBasis = workStream.Clone() as IFlowStream;
				setIPPP();
				Initialise((PropertyPackage)(-1), typeOfInterpolator, typeOfxProperty, xValue, xGrid, typeOfyProperty, yValue, yGrid, workStream);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Initialise with HSI interface failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		public void Initialise(string CTFFilePath, InterpolatorOptions typeOfInterpolator, PropertyType typeOfxProperty, double[] xValue, double xGrid, PropertyType typeOfyProperty, double[] yValue, double yGrid, IFlowStream workStream)
		{
			if (File.Exists(CTFFilePath) && pIPPP == null)
			{
				ctfPath = CTFFilePath;
				StreamBasis = workStream.Clone() as IFlowStream;
				if (packageBase == IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.Unknown)
				{
					packageBase = IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.Default;
				}
				setIPPP();
				Initialise((PropertyPackage)(-1), typeOfInterpolator, typeOfxProperty, xValue, xGrid, typeOfyProperty, yValue, yGrid, workStream);
				return;
			}
			throw new FileNotFoundException("Specified *.ctf file does not exist");
		}

		public void Initialise(PropertyPackage typeOfPPP, InterpolatorOptions typeOfInterpolator, PropertyType typeOfxProperty, double[] xValue, double xGrid, PropertyType typeOfyProperty, double[] yValue, double yGrid, IFlowStream workStream)
		{
			lclStreamTemplate = workStream.Clone() as IFlowStream;
			if (lclxData == null)
			{
				switch ((int)typeOfxProperty + (int)typeOfyProperty)
				{
				case 3:
					if (typeOfxProperty == PropertyType.Pressure)
					{
						lclFlashType = FlashType.PTFlash;
						break;
					}
					goto default;
				case 10:
					if (typeOfxProperty == PropertyType.Pressure)
					{
						lclFlashType = FlashType.PSFlash;
						break;
					}
					goto default;
				case 5:
					if (typeOfxProperty == PropertyType.Pressure)
					{
						lclFlashType = FlashType.PHFlash;
						break;
					}
					goto default;
				case 4:
					if (typeOfxProperty == PropertyType.Temperature)
					{
						lclFlashType = FlashType.THFlash;
						break;
					}
					goto default;
				case 9:
					if (typeOfxProperty == PropertyType.Temperature)
					{
						lclFlashType = FlashType.TSFlash;
						break;
					}
					goto default;
				default:
					lclInitialised = false;
					throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("The independant variables requested are not permitted");
				}
				lclInterpolatorType = typeOfInterpolator;
				lclxData = new IndependantVar(typeOfxProperty, xValue);
				lclyData = new IndependantVar(typeOfyProperty, yValue);
				lclFlowStreams = new StreamCollection();
				lclxy2item = new ConvertXY2Item(xValue.Length, yValue.Length);
				lclpppType = typeOfPPP;
				lclxGrid = xGrid;
				lclyGrid = yGrid;
				flashLevel = FlashCalculationLevelEnum.FullSet;
				if (packageBase == IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.Unknown)
				{
					packageBase = IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.Default;
				}
				setIPPP();
			}
			lclInitialised = true;
		}

		public void ResetComposition(IFlowStream NewStreamBasis)
		{
			lclInitialised = true;
			lclFlowStreams.Clear();
			lclxy2item.Clear();
			lclStreamTemplate = NewStreamBasis.Clone() as IFlowStream;
			Mode = false;
			GenerateTable();
		}

		public void ExtendData(double xValue, double yValue)
		{
			int num = -1;
			int num2 = -1;
			int num3 = -1;
			int num4 = -1;
			if (lclInitialised)
			{
				num3 = lclxData.Find(xValue);
				num4 = lclyData.Find(yValue);
				if (num3 < 0 || num4 < 0)
				{
					double num5 = (double)(int)xValue / lclxGrid * lclxGrid;
					double num6 = num5 + lclxGrid;
					if (num3 == -3)
					{
						num6 = lclxData[0] - (double)(int)((lclxData[0] - xValue) / lclxGrid) * lclxGrid;
						num5 = num6 - lclxGrid;
						num = lclxData.Insert(num6, -3);
						lclxy2item.InsertX(num);
						num = lclxData.Insert(num5, -3);
						lclxy2item.InsertX(num);
					}
					if (num3 == -2)
					{
						num5 = lclxData[lclxData.Length() - 1] + (double)(int)((xValue - lclxData[lclxData.Length() - 1]) / lclxGrid) * lclxGrid;
						num6 = num5 + lclxGrid;
						num = lclxData.Insert(num5, -2);
						lclxy2item.InsertX(num);
						num = lclxData.Insert(num6, -2);
						lclxy2item.InsertX(num);
						num--;
					}
					if (num3 >= -1)
					{
						num = lclxData.LoBracket;
					}
					double num7 = (double)(int)(yValue / lclyGrid) * lclyGrid;
					double num8 = num7 + lclyGrid;
					if (num4 == -3)
					{
						num8 = lclyData[0] - (double)(int)((lclyData[0] - yValue) / lclyGrid) * lclyGrid;
						num7 = num8 - lclyGrid;
						num2 = lclyData.Insert(num8, -3);
						lclxy2item.InsertY(num2);
						num2 = lclyData.Insert(num7, -3);
						lclxy2item.InsertY(num2);
					}
					if (num4 == -2)
					{
						num7 = lclyData[lclyData.Length() - 1] + (double)(int)((yValue - lclyData[lclyData.Length() - 1]) / lclyGrid) * lclyGrid;
						num8 = num7 + lclyGrid;
						num2 = lclyData.Insert(num7, -2);
						lclxy2item.InsertY(num2);
						num2 = lclyData.Insert(num8, -2);
						lclxy2item.InsertY(num2);
						num2--;
					}
					if (num4 >= -1)
					{
						num2 = lclyData.LoBracket;
					}
					if (num != -1 || num2 != -1)
					{
						if (num == -1)
						{
							num3 = lclxData.Find(num6);
							if (num3 < -1)
							{
								num3 = lclxData.Insert(num6, num3);
								lclxy2item.InsertX(num3);
							}
							num = lclxData.LoBracket;
						}
						if (num2 == -1)
						{
							num4 = lclyData.Find(num8);
							if (num4 < -1)
							{
								num4 = lclyData.Insert(num8, num4);
								lclxy2item.InsertY(num4);
							}
							num2 = lclyData.LoBracket;
						}
						new Phase();
						try
						{
							setIPPP();
							pIPPP.FlashStream(lclStreamTemplate, lclFlashType, xValue, yValue);
						}
						catch (Exception ex)
						{
							throw ex;
						}
						for (int i = num2; i <= num2 + 1; i++)
						{
							for (int j = num; j <= num + 1; j++)
							{
								if (GetStream(lclxData.GetValue(j), lclyData.GetValue(i)) == null)
								{
									IFlowStream flowStream = new FlowStream();
									flowStream = lclStreamTemplate.Clone() as IFlowStream;
									pIPPP.FlashStream(flowStream, lclFlashType, lclxData.GetValue(j), lclyData.GetValue(i));
									lclFlowStreams.Add(flowStream);
									lclxy2item[j, i] = lclFlowStreams.Count - 1;
								}
							}
						}
						return;
					}
				}
				if (lclMessageLog != null)
				{
					lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Failed to extend property table range (X = " + xValue + ", Y = " + yValue + " )");
				}
				return;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Interpolator must be initialised before continuing");
		}

		public void ExtendXdata(double xValue)
		{
			int num = -1;
			if (lclInitialised)
			{
				num = lclxData.Find(xValue);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					new Phase();
					try
					{
						setIPPP();
						pIPPP.FlashStream(lclStreamTemplate, lclFlashType, xValue, lclyData.GetValue(0));
					}
					catch (Exception ex)
					{
						throw ex;
					}
					int num2 = lclyData.Length();
					num = lclxData.Insert(xValue, num);
					lclxy2item.InsertX(num);
					for (int i = 0; i < num2; i++)
					{
						IFlowStream flowStream = new FlowStream();
						flowStream = lclStreamTemplate.Clone() as IFlowStream;
						pIPPP.FlashStream(flowStream, lclFlashType, lclxData.GetValue(num), lclyData.GetValue(i));
						checkNumberOfPhases(flowStream);
						lclFlowStreams.Add(flowStream);
						lclxy2item[num, i] = lclFlowStreams.Count - 1;
					}
				}
				else if (lclMessageLog != null)
				{
					lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Failed to extend property table range (X = " + xValue + " )");
				}
				return;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Interpolator must be initialised before continuing");
		}

		public void ExtendYdata(double yValue)
		{
			int num = -1;
			if (lclInitialised)
			{
				num = lclyData.Find(yValue);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					new Phase();
					try
					{
						setIPPP();
						pIPPP.FlashStream(lclStreamTemplate, lclFlashType, lclxData.GetValue(0), yValue);
					}
					catch (Exception ex)
					{
						throw ex;
					}
					int num2 = lclxData.Length();
					num = lclyData.Insert(yValue, num);
					lclxy2item.InsertY(num);
					for (int i = 0; i < num2; i++)
					{
						IFlowStream flowStream = new FlowStream();
						flowStream = lclStreamTemplate.Clone() as IFlowStream;
						pIPPP.FlashStream(flowStream, lclFlashType, lclxData.GetValue(i), lclyData.GetValue(num));
						checkNumberOfPhases(flowStream);
						lclFlowStreams.Add(flowStream);
						lclxy2item[i, num] = lclFlowStreams.Count - 1;
					}
				}
				else if (lclMessageLog != null)
				{
					lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Failed to extend property table range (Y = " + yValue + " )");
				}
				return;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Interpolator must be initialised before continuing");
		}

		public void GenerateTable()
		{
			try
			{
				if (lclInitialised)
				{
					new Phase();
					try
					{
						setIPPP();
						pIPPP.FlashStream(lclStreamTemplate, lclFlashType, lclxData.GetValue(0), lclyData.GetValue(0));
					}
					catch (Exception ex)
					{
						throw ex;
					}
					int num = lclxData.Length() - 1;
					int num2 = lclyData.Length() - 1;
					for (int i = 0; i <= num; i++)
					{
						for (int j = 0; j <= num2; j++)
						{
							IFlowStream flowStream = lclStreamTemplate.Clone() as IFlowStream;
							pIPPP.FlashStream(flowStream, lclFlashType, lclxData.GetValue(i), lclyData.GetValue(j));
							checkNumberOfPhases(flowStream);
							lclFlowStreams.Add(flowStream);
							lclxy2item[i, j] = lclFlowStreams.Count - 1;
						}
					}
					return;
				}
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Interpolator must be initialised before continuing");
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Generate Table Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		private bool checkNumberOfPhases(IFlowStream workStream)
		{
			IPhase phase = null;
			bool flag = false;
			bool flag2 = false;
			bool flag3 = false;
			bool flag4 = false;
			bool flag5 = false;
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			try
			{
				phase = workStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
				if (phase != null)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				phase = workStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid);
				if (phase != null)
				{
					flag2 = true;
				}
				phase = workStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Aqueous);
				if (phase != null)
				{
					flag3 = true;
				}
				if (flag2 || flag3)
				{
					flag5 = true;
				}
				if (flag5)
				{
					if (workStream.NumberOfPhases == 2)
					{
						if (!flag && !flag3)
						{
							flag4 = true;
						}
					}
					else if (workStream.NumberOfPhases == 3 && !flag3)
					{
						flag4 = true;
					}
				}
				if (flag4 && lclMessageLog != null)
				{
					lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Warning, "Non aqueous liquid phase detected as second liquid phase.");
				}
				if (mCalculatedPhases == CalculatedPhasesEnum.Variable)
				{
					if (!flag && !flag5)
					{
						throw new Exception("Neither liquid nor vapour detected in flow stream.");
					}
					if (!flag)
					{
						workStream.NumberOfPhases++;
						workStream.GetPhaseByID(workStream.NumberOfPhases).PhaseType = PhaseType.Vapour;
					}
					if (!flag5)
					{
						workStream.NumberOfPhases++;
						workStream.GetPhaseByID(workStream.NumberOfPhases).PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
					}
					phase = workStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Aqueous);
					if (phase != null)
					{
						phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
					}
				}
				else if (mCalculatedPhases == CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquid)
				{
					phase = workStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Aqueous);
					if (phase != null)
					{
						phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Liquid;
					}
				}
				else if (mCalculatedPhases == CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid)
				{
					phase = workStream.GetPhaseByID(workStream.NumberOfPhases);
					phase.PhaseType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
					double num3 = 0.0;
					phase = workStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
					if (phase != null)
					{
						num3 += phase.MolarFraction.Value;
					}
					phase = workStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid);
					if (phase != null)
					{
						num3 += phase.MolarFraction.Value;
					}
					if (num3 > 0.0)
					{
						phase = workStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
						if (phase != null)
						{
							phase.MolarFraction.Value /= num3;
						}
						phase = workStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid);
						if (phase != null)
						{
							phase.MolarFraction.Value /= num3;
						}
					}
					phase = workStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Aqueous);
					if (phase != null)
					{
						phase.MolarFraction.Value = 1.0;
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				throw ex;
			}
			if (mCalculatedPhases == CalculatedPhasesEnum.Variable)
			{
				IPhase phase2 = workStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour).Clone() as IPhase;
				IPhase phase3 = workStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Liquid).Clone() as IPhase;
				workStream.SetPhaseByID(1, (IPhase)phase2.Clone());
				workStream.SetPhaseByID(2, (IPhase)phase3.Clone());
			}
			num2 = workStream.NumberOfPhases;
			for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
			{
				phase = workStream.GetPhaseByID(num + 1);
				if (phase.MolarFraction.Value == 0.0)
				{
					phase.Density.Value = 0.0;
					phase.Viscosity.Value = 0.0;
					phase.InternalEnergy.Value = 0.0;
					phase.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure.Value = 0.0;
					phase.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature.Value = 0.0;
					phase.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure.Value = 0.0;
					phase.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature.Value = 0.0;
					phase.Cp.Value = 0.0;
					phase.MolecularWeight.Value = 0.0;
					phase.SurfaceTension.Value = 0.0;
				}
			}
			if (flag && flag5)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public IFlowStream GetStream(double xValue, double yValue)
		{
			if (lclInitialised)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				int num = lclxData.Find(xValue);
				int num2 = lclyData.Find(yValue);
				if (num == -3)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_0104;
					}
					ExtendXdata(xValue);
					num = lclxData.Find(xValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num2 == -3)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_0104;
					}
					ExtendYdata(yValue);
					num2 = lclyData.Find(yValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num == -2)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_0151;
					}
					ExtendXdata(xValue);
					num = lclxData.Find(xValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num2 == -2)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_0151;
					}
					ExtendYdata(yValue);
					num2 = lclyData.Find(yValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num != -1 && num2 != -1)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						ScreenData();
					}
					int num3 = lclxy2item[num, num2];
					if (num3 == -1)
					{
						return null;
					}
					return lclFlowStreams.Item(num3);
				}
				if (lclMessageLog != null)
				{
					lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Failed to get the requested property value (X = " + xValue + ", Y = " + yValue + " )");
				}
				return null;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Interpolator must be initialised before continuing");
			IL_0104:
			if (lclMessageLog != null)
			{
				lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Requested index value is below existing range (X = " + xValue + ", Y = " + yValue + " )");
			}
			return null;
			IL_0151:
			if (lclMessageLog != null)
			{
				lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Requested index value is above existing range (X = " + xValue + ", Y = " + yValue + " )");
			}
			return null;
		}

		public double InterpolateProperty(PropertyType typeOfProperty, PhaseType typeOfPhase, double xValue, double yValue)
		{
			if (lclInitialised)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				int num = lclxData.Find(xValue);
				int num2 = lclyData.Find(yValue);
				if (num == -3)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_00e6;
					}
					ExtendXdata(xValue);
					num = lclxData.Find(xValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num2 == -3)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_00e6;
					}
					ExtendYdata(yValue);
					num2 = lclyData.Find(yValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num == -2)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_0137;
					}
					ExtendXdata(xValue);
					num = lclxData.Find(xValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num2 == -2)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_0137;
					}
					ExtendYdata(yValue);
					num2 = lclyData.Find(yValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					ScreenData();
				}
				return DoInterpolation(typeOfProperty, typeOfPhase, xValue, num, yValue, num2);
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Interpolator must be initialised before continuing");
			IL_00e6:
			if (lclMessageLog != null)
			{
				lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Requested index value is below existing range (X = " + xValue + ", Y = " + yValue + " )");
			}
			return RealValue.FEMPTY;
			IL_0137:
			if (lclMessageLog != null)
			{
				lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Requested index value is above existing range (X = " + xValue + ", Y = " + yValue + " )");
			}
			return RealValue.FEMPTY;
		}

		public void InterpolateStream(IFlowStream workStream, double xValue, double yValue)
		{
			if (lclInitialised)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				int num = lclxData.Find(xValue);
				int num2 = lclyData.Find(yValue);
				if (num == -3)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_0168;
					}
					double num3;
					do
					{
						num3 = lclxData[0] - lclxGrid;
						ExtendXdata(num3);
					}
					while (num3 > xValue);
					num = lclxData.Find(xValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num2 == -3)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_0168;
					}
					double num4;
					do
					{
						num4 = lclyData[0] - lclyGrid;
						ExtendYdata(num4);
					}
					while (num4 > yValue);
					num2 = lclyData.Find(yValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num == -2)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_01b4;
					}
					double num5;
					do
					{
						int index = lclxData.Length() - 1;
						num5 = lclxData[index] + lclxGrid;
						ExtendXdata(num5);
					}
					while (num5 < xValue);
					num = lclxData.Find(xValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num2 == -2)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_01b4;
					}
					double num6;
					do
					{
						int index2 = lclyData.Length() - 1;
						num6 = lclyData[index2] + lclyGrid;
						ExtendYdata(num6);
					}
					while (num6 < yValue);
					num2 = lclyData.Find(yValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					ScreenData();
				}
				DoStreamInterpolation(workStream, xValue, num, yValue, num2);
				return;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Interpolator must be initialised before continuing");
			IL_01b4:
			if (lclMessageLog != null)
			{
				lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Requested index value is above existing range (X = " + xValue + ", Y = " + yValue + " )");
			}
			return;
			IL_0168:
			if (lclMessageLog != null)
			{
				lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Requested index value is below existing range (X = " + xValue + ", Y = " + yValue + " )");
			}
		}

		private void GetADMtableEntry(int entry, bool isWaterTable, out string entryName, out PhaseType phsType, out PropertyType propType, out PropertyType derivType)
		{
			entryName = "    unknown";
			phsType = PhaseType.UnknownPhase;
			propType = PropertyType.UnknownProperty;
			derivType = PropertyType.UnknownProperty;
			switch (entry)
			{
			case 0:
				entryName = "    GAS_MOLE_FRACTION(";
				propType = PropertyType.MolarFraction;
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Vapour;
				}
				break;
			case 1:
				entryName = "    OIL_MOLE_FRACTION(";
				propType = PropertyType.MolarFraction;
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Liquid;
				}
				else
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
				}
				break;
			case 2:
				entryName = "    GAS_DENSITY(";
				propType = PropertyType.Density;
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Vapour;
				}
				break;
			case 3:
				entryName = "    OIL_DENSITY(";
				propType = PropertyType.Density;
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Liquid;
				}
				else
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
				}
				break;
			case 4:
				entryName = "    GAS_VISCOSITY(";
				propType = PropertyType.Viscosity;
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Vapour;
				}
				break;
			case 5:
				entryName = "    OIL_VISCOSITY(";
				propType = PropertyType.Viscosity;
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Liquid;
				}
				else
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
				}
				break;
			case 6:
				entryName = "    GAS_INT_ENERGY(";
				propType = PropertyType.InternalEnergy;
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Vapour;
				}
				break;
			case 7:
				entryName = "    OIL_INT_ENERGY(";
				propType = PropertyType.InternalEnergy;
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Liquid;
				}
				else
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
				}
				break;
			case 8:
				entryName = "    GAS_DR_DP(";
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Vapour;
				}
				if (useNumericalDerivatives)
				{
					propType = PropertyType.Density;
					derivType = PropertyType.Pressure;
				}
				else
				{
					propType = PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure;
				}
				break;
			case 9:
				entryName = "    OIL_DR_DP(";
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Liquid;
				}
				else
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
				}
				if (useNumericalDerivatives)
				{
					propType = PropertyType.Density;
					derivType = PropertyType.Pressure;
				}
				else
				{
					propType = PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure;
				}
				break;
			case 10:
				entryName = "    GAS_DR_DT(";
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Vapour;
				}
				if (useNumericalDerivatives)
				{
					propType = PropertyType.Density;
					derivType = PropertyType.Temperature;
				}
				else
				{
					propType = PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature;
				}
				break;
			case 11:
				entryName = "    OIL_DR_DT(";
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Liquid;
				}
				if (useNumericalDerivatives)
				{
					propType = PropertyType.Density;
					derivType = PropertyType.Temperature;
				}
				else
				{
					propType = PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature;
				}
				break;
			case 12:
				entryName = "    GAS_DU_DP(";
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Vapour;
				}
				if (useNumericalDerivatives)
				{
					propType = PropertyType.InternalEnergy;
					derivType = PropertyType.Pressure;
				}
				else
				{
					propType = PropertyType.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure;
				}
				break;
			case 13:
				entryName = "    OIL_DU_DP(";
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Liquid;
				}
				else
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
				}
				if (useNumericalDerivatives)
				{
					propType = PropertyType.InternalEnergy;
					derivType = PropertyType.Pressure;
				}
				else
				{
					propType = PropertyType.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure;
				}
				break;
			case 14:
				entryName = "    GAS_DU_DT(";
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Vapour;
				}
				if (useNumericalDerivatives)
				{
					propType = PropertyType.InternalEnergy;
					derivType = PropertyType.Temperature;
				}
				else
				{
					propType = PropertyType.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature;
				}
				break;
			case 15:
				entryName = "    OIL_DU_DT(";
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Liquid;
				}
				else
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
				}
				if (useNumericalDerivatives)
				{
					propType = PropertyType.InternalEnergy;
					derivType = PropertyType.Temperature;
				}
				else
				{
					propType = PropertyType.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature;
				}
				break;
			case 16:
				entryName = "    GAS_OIL_IF_TENSION(";
				propType = PropertyType.SurfaceTension;
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Liquid;
				}
				else
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
				}
				break;
			case 17:
				entryName = "    GAS_DH_DT(";
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Vapour;
				}
				if (useNumericalDerivatives)
				{
					propType = PropertyType.Enthalpy;
					derivType = PropertyType.Temperature;
				}
				else
				{
					propType = PropertyType.Cp;
				}
				break;
			case 18:
				entryName = "    OIL_DH_DT(";
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Liquid;
				}
				else
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
				}
				if (useNumericalDerivatives)
				{
					propType = PropertyType.Enthalpy;
					derivType = PropertyType.Temperature;
				}
				else
				{
					propType = PropertyType.Cp;
				}
				break;
			case 19:
				entryName = "    GAS_MW(";
				propType = PropertyType.MolecularWeight;
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Vapour;
				}
				break;
			case 20:
				entryName = "    OIL_MW(";
				propType = PropertyType.MolecularWeight;
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Liquid;
				}
				else
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
				}
				break;
			case 21:
				entryName = "    GAS_THCOND(";
				propType = PropertyType.Conductivity;
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Vapour;
				}
				break;
			case 22:
				entryName = "    OIL_THCOND(";
				propType = PropertyType.Conductivity;
				if (!isWaterTable)
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Liquid;
				}
				else
				{
					phsType = PhaseType.Aqueous;
				}
				break;
			default:
				entryName = "    unknown";
				propType = PropertyType.UnknownProperty;
				phsType = PhaseType.UnknownPhase;
				break;
			}
		}

		public void GetNumericalDerivative(PropertyType typeOfyProperty, PropertyType typeOfxProperty, PhaseType typeOfPhase, ref double theValue, int xIndex, ref double xValue, int yIndex, ref double yValue)
		{
			theValue = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			xValue = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			yValue = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			if (lclInitialised)
			{
				if (xIndex >= lclxData.Length() || xIndex < 0 || yIndex >= lclyData.Length() || yIndex < 0)
				{
					return;
				}
				int index = lclxy2item[xIndex, yIndex];
				IPhase phase = null;
				IFlowStream flowStream = null;
				flowStream = lclFlowStreams.Item(index);
				if (flowStream != null)
				{
					phase = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(typeOfPhase);
					if (phase == null || phase.MolarFraction.Value == 0.0)
					{
						theValue = 0.0;
					}
					else
					{
						int index2 = 0;
						int index3 = 0;
						if (typeOfxProperty == PropertyType.Temperature)
						{
							if (yIndex == 0)
							{
								index2 = lclxy2item[xIndex, 0L];
								index3 = lclxy2item[xIndex, 1L];
							}
							else if (yIndex == lclyData.Length() - 1)
							{
								index2 = lclxy2item[xIndex, yIndex - 1];
								index3 = lclxy2item[xIndex, yIndex];
							}
							else
							{
								index2 = lclxy2item[xIndex, yIndex - 1];
								index3 = lclxy2item[xIndex, yIndex + 1];
							}
						}
						if (typeOfxProperty == PropertyType.Pressure)
						{
							if (xIndex == 0)
							{
								index2 = lclxy2item[0L, yIndex];
								index3 = lclxy2item[1L, yIndex];
							}
							else if (xIndex == lclxData.Length() - 1)
							{
								index2 = lclxy2item[xIndex - 1, yIndex];
								index3 = lclxy2item[xIndex, yIndex];
							}
							else
							{
								index2 = lclxy2item[xIndex - 1, yIndex];
								index3 = lclxy2item[xIndex + 1, yIndex];
							}
						}
						IFlowStream flowStream2 = lclFlowStreams.Item(index2);
						IFlowStream flowStream3 = lclFlowStreams.Item(index3);
						if (flowStream2.GetPhaseByType(typeOfPhase) == null || flowStream2.GetPhaseByType(typeOfPhase).MolarFraction.Value == 0.0 || (phase.MolarFraction.Value < 1.0 && flowStream2.GetPhaseByType(typeOfPhase).MolarFraction.Value == 1.0))
						{
							flowStream2 = lclFlowStreams.Item(index);
						}
						if (flowStream3.GetPhaseByType(typeOfPhase) == null || flowStream3.GetPhaseByType(typeOfPhase).MolarFraction.Value == 0.0 || (phase.MolarFraction.Value < 1.0 && flowStream3.GetPhaseByType(typeOfPhase).MolarFraction.Value == 1.0))
						{
							flowStream3 = lclFlowStreams.Item(index);
						}
						double property = flowStream2.GetPhaseByType(typeOfPhase).GetProperty(typeOfyProperty);
						double property2 = flowStream3.GetPhaseByType(typeOfPhase).GetProperty(typeOfyProperty);
						double property3 = flowStream2.GetPhaseByID(0).GetProperty(typeOfxProperty);
						double property4 = flowStream3.GetPhaseByID(0).GetProperty(typeOfxProperty);
						if (property4 != property3)
						{
							theValue = (property2 - property) / (property4 - property3);
						}
						else
						{
							theValue = 0.0;
						}
					}
				}
				xValue = lclxData[xIndex];
				yValue = lclyData[yIndex];
				return;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Interpolator must be initialised before continuing");
		}

		public void PlotProperty(PropertyType typeOfyProperty, PhaseType typeOfPhase, ref double theValue, int xIndex, ref double xValue, int yIndex, ref double yValue)
		{
			if (lclInitialised)
			{
				if (xIndex < lclxData.Length() && xIndex >= 0 && yIndex < lclyData.Length() && yIndex >= 0)
				{
					int index = lclxy2item[xIndex, yIndex];
					IPhase phase = null;
					IFlowStream flowStream = null;
					flowStream = lclFlowStreams.Item(index);
					theValue = RealValue.FEMPTY;
					if (flowStream != null)
					{
						phase = flowStream.GetPhaseByType(typeOfPhase);
						if (phase == null)
						{
							theValue = 0.0;
						}
						else
						{
							theValue = phase.GetProperty(typeOfyProperty);
						}
					}
					xValue = lclxData[xIndex];
					yValue = lclyData[yIndex];
				}
				else
				{
					if (lclMessageLog != null)
					{
						lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Failed to plot the requested property value (X = " + xIndex + ", Y = " + yIndex + " )");
					}
					theValue = RealValue.FEMPTY;
					xValue = RealValue.FEMPTY;
					yValue = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				}
				return;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Interpolator must be initialised before continuing");
		}

		public void ReadADMtable(string fNameHydrocarbon, string fNameWater)
		{
		}

		public void WriteADMtable(int TableIdentifier)
		{
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = 0;
			int i = 0;
			int num3 = 1;
			double theValue = 0.0;
			double xValue = 0.0;
			double yValue = 0.0;
			CultureInfo cultureInfo = new CultureInfo("en-GB", useUserOverride: false);
			int num4 = 2;
			if (mCalculatedPhases == CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid)
			{
				num4 = 3;
			}
			new FlowStream(num4);
			new Phase();
			PropertyType derivType = PropertyType.UnknownProperty;
			string text = Environment.GetEnvironmentVariable("TEMP");
			if (text.Length > 0)
			{
				text += "\\";
			}
			string text2 = text + "PL" + TableIdentifier.ToString("000000") + ".TAB";
			string text3 = text + "PL" + TableIdentifier.ToString("000000") + ".TWB";
			if (IsReady)
			{
				if (File.Exists(text2))
				{
					File.Delete(text2);
				}
				StreamWriter streamWriter = File.CreateText(text2);
				streamWriter.Write("FLUID(");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("  NAME(\"PVT Table Definition\")");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("  DESCRIPTION(\"IPPP Interpolator PVT TABLE\")");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Flush();
				streamWriter.Write("  MOLE_FRACTIONS(");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("    COMPONENT(");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("      NAME(\"METHANE\"), FRACTION(1.000  ))");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("      )");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Flush();
				streamWriter.Write("  PROPERTY_ENTRY(");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("    NAME(\"MFLASH ENTRIES\")");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("    DESCRIPTION(\"" + text2 + "\")");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("    TABLE_ORDER(\"TP\")");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Flush();
				num3 = 1;
				streamWriter.Write("    PRESSURE(");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				for (num = 0; num < Xlength; num++)
				{
					streamWriter.Write("      ");
					PlotProperty(PropertyType.Pressure, PhaseType.Vapour, ref theValue, num, ref xValue, 0, ref yValue);
					streamWriter.Write(xValue.ToString("E", cultureInfo));
					if (num3 == 4)
					{
						streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
						num3 = 0;
					}
					num3++;
				}
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("    )");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Flush();
				num3 = 1;
				streamWriter.Write("TEMPERATURE(");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				for (num2 = 0; num2 < Ylength; num2++)
				{
					streamWriter.Write("      ");
					PlotProperty(PropertyType.Temperature, PhaseType.Vapour, ref theValue, 0, ref xValue, num2, ref yValue);
					streamWriter.Write(yValue.ToString("E", cultureInfo));
					if (num3 == 4)
					{
						streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
						num3 = 0;
					}
					num3++;
				}
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("    )");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Flush();
				double num5 = 1.0;
				string entryName;
				PhaseType phsType;
				PropertyType propType;
				for (; i < 23; i++)
				{
					bool flag = false;
					GetADMtableEntry(i, isWaterTable: false, out entryName, out phsType, out propType, out derivType);
					if (derivType != PropertyType.UnknownProperty)
					{
						flag = true;
					}
					num3 = 1;
					streamWriter.Write(entryName);
					streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
					for (num = 0; num < Xlength; num++)
					{
						for (num2 = 0; num2 < Ylength; num2++)
						{
							streamWriter.Write("      ");
							if (!flag)
							{
								PlotProperty(propType, phsType, ref theValue, num, ref xValue, num2, ref yValue);
							}
							else
							{
								GetNumericalDerivative(propType, derivType, phsType, ref theValue, num, ref xValue, num2, ref yValue);
							}
							if (theValue == RealValue.FEMPTY)
							{
								theValue = 0.0;
							}
							theValue *= num5;
							streamWriter.Write(theValue.ToString("E", cultureInfo));
							if (num3 == 4)
							{
								streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
								num3 = 0;
							}
							num3++;
						}
					}
					streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
					streamWriter.Write("    )");
					streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
					streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
					streamWriter.Flush();
				}
				streamWriter.Write("    )");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("  )");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write(")");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Close();
				if (num4 != 3)
				{
					return;
				}
				if (File.Exists(text3))
				{
					File.Delete(text3);
				}
				streamWriter = File.CreateText(text3);
				streamWriter.Write("FLUID(");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("  NAME(\"water PVT Table Definition\")");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("  DESCRIPTION(\"IPPP Interpolator PVT TABLE\")");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Flush();
				streamWriter.Write("  MOLE_FRACTIONS(");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("    COMPONENT(");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("      NAME(\"WATER\"), FRACTION(1.000  ))");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("      )");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Flush();
				streamWriter.Write("  PROPERTY_ENTRY(");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("    NAME(\"MFLASH ENTRIES\")");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("    DESCRIPTION(\"" + text3 + "\")");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("    TABLE_ORDER(\"TP\")");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Flush();
				num3 = 1;
				streamWriter.Write("    PRESSURE(");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				for (num = 0; num < Xlength; num++)
				{
					streamWriter.Write("      ");
					PlotProperty(PropertyType.Pressure, PhaseType.Vapour, ref theValue, num, ref xValue, 0, ref yValue);
					streamWriter.Write(xValue.ToString("E", cultureInfo));
					if (num3 == 4)
					{
						streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
						num3 = 0;
					}
					num3++;
				}
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("    )");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Flush();
				num3 = 1;
				streamWriter.Write("TEMPERATURE(");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				for (num2 = 0; num2 < Ylength; num2++)
				{
					streamWriter.Write("      ");
					PlotProperty(PropertyType.Temperature, PhaseType.Vapour, ref theValue, 0, ref xValue, num2, ref yValue);
					streamWriter.Write(yValue.ToString("E", cultureInfo));
					if (num3 == 4)
					{
						streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
						num3 = 0;
					}
					num3++;
				}
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("    )");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Flush();
				for (i = 0; i < 23; i++)
				{
					bool flag2 = false;
					GetADMtableEntry(i, isWaterTable: true, out entryName, out phsType, out propType, out derivType);
					if (derivType != PropertyType.UnknownProperty)
					{
						flag2 = true;
					}
					num3 = 1;
					streamWriter.Write(entryName);
					streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
					for (num = 0; num < Xlength; num++)
					{
						for (num2 = 0; num2 < Ylength; num2++)
						{
							streamWriter.Write("      ");
							if (phsType != PhaseType.UnknownPhase)
							{
								if (!flag2)
								{
									PlotProperty(propType, phsType, ref theValue, num, ref xValue, num2, ref yValue);
								}
								else
								{
									GetNumericalDerivative(propType, derivType, phsType, ref theValue, num, ref xValue, num2, ref yValue);
								}
							}
							else
							{
								theValue = 0.0;
							}
							theValue *= num5;
							streamWriter.Write(theValue.ToString("E", cultureInfo));
							if (num3 == 4)
							{
								streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
								num3 = 0;
							}
							num3++;
						}
					}
					streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
					streamWriter.Write("    )");
					streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
					streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
					streamWriter.Flush();
				}
				streamWriter.Write("    )");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write("  )");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Write(")");
				streamWriter.Write(streamWriter.NewLine);
				streamWriter.Close();
				return;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Failed to open ADM property table - " + text3.ToString());
		}

		public void ModifySinglePhase()
		{
			fillPhase(PhaseType.Vapour);
			fillPhase(PhaseType.Liquid);
		}

		private void fillPhase(PhaseType phaseType)
		{
			int xlength = Xlength;
			int ylength = Ylength;
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < xlength; i++)
			{
				bool flag2 = false;
				if (lclFlowStreams.Item(lclxy2item[i, 0L]).GetPhaseByType(phaseType).Density.Value > 0.0)
				{
					flag2 = true;
					flag = true;
				}
				else
				{
					for (int j = 0; j < ylength; j++)
					{
						if (lclFlowStreams.Item(lclxy2item[i, j]).GetPhaseByType(phaseType).Density.Value > 0.0)
						{
							copyPhaseProperties(lclFlowStreams.Item(lclxy2item[i, j]).GetPhaseByType(phaseType), lclFlowStreams.Item(lclxy2item[i, 0L]).GetPhaseByType(phaseType));
							flag2 = true;
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (!flag2)
				{
					continue;
				}
				for (int j = 1; j < ylength; j++)
				{
					if (lclFlowStreams.Item(lclxy2item[i, j]).GetPhaseByType(phaseType).Density.Value <= 0.0)
					{
						copyPhaseProperties(lclFlowStreams.Item(lclxy2item[i, j - 1]).GetPhaseByType(phaseType), lclFlowStreams.Item(lclxy2item[i, j]).GetPhaseByType(phaseType));
					}
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				throw new Exception("Error setting up property table: no two phase points found.");
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < ylength; j++)
			{
				bool flag2 = false;
				if (lclFlowStreams.Item(lclxy2item[0L, j]).GetPhaseByType(phaseType).Density.Value > 0.0)
				{
					flag2 = true;
				}
				else
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < xlength; i++)
					{
						if (lclFlowStreams.Item(lclxy2item[i, j]).GetPhaseByType(phaseType).Density.Value > 0.0)
						{
							copyPhaseProperties(lclFlowStreams.Item(lclxy2item[i, j]).GetPhaseByType(phaseType), lclFlowStreams.Item(lclxy2item[0L, j]).GetPhaseByType(phaseType));
							flag2 = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					for (int i = 1; i < xlength; i++)
					{
						if (lclFlowStreams.Item(lclxy2item[i, j]).GetPhaseByType(phaseType).Density.Value <= 0.0)
						{
							copyPhaseProperties(lclFlowStreams.Item(lclxy2item[i - 1, j]).GetPhaseByType(phaseType), lclFlowStreams.Item(lclxy2item[i, j]).GetPhaseByType(phaseType));
						}
					}
					continue;
				}
				throw new Exception("Error setting up property table.");
			}
		}

		private void copyPhaseProperties(IPhase source, IPhase sink)
		{
			if (source.PhaseType != sink.PhaseType)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException("Phase type of source and sink do not match.");
			}
			sink.MolecularWeight = new RealValue(0.01);
			sink.MolarFraction = new RealValue(0.0);
			sink.MassFraction = new RealValue(0.0);
			sink.Density = new RealValue(source.Density);
			sink.Viscosity = new RealValue(source.Viscosity);
			sink.InternalEnergy = new RealValue(source.InternalEnergy);
			sink.Cp = new RealValue(source.Cp);
			sink.Cv = new RealValue(source.Cv);
			sink.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure = new RealValue(source.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure);
			sink.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature = new RealValue(source.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature);
			sink.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure = new RealValue(source.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure);
			sink.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature = new RealValue(source.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature);
			sink.SurfaceTension = new RealValue(source.SurfaceTension);
		}

		public void ScreenData()
		{
			double num = 0.05;
			IPhase phase = new Phase();
			if (lclFlashType != FlashType.PTFlash)
			{
				if (lclMessageLog != null)
				{
					lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Failed to screen the property tables - Please specify a PT Flash");
				}
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				setIPPP();
				pIPPP.FlashStream(lclStreamTemplate, FlashType.PTFlash, lclxData[0], lclyData[0]);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				throw ex;
			}
			int num2 = lclxData.Length();
			int num3 = lclyData.Length();
			double[] array = new double[num2];
			double[] array2 = new double[num2];
			int num4 = 0;
			while (true)
			{
				if (num4 < num2)
				{
					array[num4] = pIPPP.CalculateBubblePoint(lclStreamTemplate, lclxData[num4]);
					int num5 = lclyData.Find(array[num4]);
					if ((uint)(num5 - -3) > 2u)
					{
						if (num5 != 0)
						{
							break;
						}
					}
					else if (!(array[num4] < 0.0) && lclyData[lclyData.LoBracket] * 1.05 < array[num4] && array[num4] < lclyData[lclyData.HiBracket] * 0.95)
					{
						ExtendYdata(array[num4]);
					}
					array2[num4] = pIPPP.CalculateDewPoint(lclStreamTemplate, lclxData[num4]);
					switch (lclyData.Find(array2[num4]))
					{
					case -3:
					case -2:
					case -1:
						if (!(array2[num4] < 0.0) && lclyData[lclyData.LoBracket] * 1.05 < array2[num4] && array2[num4] < lclyData[lclyData.HiBracket] * 0.95)
						{
							ExtendYdata(array2[num4]);
						}
						goto IL_01e9;
					case 0:
						goto IL_01e9;
					}
					break;
				}
				num2 = lclxData.Length() - 1;
				num3 = lclyData.Length() - 1;
				for (int i = 0; i < num2; i++)
				{
					double num6 = lclxData[i];
					lclxData.Find(num6);
					for (int j = 0; j < num3; j++)
					{
						double num7 = lclyData[j];
						lclyData.Find(num7);
						double num8 = DoInterpolation(PropertyType.Density, PhaseType.Vapour, num6, i, num7, j);
						if (num8 == RealValue.FEMPTY)
						{
							continue;
						}
						pIPPP.FlashStream(lclStreamTemplate, lclFlashType, num6, num7);
						phase = lclStreamTemplate.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
						if (phase != null)
						{
							double property = phase.GetProperty(PropertyType.Density);
							if (property * (1.0 + num) < num8 || num8 < property * (1.0 - num))
							{
								throw new Exception("Property table not self consistent");
							}
						}
					}
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < num2; i++)
				{
					double num6 = lclxData[i] + (lclxData[i + 1] - lclxData[i]) / 2.0;
					lclxData.Find(num6);
					for (int j = 0; j < num3; j++)
					{
						double num7 = lclyData[j];
						lclyData.Find(num7);
						double num8 = DoInterpolation(PropertyType.Density, PhaseType.Vapour, num6, i, num7, j);
						if (num8 == RealValue.FEMPTY)
						{
							continue;
						}
						pIPPP.FlashStream(lclStreamTemplate, lclFlashType, num6, num7);
						phase = lclStreamTemplate.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
						if (phase != null)
						{
							double property = phase.GetProperty(PropertyType.Density);
							if (property * (1.0 + num) < num8 || num8 < property * (1.0 - num))
							{
								ExtendXdata(num6);
								num2 = lclxData.Length() - 1;
								i--;
								break;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < num2; i++)
				{
					double num6 = lclxData[i];
					lclxData.Find(num6);
					for (int j = 0; j < num3; j++)
					{
						double num7 = lclyData[j] + (lclyData[j + 1] - lclyData[j]) / 2.0;
						lclyData.Find(num7);
						double num8 = DoInterpolation(PropertyType.Density, PhaseType.Vapour, num6, i, num7, j);
						if (num8 == RealValue.FEMPTY)
						{
							continue;
						}
						pIPPP.FlashStream(lclStreamTemplate, lclFlashType, num6, num7);
						phase = lclStreamTemplate.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Vapour);
						if (phase != null)
						{
							double property = phase.GetProperty(PropertyType.Density);
							if (property * (1.0 + num) < num8 || num8 < property * (1.0 - num))
							{
								ExtendYdata(num7);
								num3 = lclyData.Length() - 1;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return;
				IL_01e9:
				num4++;
			}
			if (lclMessageLog != null)
			{
				lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Failed to screen the property tables");
			}
		}

		public void SetStream(double xValue, double yValue, IFlowStream newStream)
		{
			if (lclInitialised)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				int num = lclxData.Find(xValue);
				int num2 = lclyData.Find(yValue);
				if (num == -3)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_0169;
					}
					ExtendXdata(xValue);
					num = lclxData.Find(xValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num2 == -3)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_0169;
					}
					ExtendYdata(yValue);
					num2 = lclyData.Find(yValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num == -2)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_01b4;
					}
					ExtendXdata(xValue);
					num = lclxData.Find(xValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num2 == -2)
				{
					if (!lclExtendMode)
					{
						goto IL_01b4;
					}
					ExtendYdata(yValue);
					num2 = lclyData.Find(yValue);
					flag = true;
				}
				if (num != -1 && num2 != -1)
				{
					if (flag)
					{
						ScreenData();
					}
					if (lclxy2item[num, num2] < 0)
					{
						lclFlowStreams.Add(newStream);
						lclxy2item[num, num2] = lclFlowStreams.Count - 1;
					}
					else if (lclMessageLog != null)
					{
						lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Warning, "Failed to add stream as it already specified (X = " + xValue + ", Y = " + yValue + " )");
					}
					return;
				}
				goto IL_01ff;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Interpolator must be initialised before continuing");
			IL_01ff:
			if (lclMessageLog != null)
			{
				lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Failed to set the requested property value (X = " + xValue + ", Y = " + yValue + " )");
			}
			return;
			IL_01b4:
			if (lclMessageLog != null)
			{
				lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Requested index value is above existing range (X = " + xValue + ", Y = " + yValue + " )");
			}
			goto IL_01ff;
			IL_0169:
			if (lclMessageLog != null)
			{
				lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Error, "Requested index value is below existing range (X = " + xValue + ", Y = " + yValue + " )");
			}
			goto IL_01b4;
		}

		public double CalculateBubblePoint(IFlowStream workStream)
		{
			if (workStream.StreamID != lclStreamTemplate.StreamID)
			{
				if (lclMessageLog != null)
				{
					lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Fatal, "The Stream ID differs from Property Interpolator Stream ID");
				}
				return RealValue.FEMPTY;
			}
			try
			{
				setIPPP();
				return pIPPP.CalculateBubblePoint(workStream);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				throw ex;
			}
		}

		public double CalculateProperty(IFlowStream workStream, PropertyType typeOfProperty, PhaseType typeOfPhase, FlashType typeOfFlash)
		{
			if (GetFlashData(workStream, typeOfFlash, out var data, out var data2))
			{
				return CalculateProperty(workStream, typeOfProperty, typeOfPhase, typeOfFlash, data, data2);
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Failed to retreive flash data");
		}

		public double CalculateProperty(IFlowStream workStream, PropertyType typeOfProperty, PhaseType typeOfPhase, FlashType typeOfFlash, double data1, double data2)
		{
			if (workStream.StreamID != lclStreamTemplate.StreamID)
			{
				if (lclMessageLog != null)
				{
					lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Fatal, "The Stream ID differs from Property Interpolator Stream ID");
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (typeOfFlash == lclFlashType)
				{
					return InterpolateProperty(typeOfProperty, typeOfPhase, data1, data2);
				}
				if (lclMessageLog != null)
				{
					lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Fatal, "The Flash Type requested is differs from Property Interpolator Flash Type");
				}
			}
			return RealValue.FEMPTY;
		}

		{
			if (GetFlashData(workStream, typeOfFlash, out var data, out var data2))
			{
				return CalculateSurfaceTension(workStream, typeOfPhase, typeOfFlash, data, data2);
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Failed to retreive flash data");
		}

		public double CalculateSurfaceTension(IFlowStream workStream, PhaseType typeOfPhase, FlashType typeOfFlash, double data1, double data2)
		{
			if (workStream.StreamID != lclStreamTemplate.StreamID)
			{
				if (lclMessageLog != null)
				{
					lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Fatal, "The Stream ID differs from Property Interpolator Stream ID");
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (typeOfFlash == lclFlashType)
				{
					try
					{
						setIPPP();
						return pIPPP.CalculateSurfaceTension(workStream, typeOfPhase, typeOfFlash);
					}
					catch (Exception ex)
					{
						throw ex;
					}
				}
				if (lclMessageLog != null)
				{
					lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Fatal, "The Flash Type requested is differs from Property Interpolator Flash Type");
				}
			}
			return RealValue.FEMPTY;
		}

		public void FlashStream(IFlowStream workStream, FlashType typeOfFlash)
		{
			if (GetFlashData(workStream, typeOfFlash, out var data, out var data2))
			{
				FlashStream(workStream, typeOfFlash, data, data2);
				return;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Failed to retreive flash data");
		}

		public void FlashStream(IFlowStream workStream, FlashType typeOfFlash, double data1, double data2)
		{
			if (workStream.StreamID != lclStreamTemplate.StreamID)
			{
				if (lclMessageLog != null)
				{
					lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Fatal, "The Stream ID differs from Property Interpolator Stream ID");
				}
				return;
			}
			if (typeOfFlash != lclFlashType)
			{
				if (lclMessageLog != null)
				{
					lclMessageLog.Add(MessageLogType.Fatal, "The Flash Type requested is differs from Property Interpolator Flash Type");
				}
				return;
			}
			if (lclInitialised)
			{
				InterpolateStream(workStream, data1, data2);
				return;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Interpolator must be initialised before continuing");
		}

		public void ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(IFlowStream workStream, FlashType typeOfFlash)
		{
			if (GetFlashData(workStream, typeOfFlash, out var data, out var data2))
			{
				ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(workStream, typeOfFlash, data, data2);
				return;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Failed to retreive flash data");
		}

		public void ReflashStreamWithSameComposition(IFlowStream workStream, FlashType typeOfFlash, double data1, double data2)
		{
			try
			{
				if (lclInitialised)
				{
					pIPPP.FlashStream(workStream, lclFlashType, data1, data2);
					return;
				}
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Interpolator must be initialised before continuing");
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("Reflash Stream With Same Composition Failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		public void GenerateTable(CalculatedPhasesEnum CalcPhases)
		{
			if (lclInitialised)
			{
				if (CalcPhases == CalculatedPhasesEnum.VapourLiquidLiquid)
				{
					cMethod = CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases.DoNotCombine;
				}
				mCalculatedPhases = CalcPhases;
				GenerateTable();
				return;
			}
			throw new InvalidOperationException("Interpolator must be initialised before continuing");
		}

		public void ResetComposition(IFlowStream NewStreamBasis, CalculatedPhasesEnum cPEnum, CombinationMethodForLiquidPhases cMLPhases)
		{
			lclInitialised = true;
			lclFlowStreams.Clear();
			lclxy2item.Clear();
			lclStreamTemplate = NewStreamBasis.Clone() as IFlowStream;
			Mode = false;
			cMethod = cMLPhases;
			mCalculatedPhases = cPEnum;
			IAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase obj = (IAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase)pIPPP;
			obj.LiquidPhaseCombination = cMethod;
			obj.CalculatedPhases = mCalculatedPhases;
			if (pIPPP is AHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase aHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase)
			{
				aHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase.IsFixedPhaseSet = true;
			}
			GenerateTable();
		}

		private bool GetFlashData(IFlowStream workStream, FlashType typeOfFlash, out double data1, out double data2)
		{
			IPhase phaseByType = workStream.GetPhaseByType(PhaseType.Overall);
			if (phaseByType == null)
			{
				data1 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				data2 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				return false;
			}
			switch (typeOfFlash)
			{
			case FlashType.PTFlash:
				data1 = phaseByType.Pressure.Value;
				data2 = phaseByType.Temperature.Value;
				break;
			case FlashType.PSFlash:
				data1 = phaseByType.Pressure.Value;
				data2 = phaseByType.Entropy.Value;
				break;
			case FlashType.PHFlash:
				data1 = phaseByType.Pressure.Value;
				data2 = phaseByType.Enthalpy.Value;
				break;
			case FlashType.THFlash:
				data1 = phaseByType.Temperature.Value;
				data2 = phaseByType.Enthalpy.Value;
				break;
			case FlashType.TSFlash:
				data1 = phaseByType.Temperature.Value;
				data2 = phaseByType.Entropy.Value;
				break;
			default:
				data1 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				data2 = RealValue.FEMPTY;
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private double DoInterpolation(PropertyType typeOfProperty, PhaseType typeOfPhase, double xValue, int xIndex, double yValue, int yIndex)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			double num4 = 0.0;
			if (lclxData.LoBracket == -1 || lclxData.HiBracket == -1 || lclyData.LoBracket == -1 || lclyData.HiBracket == -1)
			{
				return RealValue.FEMPTY;
			}
			double num5 = (xValue - lclxData[lclxData.LoBracket]) / (lclxData[lclxData.HiBracket] - lclxData[lclxData.LoBracket]);
			double num6 = (yValue - lclyData[lclyData.LoBracket]) / (lclyData[lclyData.HiBracket] - lclyData[lclyData.LoBracket]);
			int num7 = lclxy2item[lclxData.LoBracket, lclyData.LoBracket];
			if (num7 == -1)
			{
				return RealValue.FEMPTY;
			}
			IPhase phaseByType = lclFlowStreams.Item(num7).GetPhaseByType(typeOfPhase);
			if (phaseByType != null)
			{
				num = phaseByType.GetProperty(typeOfProperty);
				num7 = lclxy2item[lclxData.LoBracket, lclyData.HiBracket];
				if (num7 == -1)
				{
					return RealValue.FEMPTY;
				}
				phaseByType = lclFlowStreams.Item(num7).GetPhaseByType(typeOfPhase);
				if (phaseByType != null)
				{
					num2 = phaseByType.GetProperty(typeOfProperty);
					num7 = lclxy2item[lclxData.HiBracket, lclyData.LoBracket];
					if (num7 == -1)
					{
						return RealValue.FEMPTY;
					}
					phaseByType = lclFlowStreams.Item(num7).GetPhaseByType(typeOfPhase);
					if (phaseByType != null)
					{
						num3 = phaseByType.GetProperty(typeOfProperty);
						num7 = lclxy2item[lclxData.HiBracket, lclyData.HiBracket];
						if (num7 == -1)
						{
							return RealValue.FEMPTY;
						}
						phaseByType = lclFlowStreams.Item(num7).GetPhaseByType(typeOfPhase);
						if (phaseByType != null)
						{
							num4 = phaseByType.GetProperty(typeOfProperty);
							if (num == 0.0 || num2 == 0.0)
							{
								if (num == 0.0)
								{
									num = num2;
								}
								else
								{
									num2 = num;
								}
							}
							if (num3 == 0.0 || num4 == 0.0)
							{
								if (num3 == 0.0)
								{
									num3 = num4;
								}
								else
								{
									num4 = num3;
								}
							}
							double num8 = num + num6 * (num2 - num);
							double num9 = num3 + num6 * (num4 - num3);
							return num8 + num5 * (num9 - num8);
						}
						return RealValue.FEMPTY;
					}
					return RealValue.FEMPTY;
				}
				return RealValue.FEMPTY;
			}
			return RealValue.FEMPTY;
		}

		private void DoStreamInterpolation(IFlowStream workStream, double xValue, int xIndex, double yValue, int yIndex)
		{
			if (workStream == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("workStream");
			}
			if (lclxData.LoBracket == -1 || lclxData.HiBracket == -1 || lclyData.LoBracket == -1 || lclyData.HiBracket == -1)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("DoStreamInterpolation - interpolation not bracketed, xValue = " + xValue + " yValue = " + yValue);
			}
			IFlowStream stream = GetStream(lclxData[lclxData.LoBracket], lclyData[lclyData.LoBracket]);
			if (workStream.NumberOfPhases != stream.NumberOfPhases)
			{
				workStream.NumberOfPhases = stream.NumberOfPhases;
				for (int i = 0; i < workStream.NumberOfPhases; i++)
				{
					workStream.GetPhaseByID(i + 1).PhaseType = stream.GetPhaseByID(i + 1).PhaseType;
				}
			}
			for (int j = 0; j <= workStream.NumberOfPhases; j++)
			{
				IPhase phaseByID = workStream.GetPhaseByID(j);
				phaseByID.Conductivity.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.Conductivity, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.Cp.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.Cp, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.CriticalPressure.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.CriticalPressure, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.Cv.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.Cv, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.Density.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.Density, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.Enthalpy.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.Enthalpy, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.Entropy.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.Entropy, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.InternalEnergy.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.InternalEnergy, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.MassFraction.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.MassFraction, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.MolarFraction.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.MolarFraction, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.MolecularWeight.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.MolecularWeight, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.Pressure.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.Pressure, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.SurfaceTension.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.SurfaceTension, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.Temperature.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.Temperature, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.Viscosity.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.Viscosity, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
				phaseByID.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature.Value = InterpolateProperty(PropertyType.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature, phaseByID.PhaseType, xValue, yValue);
			}
		}

		private void setIPPP()
		{
			IAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase = null;
			IPPPWithCOMThermoBase iPPPWithCOMThermoBase = null;
			try
			{
				if (pIPPP == null)
				{
					iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase = createIPPP();
					if (!(iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase is AHPPPWithAspenProperties))
					{
						iPPPWithCOMThermoBase = iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase as IPPPWithCOMThermoBase;
						if (lclpppType != (PropertyPackage)(-1))
						{
							iPPPWithCOMThermoBase.SetPropertyPackageByEnum(lclpppType);
						}
						else
						{
							iPPPWithCOMThermoBase.SetPropertyPackageByEnum(PropertyPackage.PengRobinson);
						}
						if (ctfPath != "" && (packageBase == IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.MultiflashThroughCOMThermo || packageBase == IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.Default))
						{
							iPPPWithCOMThermoBase.InitFromCTF(ctfPath, lclStreamTemplate);
						}
						else
						{
							iPPPWithCOMThermoBase.InitFromStream(lclStreamTemplate);
						}
					}
					pIPPP = iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase;
				}
				pIPPP.FlashLevel = flashLevel;
				IAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase obj = (IAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase)pIPPP;
				obj.LiquidPhaseCombination = cMethod;
				obj.CalculatedPhases = mCalculatedPhases;
				obj.ThermoBase = ThermoBaseEnum.NotIncludeHF;
				if (pIPPP is AHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase aHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase)
				{
					aHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase.IsFixedPhaseSet = true;
				}
				if (pIPPP is AHPPPWithFlarenet)
				{
					useNumericalDerivatives = true;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				throw ex;
			}
		}

		private IAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase createIPPP()
		{
			try
			{
				IAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase;
				switch (packageBase)
				{
				case IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.AspenProperty:
					iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase = new AHPPPWithAspenProperties();
					checkHBase = BooleanSwitchEnum.On;
					break;
				case IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.Default:
				case IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.COMThermoPureComp:
					iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase = new ImpIPPPWithCOMThermoPureComp();
					checkHBase = BooleanSwitchEnum.On;
					useNumericalDerivatives = true;
					break;
				case IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.COMThermoBlackOil:
					iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase = new AHPPPWithBlackOilThroughCOMThermo();
					checkHBase = BooleanSwitchEnum.Undefined;
					break;
				case IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.MultiflashThroughCOMThermo:
					iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase = new ImpIPPPWithMultiflashThroughCOMThermo();
					if (checkHBase == BooleanSwitchEnum.Undefined)
					{
						checkHBase = BooleanSwitchEnum.On;
					}
					break;
				case IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.FlarenetProperty:
					throw new Exception("Cannot create a Flarenet Property Package. Use the set InternalPropPackage property.");
				default:
					throw new Exception("Unsupported package base found.");
				}
				iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase.CheckEnthalpyBase = checkHBase;
				iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase.LicenseCookie = licenseCookie;
				iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase.HysysSecurity = myHysysSecurity;
				if (!(iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase is AHPPPWithAspenProperties))
				{
					(iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase as IPPPWithCOMThermoBase).CheckEnthalpyBase = checkHBase;
				}
				return iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase;
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("createIPPP failed. ", innerException);
			}
		}

		public void InitFromStrings(IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum package, string sPropertyMgr, string sCompoundCollection, string sExtPackageDef, string sExtFlashdef, IFlowStream stream)
		{
			if (packageBase != package || pIPPP == null)
			{
				packageBase = package;
				IAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase = createIPPP();
				pIPPP = iAHPhysicalPropertyPackageBase;
				pIPPP.FlashLevel = flashLevel;
			}
			if (package != IPPPOptionalPackageBaseEnum.AspenProperty)
			{
				((IPPPWithCOMThermoBase)pIPPP).InitFromStrings(sPropertyMgr, sCompoundCollection, sExtPackageDef, sExtFlashdef, stream);
			}
			else
			{
				((IPPPWithAspenProperties)pIPPP).LinkedFromHysys(stream);
			}
		}
	}

	internal class IndependantVar
	{
		private PropertyType lclRangeType = (PropertyType)(-1);

		private double[] lclRange;

		private int lclLoBracket;

		private int lclHiBracket;

		internal double[] Values
		{
			get
			{
				if (lclRange != null)
				{
					double[] array = new double[lclRange.Length];
					lclRange.CopyTo(array, 0);
					return array;
				}
				return null;
			}
			set
			{
				if (lclRange == null)
				{
					lclRange = new double[value.Length];
					value.CopyTo(lclRange, 0);
				}
			}
		}

		internal int HiBracket
		{
			get
			{
				if (lclHiBracket >= 0)
				{
					return lclHiBracket;
				}
				return (int)RealValue.FEMPTY;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value > 0 && value < lclRange.Length)
				{
					lclHiBracket = value;
				}
			}
		}

		internal PropertyType IndependantVariableType
		{
			get
			{
				return lclRangeType;
			}
			set
			{
				if (lclRangeType < PropertyType.Temperature)
				{
					lclRangeType = value;
				}
			}
		}

		internal int LoBracket
		{
			get
			{
				if (lclHiBracket >= 0)
				{
					return lclLoBracket;
				}
				return (int)RealValue.FEMPTY;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value > 0 && value < lclRange.Length)
				{
					lclLoBracket = value;
				}
			}
		}

		internal double this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				if (index < 0 || index >= lclRange.Length)
				{
					return RealValue.FEMPTY;
				}
				return lclRange[index];
			}
			set
			{
				if (index >= 0 && index < lclRange.Length)
				{
					lclRange[index] = value;
				}
			}
		}

		internal IndependantVar(PropertyType propType, double[] values)
		{
			lclRangeType = propType;
			Values = values;
			lclLoBracket = -1;
			lclHiBracket = -1;
		}

		internal void CopyTo(double[] array, int index)
		{
			lclRange.CopyTo(array, index);
		}

		internal int Find(double theValue)
		{
			int num = lclRange.Length;
			if (theValue < lclRange[0])
			{
				lclLoBracket = -1;
				lclHiBracket = 0;
				return -3;
			}
			if (theValue == lclRange[0])
			{
				lclLoBracket = 0;
				lclHiBracket = 1;
				return 0;
			}
			if (theValue > lclRange[num - 1])
			{
				lclLoBracket = -1;
				lclHiBracket = num - 1;
				return -2;
			}
			if (theValue == lclRange[num - 1])
			{
				lclLoBracket = num - 2;
				lclHiBracket = num - 1;
				return num - 1;
			}
			int num2 = 0;
			int num3 = num - 1;
			while (num2 < num3)
			{
				int num4 = (num2 + num3) / 2;
				if (theValue > lclRange[num4])
				{
					num2 = num4 + 1;
					continue;
				}
				if (theValue == lclRange[num4])
				{
					lclLoBracket = num4 - 1;
					lclHiBracket = num4;
					return num4;
				}
				num3 = num4;
			}
			lclLoBracket = num2 - 1;
			lclHiBracket = num2;
			return -1;
		}

		internal double GetValue(int index)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index >= lclRange.Length)
			{
				return RealValue.FEMPTY;
			}
			return lclRange[index];
		}

		internal int Insert(double theValue, int flag)
		{
			int num = lclRange.Length;
			if (num == 0)
			{
				InsertAt(0, theValue);
				return 0;
			}
			if (Find(theValue) >= 0)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			switch (flag)
			{
			case -3:
				InsertAt(0, theValue);
				flag = 0;
				break;
			case -2:
				InsertAt(num, theValue);
				flag = num;
				break;
			default:
				InsertAt(lclHiBracket, theValue);
				flag = lclHiBracket;
				break;
			}
			return flag;
		}

		internal int Length()
		{
			return lclRange.Length;
		}

		internal void SetValue(int Index, double theValue)
		{
			if (Index >= 0 && Index < lclRange.Length)
			{
				lclRange[Index] = theValue;
			}
		}

		private void InsertAt(int index, double theValue)
		{
			double[] array = new double[lclRange.Length + 1];
			for (int i = 0; i < lclRange.Length; i++)
			{
				if (i < index)
				{
					array[i] = lclRange[i];
				}
				if (i >= index)
				{
					array[i + 1] = lclRange[i];
				}
			}
			array[index] = theValue;
			lclRange = array;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public delegate void IPPPFlashCallBack(int index, out IFlowStream outStream, FlashType typeOfFlash, double data1, double data2);
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public delegate int IPPPBlendCallBack(int indexA, int indexB);
	[Guid("CC72E2E9-2B0F-4A8D-9305-AFB84978352C")]
	[ClassInterface(ClassInterfaceType.None)]
	public class IPPPCollection : I_IPPPCollection
	{
		private ArrayList lclCollection;

		private ArrayList refStreams;

		public int Count => lclCollection.Count;

		public IPPPCollection()
		{
			lclCollection = new ArrayList();
			refStreams = new ArrayList();
		}

		public void add(IPhysicalPropertyPackage mIPPP, IFlowStream mIFS)
		{
			lclCollection.Add(mIPPP);
			refStreams.Add(mIFS);
		}

		public void DeleteWithIndex(int i)
		{
			if (i < lclCollection.Count)
			{
				lclCollection.RemoveAt(i);
				refStreams.RemoveAt(i);
				return;
			}
			throw new Exception("Index larger than count");
		}

		public IPhysicalPropertyPackage getIPPP(int pos)
		{
			if (pos < Count)
			{
				return lclCollection[pos] as IPhysicalPropertyPackage;
			}
			throw new Exception("Index larger than count");
		}

		public IFlowStream getStream(int pos)
		{
			if (pos < Count)
			{
				return refStreams[pos] as IFlowStream;
			}
			throw new Exception("Index larger than count");
		}

		public void FlashIPPP(int index, out IFlowStream outStream, FlashType typeOfFlash, double data1, double data2)
		{
			if (index > lclCollection.Count)
			{
				throw new Exception("Index is larger than count");
			}
			if (index < 0)
			{
				throw new Exception("Index is negative");
			}
			try
			{
				IPhysicalPropertyPackage iPPP = getIPPP(index);
				IFlowStream stream = getStream(index);
				iPPP.FlashStream(stream, typeOfFlash, data1, data2);
				outStream = stream;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				throw ex;
			}
		}

		public int BlendIPPP(int indexA, int indexB)
		{
			if (indexA > lclCollection.Count)
			{
				throw new Exception("Index A is larger than count");
			}
			if (indexA < 0)
			{
				throw new Exception("Index A is negative");
			}
			if (indexB > lclCollection.Count)
			{
				throw new Exception("Index B is larger than count");
			}
			if (indexB < 0)
			{
				throw new Exception("Index B is negative");
			}
			return -1;
		}
	}
	[Guid("48279813-4171-4DAE-9D3A-47487341455F")]
	public interface I_IPPPCollection
	{
		int Count { get; }

		void add(IPhysicalPropertyPackage mIPPP, IFlowStream mIFS);

		void DeleteWithIndex(int i);

		IPhysicalPropertyPackage getIPPP(int pos);

		[ComVisible(false)]
		void FlashIPPP(int index, out IFlowStream outStream, FlashType typeOfFlash, double data1, double data2);

		[ComVisible(false)]
		int BlendIPPP(int indexA, int indexB);
	}

		public bool isMixture
		{
			get
			{
				bool result = false;
				if (NumberOfCompounds > 1)
				{
					result = true;
				}
				else
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < NumberOfCompounds; i++)
					{
						if (GetComponentByID(i).IsHypothetical)
						{
							result = true;
							break;
						}
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
		}
