				volumeFraction = value;
			}
		}

		public Phase()
		{
			PhaseInit(0);
		}

		public Phase(int nCompounds)
		{
			PhaseInit(nCompounds);
		}

		public Phase(Phase refPhase)
		{
			PhaseInit(0);
			for (int i = 0; i < refPhase.NumberOfCompounds; i++)
			{
				AddComponent((Component)refPhase.components[i]);
				ComponentMoleFractions[i].Value = refPhase.cmpmolefractions[i].Value;
			}
			Temperature = new RealValue(refPhase.temperature);
			Pressure = new RealValue(refPhase.pressure);
			Enthalpy = new RealValue(refPhase.enthalpy);
			StagnationEnthalpy = new RealValue(refPhase.StagnationEnthalpy);
			InternalEnergy = new RealValue(refPhase.internalenergy);
			Density = new RealValue(refPhase.density);
			Cp = new RealValue(refPhase.cp);
			Cv = new RealValue(refPhase.cv);
			Entropy = new RealValue(refPhase.entropy);
			CriticalPressure = new RealValue(refPhase.criticalpressure);
			Conductivity = new RealValue(refPhase.conductivity);
			Viscosity = new RealValue(refPhase.viscosity);
			SurfaceTension = new RealValue(refPhase.surfaceTension);
			MassFraction = new RealValue(refPhase.massfraction);
			MolarFraction = new RealValue(refPhase.molarfraction);
			MolarVapourFraction = new RealValue(refPhase.molarvapourfraction);
			MolecularWeight = new RealValue(refPhase.molecularweight);
			PhaseType = refPhase.phasetype;
			SonicVelocity = new RealValue(refPhase.sonicVelocity);
			DerivativeOfDensityToPressure = new RealValue(refPhase.dRhoDP);
			DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature = new RealValue(refPhase.dRhoDT);
			DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure = new RealValue(refPhase.dUDP);
			DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature = new RealValue(refPhase.dUdT);
			MassFraction = new RealValue(refPhase.massfraction);
			VolumeFlowrate = new RealValue(refPhase.volumeFlowrate);
			VolumeFraction = new RealValue(refPhase.volumeFraction);
		}

		~Phase()
		{
			Dispose(Disposing: false);
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(Disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool Disposing)
		{
			if (!mDisposed && Disposing)
			{
				int num = -1;
				int num2 = -1;
				num2 = cmpmassfractions.Count;
				for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
				{
					((IDisposable)cmpmassfractions[num]).Dispose();
				}
				num2 = cmpmolefractions.Count;
				for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
				{
					((IDisposable)cmpmolefractions[num]).Dispose();
				}
			}
			mDisposed = true;
		}

		public int AddComponent(IComponent newComp)
		{
			int num = -32767;
			cmpmassfractions.Add(new LiteRealValueWithEvent(UpdateMoleFractions));
			cmpmolefractions.Add(new LiteRealValueWithEvent(UpdateMassFractions));
			num = components.Add(newComp) + 1;
			if (num != cmpmassfractions.Count || num != cmpmolefractions.Count)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Internal Component Count Mismatch");
			}
			return num - 1;
		}

		public int AddNewComponent(int ID, string Name, double MolWt)
		{
			cmpmassfractions.Add(new LiteRealValueWithEvent(UpdateMoleFractions));
			cmpmolefractions.Add(new LiteRealValueWithEvent(UpdateMassFractions));
			int num = components.Add(new Component(Name, ID, MolWt)) + 1;
			if (num != cmpmassfractions.Count || num != cmpmolefractions.Count)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Internal Component Count Mismatch");
			}
			return num - 1;
		}

		public int AddNewComponent(int ID)
		{
			cmpmassfractions.Add(new LiteRealValueWithEvent(UpdateMoleFractions));
			cmpmolefractions.Add(new LiteRealValueWithEvent(UpdateMassFractions));
			int num = components.Add(new Component("", ID, RealValue.FEMPTY)) + 1;
			if (num != cmpmassfractions.Count || num != cmpmolefractions.Count)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException("Internal Component Count Mismatch");
			}
			return num - 1;
		}

		public virtual void AvaragePhase(IPhase phase1, IPhase phase2, double localDistance, double totalDistance, bool overrideExistingData)
		{
			try
			{
				IRealValue realValue = null;
				IRealValue realValue2 = null;
				if (phase1.PhaseType != phase2.PhaseType)
				{
					throw new Exception("Phase types are different can not average values");
				}
				PhaseType = phase1.PhaseType;
				IRealValue val = phase1.Conductivity;
				realValue = phase2.Conductivity;
				realValue2 = Conductivity;
				PlugAverageValue(val, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue val2 = phase1.Cp;
				realValue = phase2.Cp;
				realValue2 = cp;
				PlugAverageValue(val2, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue criticalPressure = phase1.CriticalPressure;
				realValue = phase2.CriticalPressure;
				realValue2 = CriticalPressure;
				PlugAverageValue(criticalPressure, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue val3 = phase1.Cv;
				realValue = phase2.Cv;
				realValue2 = Cv;
				PlugAverageValue(val3, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue val4 = phase1.Density;
				realValue = phase2.Density;
				realValue2 = Density;
				PlugAverageValue(val4, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue val5 = phase1.Enthalpy;
				realValue = phase2.Enthalpy;
				realValue2 = Enthalpy;
				PlugAverageValue(val5, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue val6 = phase1.Entropy;
				realValue = phase2.Entropy;
				realValue2 = Entropy;
				PlugAverageValue(val6, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue internalEnergy = phase1.InternalEnergy;
				realValue = phase2.InternalEnergy;
				realValue2 = InternalEnergy;
				PlugAverageValue(internalEnergy, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue massFraction = phase1.MassFraction;
				realValue = phase2.MassFraction;
				realValue2 = MassFraction;
				PlugAverageValue(massFraction, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue molarFraction = phase1.MolarFraction;
				realValue = phase2.MolarFraction;
				realValue2 = MolarFraction;
				PlugAverageValue(molarFraction, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue molarVapourFraction = phase1.MolarVapourFraction;
				realValue = phase2.MolarVapourFraction;
				realValue2 = MolarVapourFraction;
				PlugAverageValue(molarVapourFraction, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue molecularWeight = phase1.MolecularWeight;
				realValue = phase2.MolecularWeight;
				realValue2 = MolecularWeight;
				PlugAverageValue(molecularWeight, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue val7 = phase1.Pressure;
				realValue = phase2.Pressure;
				realValue2 = Pressure;
				PlugAverageValue(val7, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue val8 = phase1.SurfaceTension;
				realValue = phase2.SurfaceTension;
				realValue2 = SurfaceTension;
				PlugAverageValue(val8, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue val9 = phase1.Temperature;
				realValue = phase2.Temperature;
				realValue2 = Temperature;
				PlugAverageValue(val9, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue val10 = phase1.Viscosity;
				realValue = phase2.Viscosity;
				realValue2 = Viscosity;
				PlugAverageValue(val10, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue val11 = phase1.SonicVelocity;
				realValue = phase2.SonicVelocity;
				realValue2 = SonicVelocity;
				PlugAverageValue(val11, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue derivativeOfDensityToPressure = phase1.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure;
				realValue = phase2.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure;
				realValue2 = DerivativeOfDensityToPressure;
				PlugAverageValue(derivativeOfDensityToPressure, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue derivativeOfDensityToTemperature = phase1.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature;
				realValue = phase2.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature;
				realValue2 = DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature;
				PlugAverageValue(derivativeOfDensityToTemperature, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue derivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure = phase1.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure;
				realValue = phase2.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure;
				realValue2 = DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure;
				PlugAverageValue(derivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
				IRealValue derivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature = phase1.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature;
				realValue = phase2.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature;
				realValue2 = DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature;
				PlugAverageValue(derivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature, realValue, realValue2, overrideExistingData, localDistance, totalDistance);
			}
			catch (Exception innerException)
			{
				throw new Exception("AvaragePhase failed", innerException);
			}
		}

		public static void PlugAverageValue(IRealValue val1, IRealValue val2, IRealValue valLocal, bool overrideFlowStream, double localDistance, double totalDistance)
		{
			if (!val1.IsUnknown && !val2.IsUnknown && ((!valLocal.IsUnknown && overrideFlowStream) || valLocal.IsUnknown))
			{
				valLocal.Value = val1.Value + (val2.Value - val1.Value) * localDistance / totalDistance;
			}
		}

		public void SetComponent(int IComp, IComponent Comp)
		{
			if (IComp >= components.Count)
			{
				components.Add(Comp);
			}
			else
			{
				components[IComp] = Comp;
			}
		}

		public void SetComponentValues(int IComp, int ID, string Name, double MoleWt)
		{
			if (IComp >= components.Count)
			{
				components.Add(new Component(Name, ID, MoleWt));
				return;
			}
			Component component = components[IComp] as Component;
			component.ID = ID;
			component.Name = Name;
			component.MolecularWeight.Value = MoleWt;
			if (Math.Abs(MoleWt - 18.016) < 0.001)
			{
				component.Alias = "Water";
			}
		}

		public void GetComponentValues(int IComp, out int ID, out string Name, out double MoleWt)
		{
			IComponent component = components[IComp] as IComponent;
			ID = component.ID;
			Name = component.Name;
			MoleWt = component.MolecularWeight.Value;
		}

		public IComponent GetComponent(int IComp)
		{
			return components[IComp] as IComponent;
		}

		public IComponent GetComponentByID(int anID)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < components.Count; i++)
			{
				if (((IComponent)components[i]).ID == anID)
				{
					return (IComponent)components[i];
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void RemoveAllComponents()
		{
			cmpmassfractions.Clear();
			cmpmolefractions.Clear();
			components.Clear();
		}

		public virtual void UpdatePhaseByValue(IPhase refPhase)
		{
			if (refPhase == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("refPhase", "Null Argument");
			}
			if (this == refPhase)
			{
				return;
			}
			int numberOfCompounds = refPhase.NumberOfCompounds;
			if (numberOfCompounds != NumberOfCompounds)
			{
				components = new ArrayList(numberOfCompounds);
				cmpmassfractions = new FractionList();
				cmpmolefractions = new FractionList();
				for (int i = 0; i < numberOfCompounds; i++)
				{
					AddComponent((Component)refPhase.GetComponent(i));
					ComponentMoleFractions[i].Value = refPhase.ComponentMoleFractions[i].Value;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < numberOfCompounds; j++)
				{
					GetComponent(j).UpdateByValue(refPhase.GetComponent(j));
					ComponentMoleFractions[j].Value = refPhase.ComponentMoleFractions[j].Value;
				}
			}
			conductivity.AssignFrom(refPhase.Conductivity);
			criticalpressure.AssignFrom(refPhase.CriticalPressure);
			cp.AssignFrom(refPhase.Cp);
			cv.AssignFrom(refPhase.Cv);
			density.AssignFrom(refPhase.Density);
			enthalpy.AssignFrom(refPhase.Enthalpy);
			entropy.AssignFrom(refPhase.Entropy);
			internalenergy.AssignFrom(refPhase.InternalEnergy);
			massfraction.AssignFrom(refPhase.MassFraction);
			molarfraction.AssignFrom(refPhase.MolarFraction);
			molarvapourfraction.AssignFrom(refPhase.MolarVapourFraction);
			molecularweight.AssignFrom(refPhase.MolecularWeight);
			phasetype = refPhase.PhaseType;
			pressure.AssignFrom(refPhase.Pressure);
			surfaceTension.AssignFrom(refPhase.SurfaceTension);
			temperature.AssignFrom(refPhase.Temperature);
			viscosity.AssignFrom(refPhase.Viscosity);
			sonicVelocity.AssignFrom(refPhase.SonicVelocity);
			dRhoDP.AssignFrom(refPhase.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure);
			dRhoDT.AssignFrom(refPhase.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature);
			dUDP.AssignFrom(refPhase.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure);
			dUdT.AssignFrom(refPhase.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature);
			massFlowrate.AssignFrom(refPhase.MassFlowrate);
		}

		public virtual object Clone()
		{
			return new Phase(this);
		}

		public void Erase()
		{
			EraseState();
		}

		public void EraseState()
		{
			pressure.Erase();
			temperature.Erase();
			enthalpy.Erase();
			entropy.Erase();
			density.Erase();
		}

		public virtual void CopyStateParameters(IPhase targetPhase)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < NumberOfCompounds; i++)
			{
				targetPhase.SetComponent(i, (IComponent)components[i]);
				targetPhase.ComponentMoleFractions[i].Value = cmpmolefractions[i].Value;
			}
			targetPhase.Temperature.Value = temperature.Value;
			targetPhase.Pressure.Value = pressure.Value;
			targetPhase.Enthalpy.Value = enthalpy.Value;
			targetPhase.InternalEnergy.Value = internalenergy.Value;
			targetPhase.Density.Value = density.Value;
			targetPhase.Entropy.Value = entropy.Value;
			targetPhase.MassFraction.Value = massfraction.Value;
			targetPhase.MolarFraction.Value = molarfraction.Value;
			targetPhase.MolarVapourFraction.Value = molarvapourfraction.Value;
			targetPhase.MolecularWeight.Value = molecularweight.Value;
			targetPhase.Viscosity.Value = viscosity.Value;
		}

		public double GetProperty(PropertyType PropertyName)
		{
			return PropertyName switch
			{
				PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToPressure => dRhoDP.Value, 
				PropertyType.DerivativeOfDensityToTemperature => dRhoDT.Value, 
				PropertyType.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToPressure => dUDP.Value, 
				PropertyType.DerivativeOfInternalEnergyToTemperature => dUdT.Value, 
				PropertyType.Conductivity => Conductivity.Value, 
				PropertyType.Cp => Cp.Value, 
				PropertyType.Cv => Cv.Value, 
				PropertyType.Density => Density.Value, 
				PropertyType.Enthalpy => Enthalpy.Value, 
				PropertyType.Entropy => Entropy.Value, 
				PropertyType.InternalEnergy => InternalEnergy.Value, 
				PropertyType.Pressure => Pressure.Value, 
				PropertyType.Temperature => Temperature.Value, 
				PropertyType.Viscosity => Viscosity.Value, 
				PropertyType.CriticalPressure => CriticalPressure.Value, 
				PropertyType.MassFraction => MassFraction.Value, 
				PropertyType.MolarFraction => MolarFraction.Value, 
				PropertyType.MolecularWeight => MolecularWeight.Value, 
				PropertyType.SurfaceTension => SurfaceTension.Value, 
				PropertyType.SonicVelocity => SonicVelocity.Value, 
				_ => 0.0, 
			};
		}

		internal bool IsTrueAqueous()
		{
			int num = -32767;
			int num2 = -32767;
			if (phasetype != PhaseType.Aqueous && phasetype != PhaseType.Liquid)
			{
				return false;
			}
			num2 = components.Count;
			for (num = 0; num < num2; num++)
			{
				if (((Component)components[num]).Alias == "Water" && cmpmolefractions[num].Value > 0.25)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void EraseMassFractions()
		{
			int count = cmpmassfractions.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				cmpmassfractions[i].Erase();
			}
		}

		private void EraseMoleFractions()
		{
			int count = cmpmolefractions.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				cmpmolefractions[i].Erase();
			}
		}

		private void PhaseInit(int Ncomp)
		{
			RealValueWithCallBack realValueWithCallBack = null;
			cmpmassfractions = new FractionList();
			cmpmolefractions = new FractionList();
			for (int i = 0; i < Ncomp; i++)
			{
				realValueWithCallBack = new RealValueWithEvent(UpdateMoleFractions);
				cmpmassfractions.Add(realValueWithCallBack);
				realValueWithCallBack = new RealValueWithEvent(UpdateMassFractions);
				cmpmolefractions.Add(realValueWithCallBack);
			}
			components = new ArrayList(Ncomp);
			conductivity = new RealValue();
			cp = new RealValue();
			criticalpressure = new RealValue();
			cv = new RealValue();
			density = new RealValue();
			enthalpy = new RealValue();
			StagnationEnthalpy = new RealValue();
			entropy = new RealValue();
			internalenergy = new RealValue();
			massfraction = new RealValue();
			molarfraction = new RealValue();
			molarvapourfraction = new RealValue();
			molecularweight = new RealValue();
			phasetype = PhaseType.UnknownPhase;
			pressure = new RealValue();
			surfaceTension = new RealValue();
			temperature = new RealValue();
			viscosity = new RealValue();
			sonicVelocity = new RealValue();
			dRhoDP = new RealValue();
			dRhoDT = new RealValue();
			dUDP = new RealValue();
			dUdT = new RealValue();
			massFlowrate = new RealValue();
			volumeFlowrate = new RealValue();
			volumeFraction = new RealValue();
		}

		private void UpdateMassFractions()
		{
			if (!ValidateComponentData())
			{
				return;
			}
			double num = 0.0;
			int count = cmpmolefractions.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (cmpmolefractions[i].IsUnknown)
				{
					EraseMassFractions();
					return;
				}
				num += cmpmolefractions[i].Value;
			}
			if (Math.Abs(1.0 - num) >= 1E-05)
			{
				EraseMassFractions();
				return;
			}
			double num2 = 0.0;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				IComponent component = (IComponent)components[i];
				num2 += component.MolecularWeight.Value * cmpmolefractions[i].Value;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				IComponent component2 = (IComponent)components[i];
				((LiteRealValueWithEvent)cmpmassfractions[i]).SetValueWithoutCallBack(component2.MolecularWeight.Value * cmpmolefractions[i].Value / num2);
			}
			UpdateMolecularWeight();
		}

		private void UpdateMoleFractions()
		{
			if (!ValidateComponentData())
			{
				return;
			}
			double num = 0.0;
			int count = cmpmassfractions.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				if (cmpmassfractions[i].IsUnknown)
				{
					EraseMoleFractions();
					return;
				}
				num += cmpmassfractions[i].Value;
			}
			if (Math.Abs(1.0 - num) >= 1E-05)
			{
				EraseMoleFractions();
				return;
			}
			double num2 = 0.0;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				IComponent component = (IComponent)components[i];
				num2 += cmpmassfractions[i].Value / component.MolecularWeight.Value;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				IComponent component2 = (IComponent)components[i];
				((LiteRealValueWithEvent)cmpmolefractions[i]).SetValueWithoutCallBack(cmpmassfractions[i].Value / (component2.MolecularWeight.Value * num2));
			}
			UpdateMolecularWeight();
		}

		private void UpdateMolecularWeight()
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			int count = components.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				IComponent component = (IComponent)components[i];
				num += cmpmolefractions[i].Value * component.MolecularWeight.Value;
			}
			molecularweight.Value = num;
		}

		private bool ValidateComponentData()
		{
			int count = components.Count;
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				double value = ((IComponent)components[i]).MolecularWeight.Value;
				if (value <= 0.0 || value > 10000.0)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class DPCurvePoint
	{
		private double mMassFlow;

		private double mPressureDrop;

		public double MassFlow
		{
			get
			{
				return mMassFlow;
			}
			set
			{
				mMassFlow = value;
			}
		}

		public double PressureDrop
		{
			get
			{
				return mPressureDrop;
			}
			set
			{
				mPressureDrop = value;
			}
		}

		public DPCurvePoint()
		{
			mMassFlow = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			mPressureDrop = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		}

		public DPCurvePoint(double massflow, double dp)
		{
			mMassFlow = massflow;
			mPressureDrop = dp;
		}
	}
	[Guid("CACEBA3C-94CF-42B8-A0BB-D624CF71511E")]
	public interface IPressureDropCurve
	{
		double MolecularWeight { get; set; }

		int AddPoint(double massFlow, double pressureDrop);

		bool RemovePoint(double massFlow);

		double GetPressureDrop(double massFlow, bool extrapolate);

		void Clear();
	}
	[Guid("8CF7DE41-9E4F-46B7-894A-1BD3CB0EE36F")]
	[ClassInterface(ClassInterfaceType.None)]
	public class PressureDropCurve : CollectionBase, IPressureDropCurve
	{
		private class AscendingMassFlowSorter : IComparer
		{
			public int Compare(object x, object y)
			{
				int result = 0;
				DPCurvePoint dPCurvePoint = (DPCurvePoint)x;
				DPCurvePoint dPCurvePoint2 = (DPCurvePoint)y;
				if (dPCurvePoint.MassFlow < dPCurvePoint2.MassFlow)
				{
					result = -1;
				}
				else if (dPCurvePoint.MassFlow > dPCurvePoint2.MassFlow)
				{
					result = 1;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		private double mMW;

		public double MolecularWeight
		{
			get
			{
				return mMW;
			}
			set
			{
				mMW = value;
			}
		}

		public DPCurvePoint this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return (DPCurvePoint)base.List[index];
			}
			set
			{
				base.List[index] = value;
			}
		}

		public PressureDropCurve()
		{
			mMW = RealValue.FEMPTY;
		}

		public PressureDropCurve(double mw)
		{
			mMW = mw;
		}

		public int AddPoint(double massFlow, double pressureDrop)
		{
			DPCurvePoint point = new DPCurvePoint(massFlow, pressureDrop);
			return Add(point);
		}

		public double GetPressureDrop(double massFlow, bool extrapolate)
		{
			double fEMPTY = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			int count = base.List.Count;
			if (count == 1 && this[0].MassFlow == 0.0)
			{
				return this[0].PressureDrop;
			}
			if (FindBoundingPoints(massFlow, out var iLower, out var iUpper))
			{
				if (iLower == iUpper)
				{
					fEMPTY = this[iLower].PressureDrop;
				}
				else
				{
					double num = (this[iUpper].PressureDrop - this[iLower].PressureDrop) / (this[iUpper].MassFlow - this[iLower].MassFlow);
					double num2 = this[iUpper].PressureDrop - num * this[iUpper].MassFlow;
					fEMPTY = num * massFlow + num2;
				}
			}
			else if (massFlow < this[0].MassFlow)
			{
				fEMPTY = this[0].PressureDrop;
				if (extrapolate)
				{
					fEMPTY = Math.Pow(fEMPTY * (massFlow / this[0].MassFlow), 2.0);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				fEMPTY = this[count - 1].PressureDrop;
				if (extrapolate)
				{
					fEMPTY = Math.Pow(fEMPTY * (massFlow / this[count - 1].MassFlow), 2.0);
				}
			}
			return fEMPTY;
		}

		public bool RemovePoint(double massFlow)
		{
			int iLower;
			int iUpper;
			bool num = FindBoundingPoints(massFlow, out iLower, out iUpper);
			if (num && iLower == iUpper)
			{
				base.List.RemoveAt(iLower);
			}
			return num;
		}

		public int Add(DPCurvePoint point)
		{
			return base.List.Add(point);
		}

		public void Insert(int index, DPCurvePoint point)
		{
			base.List.Insert(index, point);
		}

		public void Remove(DPCurvePoint point)
		{
			base.List.Remove(point);
		}

		public void Sort()
		{
			AscendingMassFlowSorter comparer = new AscendingMassFlowSorter();
			base.InnerList.Sort(comparer);
		}

		internal bool FindBoundingPoints(double massFlow, out int iLower, out int iUpper)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			iLower = -1;
			iUpper = -1;
			if (base.List.Count < 1)
			{
				flag = false;
			}
			else if (base.List.Count == 1)
			{
				if (massFlow == this[0].MassFlow)
				{
					flag = true;
					iLower = 0;
					iUpper = 0;
				}
			}
			else if (base.List.Count > 1)
			{
				Sort();
				int num = 1;
				while (!flag && num < base.List.Count)
				{
					if (massFlow == this[num - 1].MassFlow)
					{
						flag = true;
						iLower = num - 1;
						iUpper = iLower;
					}
					else if (massFlow > this[num - 1].MassFlow && massFlow < this[num].MassFlow)
					{
						flag = true;
						iLower = num - 1;
						iUpper = num;
					}
					else if (massFlow == this[num].MassFlow)
					{
						flag = true;
						iLower = num;
						iUpper = num;
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
			return flag;
		}
	}
	[Guid("AC89A7F6-AD6C-48A0-A449-FCB49197350A")]
	public interface IPressureDropCurveCollection
	{
		double ReferenceTemperature { get; set; }

		bool ExtrapolateMW { get; set; }

		bool ExtrapolateFlow { get; set; }

		int Add(IPressureDropCurve curve);

		void Remove(IPressureDropCurve curve);

		IPressureDropCurve GetCurve(double molwt);

		double GetPressureDrop(double molwt, double massFlow);

		void Clear();
	}
	[Guid("876C4117-E3A3-4450-9AB2-A65C60AC754F")]
	[ClassInterface(ClassInterfaceType.None)]
	public class PressureDropCurveCollection : CollectionBase, IPressureDropCurveCollection
	{
		private class AscendingMWSorter : IComparer
		{
			public int Compare(object x, object y)
			{
				int result = 0;
				IPressureDropCurve pressureDropCurve = (IPressureDropCurve)x;
				IPressureDropCurve pressureDropCurve2 = (IPressureDropCurve)y;
				if (pressureDropCurve.MolecularWeight < pressureDropCurve2.MolecularWeight)
				{
					result = -1;
				}
				else if (pressureDropCurve.MolecularWeight > pressureDropCurve2.MolecularWeight)
				{
					result = 1;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		private double mRefTemp;

		private bool mExtrapolateMW;

		private bool mExtrapolateFlow;

		public double ReferenceTemperature
		{
			get
			{
				return mRefTemp;
			}
			set
			{
				mRefTemp = value;
			}
		}

		public bool ExtrapolateMW
		{
			get
			{
				return mExtrapolateMW;
			}
			set
			{
				mExtrapolateMW = value;
			}
		}

		public bool ExtrapolateFlow
		{
			get
			{
				return mExtrapolateFlow;
			}
			set
			{
				mExtrapolateFlow = value;
			}
		}

		public IPressureDropCurve this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return (IPressureDropCurve)base.List[index];
			}
			set
			{
				base.List[index] = value;
			}
		}

		public PressureDropCurveCollection()
		{
			mRefTemp = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			mExtrapolateMW = true;
			mExtrapolateFlow = true;
		}

		public PressureDropCurveCollection(double refTemp, bool extrapMW, bool extrapFlow)
		{
			mRefTemp = refTemp;
			mExtrapolateMW = extrapMW;
			mExtrapolateFlow = extrapFlow;
		}

		public int Add(IPressureDropCurve curve)
		{
			return base.List.Add(curve);
		}

		public void Remove(IPressureDropCurve curve)
		{
			base.List.Remove(curve);
		}

		public IPressureDropCurve GetCurve(double molwt)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			int i = 0;
			IPressureDropCurve result = null;
			for (; i < base.List.Count; i++)
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (this[i].MolecularWeight == molwt)
				{
					flag = true;
					result = this[i];
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public double GetPressureDrop(double molwt, double massFlow)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			int count = base.List.Count;
			if (FindBoundingCurves(molwt, out var iLower, out var iUpper))
			{
				if (iLower == iUpper)
				{
					num = this[iLower].GetPressureDrop(massFlow, mExtrapolateFlow);
				}
				else
				{
					double pressureDrop = this[iLower].GetPressureDrop(massFlow, mExtrapolateFlow);
					double pressureDrop2 = this[iUpper].GetPressureDrop(massFlow, mExtrapolateFlow);
					double molecularWeight = this[iLower].MolecularWeight;
					double molecularWeight2 = this[iUpper].MolecularWeight;
					double num2 = (pressureDrop2 - pressureDrop) / (molecularWeight2 - molecularWeight);
					double num3 = pressureDrop - num2 * molecularWeight;
					num = num2 * molwt + num3;
				}
			}
			else if (molwt < this[0].MolecularWeight)
			{
				num = this[0].GetPressureDrop(massFlow, mExtrapolateFlow);
				if (mExtrapolateMW)
				{
					num = num * this[0].MolecularWeight / molwt;
				}
			}
			else if (molwt > this[count - 1].MolecularWeight)
			{
				num = this[count - 1].GetPressureDrop(massFlow, mExtrapolateFlow);
				if (mExtrapolateMW)
				{
					num = num * this[count - 1].MolecularWeight / molwt;
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void Insert(int index, IPressureDropCurve curve)
		{
			base.List.Insert(index, curve);
		}

		public void Sort()
		{
			AscendingMWSorter comparer = new AscendingMWSorter();
			base.InnerList.Sort(comparer);
		}

		internal bool FindBoundingCurves(double mw, out int iLower, out int iUpper)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			iLower = -1;
			iUpper = -1;
			if (base.List.Count < 1)
			{
				flag = false;
			}
			else if (base.List.Count == 1)
			{
				if (mw == this[0].MolecularWeight)
				{
					flag = true;
					iLower = 0;
					iUpper = 0;
				}
			}
			else if (base.List.Count > 1)
			{
				Sort();
				int num = 1;
				while (!flag && num < base.List.Count)
				{
					if (mw == this[num - 1].MolecularWeight)
					{
						flag = true;
						iLower = num - 1;
						iUpper = iLower;
					}
					else if (mw > this[num - 1].MolecularWeight && mw < this[num].MolecularWeight)
					{
						flag = true;
						iLower = num - 1;
						iUpper = num;
					}
					else if (mw == this[num].MolecularWeight)
					{
						flag = true;
						iLower = num;
						iUpper = num;
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
			return flag;
		}
	}

	[Guid("4BFA01C7-FC16-4350-8456-0867CACA25F2")]

	public delegate void CallBackType();
	[Guid("080BFFE5-6B25-4203-8D55-F3185811A90D")]
	public interface IRealValue
	{
		double Value { get; set; }

		double HighLimit { get; set; }

		double LowLimit { get; set; }

		ValueState State { get; }

		bool IsUnknown { get; }

		bool HasError { get; }

		void Erase();

		void AssignFrom(IRealValue existing);

		bool IsInRange(double data);
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class RealValue : IRealValue, IComparable, ICloneable, IDisposable
	{
		public static readonly double FEMPTY = -32767.0;

		protected double mValue;

		protected double mHighLimit;

		protected double mLowLimit;

		protected ValueState mState;

		protected bool mDisposed;

		public virtual double Value
		{
			get
			{
				return mValue;
			}
			set
			{
				mValue = value;
				if (mValue == FEMPTY)
				{
					mState = ValueState.vsUnknown;
				}
				else if (!double.IsNaN(mValue))
				{
					if (mValue < mLowLimit && mLowLimit != FEMPTY)
					{
						throw new Exception("Can't set a value less than its low limit.");
					}
					if (mValue > mHighLimit && mHighLimit != FEMPTY)
					{
						throw new Exception("Can't set a value higher than its high limit.");
					}
					mState = ValueState.vsNone;
				}
				else
				{
					mState = ValueState.vsError;
				}
			}
		}

		public double HighLimit
		{
			get
			{
				return mHighLimit;
			}
			set
			{
				mHighLimit = value;
			}
		}

		public double LowLimit
		{
			get
			{
				return mLowLimit;
			}
			set
			{
				mLowLimit = value;
			}
		}

		public ValueState State => mState;

		public bool IsUnknown => (mState & ValueState.vsUnknown) > ValueState.vsNone;

		public bool HasError => (mState & ValueState.vsError) > ValueState.vsNone;

		public RealValue()
		{
			Erase();
		}

		public RealValue(IRealValue existing)
		{
			mState = existing.State;
			mValue = existing.Value;
			mHighLimit = existing.HighLimit;
			mLowLimit = existing.LowLimit;
		}

		public RealValue(double existing)
		{
			Erase();
			mState = ValueState.vsNone;
			mValue = existing;
		}

		public RealValue(double dValue, ValueState State)
		{
			Erase();
			mState = State;
			mValue = dValue;
		}

		public RealValue(double dValue, ValueState State, double lowLimit, double highLimit)
		{
			mState = State;
			mValue = dValue;
			mLowLimit = lowLimit;
			mHighLimit = highLimit;
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(Disposing: true);
			GC.SuppressFinalize(this);
		}

		protected virtual void Dispose(bool Disposing)
		{
			if (!mDisposed)
			{
			}
			mDisposed = true;
		}

		public virtual int CompareTo(object obj)
		{
			RealValue realValue = (RealValue)obj;
			if (!(obj is RealValue))
			{
				return -1;
			}
			int num = mState.CompareTo(realValue.mState);
			if (num == 0)
			{
				num = mValue.CompareTo(realValue.mValue);
			}
			return num;
		}

		public virtual object Clone()
		{
			return new RealValue(this);
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return mValue.GetHashCode() ^ mState.GetHashCode();
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (!(obj is RealValue))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return Equals((RealValue)obj);
		}

		public bool Equals(RealValue aVal)
		{
			if (aVal == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (aVal.mState == mState)
			{
				return aVal.mValue == mValue;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void AssignFrom(IRealValue existing)
		{
			mState = existing.State;
			mValue = existing.Value;
			mHighLimit = existing.HighLimit;
			mLowLimit = existing.LowLimit;
		}

		public void Erase()
		{
			mState = ValueState.vsUnknown;
			mValue = FEMPTY;
			mHighLimit = FEMPTY;
			mLowLimit = FEMPTY;
		}

		public bool IsInRange(double data)
		{
			if (data > mLowLimit && data < mHighLimit)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	[Guid("8B1F994C-2DBC-47C7-915E-831BEF6E124F")]
	public interface IRealValueWithCallBack : IRealValue
	{
		CallBackType CallBack { get; set; }

		bool IsDisabled { get; }

		void DisableCallBack();

		void EnableCallBack();
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class RealValueWithCallBack : RealValue, IRealValueWithCallBack, IRealValue
	{
		private CallBackType mCallBack;

		private bool mDisabled;

		public CallBackType CallBack
		{
			get
			{
				return mCallBack;
			}
			set
			{
				mCallBack = value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsDisabled => mDisabled;

		public override double Value
		{
			get
			{
				return mValue;
			}
			set
			{
				base.Value = value;
				if (mCallBack != null && !mDisabled)
				{
					mCallBack();
				}
			}
		}

		public RealValueWithCallBack(CallBackType CBT)
		{
			Erase();
			mCallBack = CBT;
			mDisabled = false;
		}

		public RealValueWithCallBack(IRealValueWithCallBack existing)
		{
			mState = existing.State;
			mValue = existing.Value;
			mCallBack = existing.CallBack;
			mDisabled = existing.IsDisabled;
		}

		public RealValueWithCallBack(double existing)
		{
			mState = ValueState.vsNone;
			mValue = existing;
			mCallBack = null;
			mDisabled = false;
		}

		public RealValueWithCallBack(double dValue, ValueState State)
		{
			mState = State;
			mValue = dValue;
			mCallBack = null;
			mDisabled = false;
		}

		public RealValueWithCallBack(double dValue, ValueState State, CallBackType CBT)
		{
			mState = State;
			mValue = dValue;
			mCallBack = CBT;
			mDisabled = false;
		}

		public RealValueWithCallBack(double dValue, CallBackType CBT)
		{
			mState = ValueState.vsNone;
			mValue = dValue;
			mCallBack = CBT;
			mDisabled = false;
		}

		public RealValueWithCallBack()
		{
			mCallBack = null;
			mDisabled = false;
		}

		~RealValueWithCallBack()
		{
			Dispose(Disposing: false);
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool Disposing)
		{
			if (!mDisposed)
			{
				try
				{
					if (Disposing)
					{
						mCallBack = null;
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					base.Dispose(Disposing);
				}
			}
			mDisposed = true;
		}

		public void AssignFrom(IRealValueWithCallBack existing)
		{
			if (existing == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException();
			}
			AssignFrom((IRealValue)existing);
			mCallBack = existing.CallBack;
			mDisabled = existing.IsDisabled;
		}

		public override object Clone()
		{
			return new RealValueWithCallBack(this);
		}

		public override int CompareTo(object obj)
		{
			int num = -32767;
			RealValue realValue = null;
			realValue = (RealValue)obj;
			if (realValue == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException();
			}
			num = mState.CompareTo(realValue.State);
			if (num == 0)
			{
				num = mValue.CompareTo(realValue.Value);
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void DisableCallBack()
		{
			mDisabled = true;
		}

		public void EnableCallBack()
		{
			mDisabled = false;
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (!(obj is RealValueWithCallBack))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return Equals((RealValueWithCallBack)obj);
		}

		public bool Equals(RealValueWithCallBack obj)
		{
			if (!Equals((RealValue)obj))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (mCallBack != obj.CallBack)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (mDisabled != obj.mDisabled)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return mValue.GetHashCode() ^ mState.GetHashCode() ^ mCallBack.GetHashCode();
		}
	}

	{

			base.List.Add(realValue);
		}

		public IRealValue Item(int index)
		{
			if (0 <= index && index < base.Count)
			{
				return (IRealValue)base.List[index];
			}
			return null;
		}
	}

		public override double Value
		{
			get
			{
				return mValue;
			}
			set
			{
				if (base.Value != value)

					base.Value = value;
					if (this.EventForRealValue != null)
					{
						this.EventForRealValue();
					}
				}
			}
		}

	[ComVisible(false)]
	public static class ViscosityCalculator
	{
		public static double NonPolarMixingViscosity(IPhase liqPhase1, IPhase liqPhase2)
		{
			double num = liqPhase1.MassFraction.Value / (liqPhase1.MassFraction.Value + liqPhase2.MassFraction.Value);
			double num2 = liqPhase2.MassFraction.Value / (liqPhase1.MassFraction.Value + liqPhase2.MassFraction.Value);
			return Math.Exp(num * Math.Log(liqPhase1.Viscosity.Value) + num2 * Math.Log(liqPhase2.Viscosity.Value));
		}

		public static double VolumeWeightedViscosity(IPhase phase1, IPhase phase2)
		{
			double num = phase1.MassFraction.Value / phase1.Density.Value;
			num /= phase1.MassFraction.Value / phase1.Density.Value + phase2.MassFraction.Value / phase2.Density.Value;
			return num * phase1.Viscosity.Value + (1.0 - num) * phase2.Viscosity.Value;
		}
	}

	[Guid("926E5667-8106-42F6-B609-5ABC84EE1B07")]
	public class TaylorEmulsionViscosityCorrelation : IRelativeEmulsionViscosityCorrelation
	{
		public double RelativeViscosity(IPhase dispersedPhase, IPhase continuousPhase)
		{
			double num = dispersedPhase.MassFraction.Value / (dispersedPhase.MassFraction.Value + continuousPhase.MassFraction.Value);
			double num2 = 1.0 / (1.0 + (1.0 - num) * dispersedPhase.Density.Value / num / continuousPhase.Density.Value);
			double value = dispersedPhase.Viscosity.Value;
			double value2 = continuousPhase.Viscosity.Value;
			double num3 = (value + 0.4 * value2) / (value2 + value);
			return 1.0 + 2.5 * num3 * num2;
		}
	}
	[Guid("C0F7440A-65D9-486F-876F-68128A9C8AD7")]
	public class BarneaMizrahiGenEmulsionCorrelation : IRelativeEmulsionViscosityCorrelation, ITwoParameterViscosityCorrelation
	{
		private double mK1;

		private double mK2;

		public double K1
		{
			get
			{
				return mK1;
			}
			set
			{
				mK1 = value;
			}
		}

		public double K2
		{
			get
			{
				return mK2;
			}
			set
			{
				mK2 = value;
			}
		}

		public BarneaMizrahiGenEmulsionCorrelation()
		{
			mK1 = 0.0;
			mK2 = 0.0;
		}

		public BarneaMizrahiGenEmulsionCorrelation(double K1, double K2)
		{
			mK1 = K1;
			mK2 = K2;
		}

		public double RelativeViscosity(IPhase dispersedPhase, IPhase continuousPhase)
		{
			double num = dispersedPhase.MassFraction.Value / (dispersedPhase.MassFraction.Value + continuousPhase.MassFraction.Value);
			double num2 = 1.0 / (1.0 + (1.0 - num) * dispersedPhase.Density.Value / num / continuousPhase.Density.Value);
			return Math.Exp(K1 * num2 / (1.0 - K2 * num2));
		}
	}
	[Guid("B8ABA07C-CFB4-4C2D-8C06-9E02277BC03E")]
	public class LevintonLeightonEmulsionViscosityCorrelation : IRelativeEmulsionViscosityCorrelation
	{
		public double RelativeViscosity(IPhase dispersedPhase, IPhase continuousPhase)
		{
			double num = dispersedPhase.MassFraction.Value / (dispersedPhase.MassFraction.Value + continuousPhase.MassFraction.Value);
			double num2 = 1.0 / (1.0 + (1.0 - num) * dispersedPhase.Density.Value / num / continuousPhase.Density.Value);
			double value = dispersedPhase.Viscosity.Value;
			double value2 = continuousPhase.Viscosity.Value;
			double num3 = (value + 0.4 * value2) / (value2 + value);
			double num4 = num2 + Math.Pow(num2, 1.6666666666666667) + Math.Pow(num2, 3.6666666666666665);
			return Math.Exp(2.5 * num3 * num4);
		}
	}
	[Guid("35799847-1C55-4DF9-8BEC-158AB2C118F7")]
	public class CubicPolynomialEmulsionViscosityCorrelation : IRelativeEmulsionViscosityCorrelation, IThreeParameterViscosityCorrelation
	{
		private double mK1;

		private double mK2;

		private double mK3;

		public double K1
		{
			get
			{
				return mK1;
			}
			set
			{
				mK1 = value;
			}
		}

		public double K2
		{
			get
			{
				return mK2;
			}
			set
			{
				mK2 = value;
			}
		}

		public double K3
		{
			get
			{
				return mK3;
			}
			set
			{
				mK3 = value;
			}
		}

		public CubicPolynomialEmulsionViscosityCorrelation()
		{
			mK1 = 0.0;
			mK2 = 0.0;
			mK3 = 0.0;
		}

		public CubicPolynomialEmulsionViscosityCorrelation(double linearCoeff, double quadCoeff, double cubicCoeff)
		{
			mK1 = linearCoeff;
			mK2 = quadCoeff;
			mK3 = cubicCoeff;
		}

		public double RelativeViscosity(IPhase dispersedPhase, IPhase continuousPhase)
		{
			double num = dispersedPhase.MassFraction.Value / (dispersedPhase.MassFraction.Value + continuousPhase.MassFraction.Value);
			double num2 = 1.0 / (1.0 + (1.0 - num) * dispersedPhase.Density.Value / num / continuousPhase.Density.Value);
			return 1.0 + K1 * num2 + K2 * num2 * num2 + K3 * num2 * num2 * num2;
		}
	}
	[Guid("280493D8-18AD-4076-B12C-857EC5E2B731")]
	public class VolumeEmulsionViscosityCorrelation : IEmulsionViscosityCorrelation
	{
		public double EmulsionViscosity(IPhase oil, IPhase water)
		{
			return ViscosityCalculator.VolumeWeightedViscosity(oil, water);
		}
	}
	[Guid("CCE4421F-F262-4F9A-BBF2-7CA869C23500")]
	public class HYSYSEmulsionViscosityCorrelation : IEmulsionViscosityCorrelation
	{
		public double EmulsionViscosity(IPhase oil, IPhase water)
		{
			double value = oil.Viscosity.Value;
			double value2 = water.Viscosity.Value;
			double num = oil.MassFraction.Value / (oil.MassFraction.Value + water.MassFraction.Value);
			double num2 = 1.0 / (1.0 + (1.0 - num) * oil.Density.Value / num / water.Density.Value);
			BarneaMizrahiGenEmulsionCorrelation barneaMizrahiGenEmulsionCorrelation = new BarneaMizrahiGenEmulsionCorrelation(3.6, 0.0);
			TaylorEmulsionViscosityCorrelation taylorEmulsionViscosityCorrelation = new TaylorEmulsionViscosityCorrelation();
			double num3 = value * barneaMizrahiGenEmulsionCorrelation.RelativeViscosity(water, oil);
			double num4 = value2 * taylorEmulsionViscosityCorrelation.RelativeViscosity(oil, water);
			if (num2 < 0.33)
			{
				return num4;
			}
			if (num2 >= 0.33 && num2 <= 0.5)
			{
				double num5 = (num2 - 0.33) / 0.16999999999999998;
				return num4 * (1.0 - num5) + num3 * num5;
			}
			return num3;
		}
	}
	[Guid("10E72448-E02B-4996-A8AB-3D0815F3EB16")]
	public class BrinkmanEmulsionViscosityCorrelation : IEmulsionViscosityCorrelation
	{
		public double EmulsionViscosity(IPhase oil, IPhase water)
		{
			double value = oil.Viscosity.Value;
			double value2 = water.Viscosity.Value;
			double num = oil.MassFraction.Value / (oil.MassFraction.Value + water.MassFraction.Value);
			double num2 = 1.0 / (1.0 + (1.0 - num) * oil.Density.Value / num / water.Density.Value);
			double num3 = Math.Pow(value / value2, 0.4);
			double num4 = num3 / (1.0 + num3);
			if (num2 < num4)
			{
				return value2 * Math.Pow(1.0 - num2, -2.5);
			}
			return value * Math.Pow(num2, -2.5);
		}
	}
	[Guid("94163536-F3DE-4580-8AFC-58757BFF9672")]
	public class EmulsionViscosityUsingInversionPoint : IEmulsionViscosityCorrelation, IEmulsionInversionPointMethod
	{
		private double mInversionPoint;

		private IRelativeEmulsionViscosityCorrelation mEmulsionViscosityCorr;

		public double InversionPoint
		{
			get
			{
				return mInversionPoint;
			}
			set
			{
				mInversionPoint = value;
			}
		}

		public IRelativeEmulsionViscosityCorrelation EmulsionViscosityCorrelation
		{
			get
			{
				return mEmulsionViscosityCorr;
			}
			set
			{
				mEmulsionViscosityCorr = value;
			}
		}

		public EmulsionViscosityUsingInversionPoint()
		{
			mInversionPoint = 0.5;
			mEmulsionViscosityCorr = null;
		}

		public EmulsionViscosityUsingInversionPoint(double invPt, IRelativeEmulsionViscosityCorrelation viscCorr)
		{
			mInversionPoint = invPt;
			mEmulsionViscosityCorr = viscCorr;
		}

		public double EmulsionViscosity(IPhase oil, IPhase water)
		{
			double result = RealValue.FEMPTY;
			if (oil != null && water != null && mEmulsionViscosityCorr != null)
			{
				double num = oil.MassFraction.Value / (oil.MassFraction.Value + water.MassFraction.Value);
				double num2 = 1.0 / (1.0 + (1.0 - num) * oil.Density.Value / num / water.Density.Value);
				result = ((!(1.0 - num2 > InversionPoint)) ? (mEmulsionViscosityCorr.RelativeViscosity(water, oil) * oil.Viscosity.Value) : (mEmulsionViscosityCorr.RelativeViscosity(oil, water) * water.Viscosity.Value));
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
